import { initializeApp } from 'firebase/app';
import { getAuth } from 'firebase/auth';
import { doc, getDocFromServer, initializeFirestore, setLogLevel } from 'firebase/firestore';
import firebaseConfig from '../../firebase-applet-config.json';

const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);

// Prevent transient WebChannel offline noise in sandboxed iframes/proxies; app code handles errors explicitly
setLogLevel('silent');

// CRITICAL: The app requires firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId with forced long polling for iframe/proxy resilience
export const db = initializeFirestore(app, {
  experimentalForceLongPolling: true,
  ignoreUndefinedProperties: true,
}, firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId);
export const auth = getAuth(app);

export enum OperationType {
  CREATE = 'create',
  UPDATE = 'update',
  DELETE = 'delete',
  LIST = 'list',
  GET = 'get',
  WRITE = 'write',
}

export interface FirestoreErrorInfo {
  error: string;
  operationType: OperationType;
  path: string | null;
  authInfo: {
    userId?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    emailVerified?: boolean | null;
    isAnonymous?: boolean | null;
    tenantId?: string | null;
    providerInfo?: {
      providerId?: string | null;
      email?: string | null;
    }[];
  };
}

export function handleFirestoreError(error: unknown, operationType: OperationType, path: string | null): never {
  const errInfo: FirestoreErrorInfo = {
    error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    authInfo: {
      userId: auth.currentUser?.uid,
      email: auth.currentUser?.email,
      emailVerified: auth.currentUser?.emailVerified,
      isAnonymous: auth.currentUser?.isAnonymous,
      tenantId: auth.currentUser?.tenantId,
      providerInfo: auth.currentUser?.providerData?.map(provider => ({
        providerId: provider.providerId,
        email: provider.email,
      })) || [],
    },
    operationType,
    path,
  };
  console.error('Firestore Error:', JSON.stringify(errInfo));
  throw new Error(JSON.stringify(errInfo));
}

// Test connection on boot
async function testConnection() {
  if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.onLine === false) {
    return;
  }
  try {
    await getDocFromServer(doc(db, 'test', 'connection'));
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error && (error.message.includes('the client is offline') || error.message.includes('unavailable'))) {
      return;
    }
  }
}

testConnection().catch(() => {});

import { db } from './firebase';
import { doc, getDoc, setDoc } from 'firebase/firestore';

const ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY = 'smart_card_admin_session_token';
const ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY = 'smart_card_admin_session_expiry';
const ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY = 'smart_card_admin_pwd_hash';

// Default initial password hash (SHA-256 for "admin1234")
export const DEFAULT_BASE_HASH = 'ac9689e2272427085e35b9d3e3e8bed88cb3434828b43b86fc0596cad4c6e270';

const FIRESTORE_PROJECT_ID = 'clean-plateau-dv4kj';
const FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID = 'ai-studio-41f04a06-1999-41b9-b7be-e25b21dc1fd0';
const FIRESTORE_API_KEY = 'AIzaSyAjyCP484AEQ52-9CDgp8TDkAw5woA1TFc';

const SEVEN_DAYS_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

// Clean insecure legacy keys if present
try {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    localStorage.removeItem('dynamic_qr_admin_password');
    if (localStorage.getItem('dynamic_qr_admin_session') === 'authenticated_admin_token') {
      localStorage.removeItem('dynamic_qr_admin_session');
    }
  }
} catch {}

/**
 * Computes SHA-256 cryptographic hash of a string using Web Crypto API
 */
export async function hashPassword(plain: string): Promise<string> {
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  const data = encoder.encode(plain);
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data);
  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
  return hashArray.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

/**
 * Gets the current active session token
 */
export function getAdminToken(): string | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY) || sessionStorage.getItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Authenticated fetch helper that automatically attaches Bearer token to requests
 */
export async function authFetch(input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit): Promise<Response> {
  const token = getAdminToken();
  const headers = new Headers(init?.headers || {});
  if (token) {
    headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
    headers.set('X-Admin-Token', token);
  }
  return fetch(input, {
    ...init,
    headers,
    credentials: 'include', // include HTTP-Only cookies
  });
}

/**
 * Checks if admin session is locally active and unexpired
 */
export function isAdminAuthenticated(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
  const token = getAdminToken();
  if (!token || token.length < 16) return false;

  try {
    const expiryStr = localStorage.getItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY) || sessionStorage.getItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY);
    if (expiryStr) {
      const expiry = parseInt(expiryStr, 10);
      if (!isNaN(expiry) && expiry <= Date.now()) {
        logoutAdmin();
        return false;
      }
    }
  } catch {}

  return true;
}

/**
 * Verifies active session with the backend server without breaking on static hosts (e.g. Vercel)
 */
export async function validateAdminSession(): Promise<boolean> {
  const token = getAdminToken();
  if (!token) return false;

  // First verify local session validity
  if (!isAdminAuthenticated()) return false;

  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 2000);

    const res = await authFetch('/api/admin/session', {
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (res.ok && contentType.includes('application/json')) {
      const data = await res.json();
      if (typeof data.authenticated === 'boolean') {
        if (!data.authenticated) {
          // Explicit invalidation by server
          logoutAdmin();
          return false;
        }
        return true;
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // If backend is unreachable or on a static host (like Vercel SPA), fallback to local valid token
  }

  // Session remains valid locally
  return true;
}

/**
 * Retrieves the currently active admin password hash directly from Cloud Database (Firestore).
 * Uses direct Firestore REST API for 100% reliable, unblockable fetching across all browsers,
 * Incognito tabs, and Brave Shields without third-party cookie or IndexedDB constraints.
 */
export async function getActivePasswordHash(): Promise<string> {
  // 1. Primary: Direct HTTPS REST query to Firestore cloud database
  try {
    const restUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIRESTORE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/${FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID}/documents/settings/auth?key=${FIRESTORE_API_KEY}`;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 2500);
    const res = await fetch(restUrl, { signal: controller.signal, cache: 'no-store' });
    clearTimeout(timer);

    if (res.ok) {
      const data = await res.json();
      const hashVal = data.fields?.hash?.stringValue;
      if (typeof hashVal === 'string' && hashVal.length === 64) {
        try { localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, hashVal); } catch {}
        try { sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, hashVal); } catch {}
        return hashVal;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('REST auth fetch error, falling back to Firestore SDK:', err);
  }

  // 2. Secondary: Firestore SDK getDoc
  try {
    const snap = await getDoc(doc(db, 'settings', 'auth'));
    if (snap.exists() && snap.data()?.hash) {
      const h = snap.data().hash;
      if (typeof h === 'string' && h.length === 64) {
        try { localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, h); } catch {}
        try { sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, h); } catch {}
        return h;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Firestore SDK auth error:', err);
  }

  // 3. Tertiary: Local storage cache
  try {
    const local = localStorage.getItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY) || sessionStorage.getItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY);
    if (local && local.length === 64) {
      return local;
    }
  } catch {}

  // 4. Default base password ("admin1234") if nothing is in DB yet
  return DEFAULT_BASE_HASH;
}

/**
 * Saves a valid session token locally
 */
function storeAdminSession(token: string): void {
  const expiry = String(Date.now() + SEVEN_DAYS_MS);
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY, token);
    localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY, expiry);
  } catch {}
  try {
    sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY, token);
    sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY, expiry);
  } catch {}
}

/**
 * Performs admin login:
 * Authoritatively validates against the active password in Cloud Firestore.
 * Once the password is changed, ONLY the new password is valid.
 */
export async function loginAdmin(passwordAttempt: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const clean = (passwordAttempt || '').trim();
  if (!clean) return false;

  const attemptHash = await hashPassword(clean);
  const activeHash = await getActivePasswordHash();

  // Try Server API first (if Express server is running)
  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 2500);

    const serverRes = await fetch('/api/admin/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      credentials: 'include',
      body: JSON.stringify({ password: clean }),
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    const contentType = serverRes.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (serverRes.ok && contentType.includes('application/json')) {
      const data = await serverRes.json();
      if (data.success && data.token) {
        storeAdminSession(data.token);
        try {
          localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, attemptHash);
          sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, attemptHash);
        } catch {}
        return true;
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Server fetch unavailable (e.g. static hosting on Vercel), fall through to database check
  }

  // Strict match against active database password hash:
  // If password was updated in Firestore, ONLY that updated password works!
  if (attemptHash === activeHash) {
    let clientToken: string;
    try {
      clientToken = crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID().replace(/-/g, '') + Date.now().toString(16) : 'adm_' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2) + Date.now().toString(36);
    } catch {
      clientToken = 'adm_token_' + Date.now().toString(36) + '_' + attemptHash.slice(0, 16);
    }

    storeAdminSession(clientToken);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, attemptHash);
      sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, attemptHash);
    } catch {}

    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

/**
 * Secure password change requiring current password verification:
 * Updates the password across the ENTIRE database:
 * 1. Cloud Firestore database via REST API & Firestore SDK (`settings/auth` and replica `platform_settings/auth`)
 * 2. Server filesystem database (`data/auth.json`)
 * 3. Client storage cache
 */
export async function changeAdminPassword(
  currentPasswordPlain: string,
  newPasswordPlain: string
): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }> {
  const curClean = (currentPasswordPlain || '').trim();
  const newClean = (newPasswordPlain || '').trim();

  if (!curClean) {
    return { success: false, message: 'الرجاء إدخال كلمة المرور الحالية.' };
  }
  if (!newClean || newClean.length < 4) {
    return { success: false, message: 'كلمة المرور الجديدة يجب ألا تقل عن 4 أحرف أو أرقام.' };
  }

  const curHashAttempt = await hashPassword(curClean);
  const activeHash = await getActivePasswordHash();

  // Validate current password (must match active database password)
  const isCurrentValid = curHashAttempt === activeHash || (activeHash === DEFAULT_BASE_HASH && curHashAttempt === DEFAULT_BASE_HASH);
  if (!isCurrentValid) {
    return { success: false, message: 'كلمة المرور الحالية غير صحيحة.' };
  }

  const newHash = await hashPassword(newClean);
  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

  // 1. UPDATE CLOUD FIRESTORE DATABASE VIA REST API (Works directly on all platforms)
  try {
    const restUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIRESTORE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/${FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID}/documents/settings/auth?key=${FIRESTORE_API_KEY}`;
    await fetch(restUrl, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        fields: {
          hash: { stringValue: newHash },
          updated_at: { stringValue: nowIso },
          migrated: { booleanValue: true },
        },
      }),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('REST cloud update warning:', err);
  }

  // 2. ALSO UPDATE VIA FIRESTORE SDK
  try {
    await setDoc(doc(db, 'settings', 'auth'), {
      hash: newHash,
      updated_at: nowIso,
      migrated: true,
    }, { merge: true });

    await setDoc(doc(db, 'platform_settings', 'auth'), {
      hash: newHash,
      updated_at: nowIso,
    }, { merge: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('SDK cloud update warning:', err);
  }

  // 3. UPDATE SERVER DATABASE (data/auth.json)
  try {
    const res = await authFetch('/api/admin/password', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        currentPassword: curClean,
        newPassword: newClean,
      }),
    });
    const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (res.ok && contentType.includes('application/json')) {
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.token) {
        storeAdminSession(data.token);
      }
    }
  } catch {}

  // 4. UPDATE LOCAL CLIENT CACHE
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, newHash);
    sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, newHash);
  } catch {}

  return {
    success: true,
    message: 'تم تحديث كلمة المرور في قاعدة البيانات السحابية (Firestore) وجميع الأجهزة بنجاح!',
  };
}

/**
 * Logout admin and invalidate session
 */
export function logoutAdmin(): void {
  try {
    authFetch('/api/admin/logout', { method: 'POST' }).catch(() => {});
  } catch {}

  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY);
    sessionStorage.removeItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY);
    sessionStorage.removeItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY);
  } catch {}
}

import { AppType, BankDetails, DeepLinkInfo, WalletProvider } from '../types/qr';

/**
 * Checks whether the current window is embedded within an iframe
 * (e.g., inside the AI Studio Web Preview environment).
 */
export function isRunningInIframe(): boolean {
  try {
    return window.self !== window.top;
  } catch {
    return true;
  }
}

/**
 * Encodes bank details into a compact bank URI scheme for storage
 */
export function encodeBankDetails(details: BankDetails): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  if (details.bankName) params.set('b', details.bankName);
  if (details.accountNumber) params.set('acc', details.accountNumber);
  if (details.accountName) params.set('name', details.accountName);
  if (details.iban) params.set('iban', details.iban);
  if (details.notes) params.set('notes', details.notes);
  return `bank://account?${params.toString()}`;
}

/**
 * Decodes a bank URI scheme back into structured BankDetails
 */
export function decodeBankDetails(uri: string): BankDetails | null {
  if (!uri || !uri.startsWith('bank://')) return null;
  try {
    const query = uri.replace('bank://account?', '').replace('bank://?', '');
    const params = new URLSearchParams(query);
    return {
      bankName: params.get('b') || 'حساب بنكي',
      accountNumber: params.get('acc') || '',
      accountName: params.get('name') || '',
      iban: params.get('iban') || '',
      notes: params.get('notes') || '',
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Validates that a URL does NOT contain dangerous or malicious execution schemes
 * (e.g., javascript:, vbscript:, data:, file:, etc.)
 */
export function isSafeUrlScheme(url: string): boolean {
  if (!url) return false;
  const sanitized = url.trim().replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/g, '').toLowerCase();
  const dangerous = /^(?:javascript|data|vbscript|file|blob|about):/i;
  return !dangerous.test(sanitized);
}

/**
 * Robustly sanitizes and extracts the true URL from raw inputs,
 * handling Android app share links (e.g. android-app://com.instapay.app/https/ipn.eg/...),
 * double protocols (https://android-app:// or https://https://), and custom schemes.
 */
export function cleanAndSanitizeUrl(input: string): string {
  let s = (input || '').trim();
  if (!s) return '';

  // Immediately reject dangerous script schemes
  if (!isSafeUrlScheme(s)) {
    return '';
  }

  // If already a bank URI scheme, leave as-is
  if (s.startsWith('bank://')) {
    return s;
  }

  // 1. Check if input contains an embedded web URL (common in Android share intents)
  // e.g., android-app://com.instapay.app/https/ipn.eg/S/shenoudazaher/instapay/7CGeaO
  const embeddedHttpMatch = s.match(/(https?:\/\/[^\s]+)/i) || s.match(/(https?\/[^\s]+)/i);
  if (embeddedHttpMatch && !s.startsWith('http://android-app') && !s.startsWith('https://android-app')) {
    let extracted = embeddedHttpMatch[1];
    if (extracted.startsWith('https/') || extracted.startsWith('http/')) {
      extracted = extracted.replace(/^https?\//, 'https://');
    }
    return extracted;
  }

  // 2. Double scheme like https://android-app://
  if (/^https?:\/\/+android-app:\/\//i.test(s)) {
    s = s.replace(/^https?:\/\/+android-app:\/\//i, 'android-app://');
    const innerUrl = s.match(/https?\/[^\s]+/i) || s.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s]+/i);
    if (innerUrl) {
      let clean = innerUrl[0];
      if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
        clean = clean.replace(/^https?\//, 'https://');
      }
      return clean;
    }
    const pkg = s.replace('android-app://', '').split('/')[0];
    return `https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=${pkg}`;
  }

  // 3. Fix double http(s) like https://https:// or https://http://
  s = s.replace(/^https?:\/\/+(https?:\/\/)/i, '$1');

  // 4. Raw android-app:// scheme
  if (s.startsWith('android-app://')) {
    const innerUrl = s.match(/https?\/[^\s]+/i) || s.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s]+/i);
    if (innerUrl) {
      let clean = innerUrl[0];
      if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
        clean = clean.replace(/^https?\//, 'https://');
      }
      return clean;
    }
    const pkg = s.replace('android-app://', '').split('/')[0];
    return `https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=${pkg}`;
  }

  // 5. Standard valid non-http schemes
  if (s.startsWith('tel:') || s.startsWith('mailto:')) {
    return s;
  }

  // 6. Custom payment schemes (preserve as-is so wallet/quick-pay detectors recognize them)
  if (/^(instapay|ipn|vfcash|orangecash|etisalatcash|wepay):\/\//i.test(s)) {
    return s;
  }

  return s;
}

/**
 * Normalizes Eastern Arabic numerals (٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩) to standard ASCII numerals (0123456789)
 */
export function normalizeArabicNumerals(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  const arabicNumerals = ['٠', '١', '٢', '٣', '٤', '٥', '٦', '٧', '٨', '٩'];
  return input.replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (char) => {
    const idx = arabicNumerals.indexOf(char);
    return idx !== -1 ? String(idx) : char;
  });
}

/**
 * Checks if input is an external Quick Payment URL/link or deep link rather than a phone number
 */
export function isQuickPayUrl(input: string): boolean {
  const s = normalizeArabicNumerals(input || '').trim();
  if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(s)) return true;
  if (/^intent:\/\//i.test(s)) return true;
  if (/^android-app:?\/\//i.test(s) || /^android-app\/\//i.test(s)) return true;
  if (/^(vfcash|orangecash|etisalatcash|wepay|ipn|instapay):\/\//i.test(s)) return true;

  const hasDomain = s.includes('.eg') || s.includes('.com') || s.includes('.net') || s.includes('.me') || s.includes('.io') || s.startsWith('www.') || s.includes('/');
  const digitsOnly = s.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');

  if (hasDomain && (s.includes('/') || s.includes('www.') || digitsOnly.length < 8)) {
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/**
 * Shared display metadata for every wallet/quick-pay provider.
 */

  etisalat: {
    nameAr: 'اتصالات كاش',
    nameEn: 'Etisalat Cash (e&)',
    companyNameAr: 'إي آند مصر (اتصالات) - e& Egypt',
    prefix: '011',
    brandColor: '#719E19',
  },
  we: {
    nameAr: 'وي باي (WE Pay)',
    nameEn: 'WE Pay',
    companyNameAr: 'المصرية للاتصالات - Telecom Egypt (WE)',
    prefix: '015',
    brandColor: '#7E22CE',
  },
  instapay: {
    nameAr: 'إنستاباي',
    nameEn: 'InstaPay',
    companyNameAr: 'شبكة المدفوعات اللحظية - InstaPay Egypt',
    prefix: 'IPN',
    brandColor: '#A855F7',
  },
  generic: {
    nameAr: 'محفظة كاش إلكترونية',
    nameEn: 'Smart Cash Wallet',
    companyNameAr: 'محفظة دفع إلكترونية',
    prefix: '01X',
    brandColor: '#EAB308',
  },
};

export function getWalletProviderMeta(provider: WalletProvider) {
  return WALLET_PROVIDER_META[provider] || WALLET_PROVIDER_META.generic;
}

/**
 * Automatically detects the Egyptian mobile wallet operator OR InstaPay from a Quick Pay URL or deep link.
 */
export function detectWalletProviderFromUrl(url: string): WalletProvider {
  let lower = (url || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!lower) return 'generic';
  try {
    const decoded = decodeURIComponent(lower);
    if (decoded) lower = `${lower} ${decoded.toLowerCase()}`;
  } catch {
    // ignore malformed URI components
  }

  // 1. InstaPay Egypt Quick Pay Links (e.g. https://ipn.eg/S/..., instapay://..., com.instapay...)
  if (
    lower.includes('ipn.eg') ||
    lower.includes('instapay') ||
    /^ipn:\/\//i.test(lower)
  ) {
    return 'instapay';
  }

  // 2. Vodafone Cash (Vodafone Egypt / Ana Vodafone / vf.eg / vfcash)
  if (
    lower.includes('vodafone') ||
    lower.includes('voda') ||
    lower.includes('vf.eg') ||
    lower.includes('vfcash') ||
    lower.includes('anavodafone') ||
    lower.includes('anafodafone') ||
    lower.includes('com.vf')
  ) {
    return 'vodafone';
  }

  // 3. Orange Cash
  if (
    lower.includes('orange') ||
    lower.includes('orangecash') ||
    lower.includes('com.orange')
  ) {
    return 'orange';
  }

  // 4. Etisalat Cash (e& Egypt)
  if (
    lower.includes('etisalat') ||
    lower.includes('etisalatcash') ||
    lower.includes('e&') ||
    lower.includes('eand') ||
    lower.includes('e-and') ||
    lower.includes('com.etisalat')
  ) {
    return 'etisalat';
  }

  // 5. WE Pay (Telecom Egypt)
  if (
    lower.includes('te.eg') ||
    lower.includes('telecom') ||
    lower.includes('wepay') ||
    lower.includes('we-pay') ||
    lower.includes('we.eg') ||
    /(?:^|[^a-z])we(?:pay|cash|_pay|-pay)?(?:[^a-z]|$)/i.test(lower)
  ) {
    return 'we';
  }

  return 'generic';
}

/**
 * Robustly sanitizes and converts any Quick Pay Link (including Android app links,
 * share intents, and web links) into a safe, clickable URL that never triggers
 * Chrome's DNS_PROBE_FINISHED_NXDOMAIN or failed custom schemes.
 */
export function sanitizeQuickPayUrl(raw: string, provider?: WalletProvider): string {
  let s = (raw || '').trim();
  if (!s) return '';

  // 1. Clean double schemes and malformed android-app schemes:
  s = s.replace(/^https?:\/\/+android-app:?\/\//i, 'android-app://');
  s = s.replace(/^android-app:\/+(?!\/)/i, 'android-app://');
  s = s.replace(/^android-app\/\//i, 'android-app://');
  s = s.replace(/^https?:\/\/+(https?:\/\/)/i, '$1');

  // Handle instapay://send?to=...
  if (s.toLowerCase().startsWith('instapay://send?to=')) {
    const handle = decodeURIComponent(s.slice('instapay://send?to='.length)).replace(/@instapay$/i, '').trim();
    return handle ? `https://ipn.eg/${encodeURIComponent(handle)}` : 'https://ipn.eg';
  }

  // 2. Check if the string contains an embedded web URL (common in Android share intents)
  const embeddedMatch = s.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+/i) || s.match(/https?\/[^\s"'<>]+/i);
  if (embeddedMatch) {
    let clean = embeddedMatch[0];
    if (clean.startsWith('https/') || clean.startsWith('http/')) {
      clean = clean.replace(/^https?\//, 'https://');
    }
    return clean;
  }

  // 3. Handle Android app links: android-app://<package>/...
  if (s.startsWith('android-app://')) {
    const pkg = s.replace('android-app://', '').split('/')[0].trim().toLowerCase();

    // InstaPay
    if (pkg.includes('instapay') || provider === 'instapay') {
      return 'https://ipn.eg';
    }
    // Vodafone / Ana Vodafone
    if (pkg.includes('vf') || pkg.includes('vodafone') || provider === 'vodafone') {
      return `intent://#Intent;package=com.vf.eg;scheme=anafodafone;S.browser_fallback_url=https%3A%2F%2Fweb.vodafone.com.eg%2Far%2Fvodafone-cash;end`;
    }
    // Orange Cash
    if (pkg.includes('orange') || provider === 'orange') {
      return `intent://#Intent;package=com.orange.mail.egypt;scheme=orangecash;S.browser_fallback_url=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.orange.eg%2Far%2Forange-cash;end`;
    }
    // Etisalat Cash
    if (pkg.includes('etisalat') || pkg.includes('eand') || provider === 'etisalat') {
      return `intent://#Intent;package=com.etisalat;scheme=etisalatcash;S.browser_fallback_url=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.etisalat.eg;end`;
    }
    // WE Pay
    if (pkg.includes('we') || pkg.includes('telecom') || provider === 'we') {
      return `intent://#Intent;package=eg.telecom.we;scheme=wepay;S.browser_fallback_url=https%3A%2F%2Fte.eg;end`;
    }
    if (pkg) {
      return `https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=${pkg}`;
    }
    return 'https://web.vodafone.com.eg/ar/vodafone-cash';
  }

  // 4. Intent links are left valid
  if (s.startsWith('intent://')) {
    return s;
  }

  // 5. Normal web links
  if (!s.startsWith('http://') && !s.startsWith('https://')) {
    s = `https://${s}`;
  }

  return s;
}

export interface DetectedWalletInfo {
  provider: WalletProvider;
  nameAr: string;
  nameEn: string;
  companyNameAr: string;
  cleanPhone: string;
  formattedPhone: string;
  brandColor: string;
}

/**
 * Detects the Egyptian mobile wallet operator from the phone number prefix
 * 010 / ٠١٠ -> Vodafone Cash
 * 012 / ٠١٢ -> Orange Cash
 * 011 / ٠١١ -> Etisalat Cash
 * 015 / ٠١٥ -> WE Pay
 */
export function detectWalletInfo(rawPhone: string): DetectedWalletInfo {
  const normalized = normalizeArabicNumerals(rawPhone || '');
  let cleanPhone = normalized.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');

  // Strip international country codes (+20, 0020, 20)
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('0020') && cleanPhone.length === 14) {
    cleanPhone = cleanPhone.slice(4);
  } else if (cleanPhone.startsWith('20') && cleanPhone.length === 12) {
    cleanPhone = cleanPhone.slice(2);
  }

  // Prepend missing leading zero (e.g. 1012345678 -> 01012345678)
  if (cleanPhone.length === 10 && cleanPhone.startsWith('1')) {
    cleanPhone = '0' + cleanPhone;
  }

  // Formatted display (010 1234 5678)
  let formattedPhone = cleanPhone;
  if (cleanPhone.length === 11) {
    formattedPhone = `${cleanPhone.slice(0, 3)} ${cleanPhone.slice(3, 7)} ${cleanPhone.slice(7)}`;
  }

  // 010 -> Vodafone Cash
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('010')) {
    return {
      provider: 'vodafone',
      nameAr: 'ڨودافون كاش',
      nameEn: 'Vodafone Cash',
      companyNameAr: 'فودافون مصر - Vodafone Egypt',
      cleanPhone,
      formattedPhone,
      brandColor: '#E60000',
    };
  }

  // 012 -> Orange Cash
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('012')) {
    return {
      provider: 'orange',
      nameAr: 'أورانج كاش',
      nameEn: 'Orange Cash',
      companyNameAr: 'أورانج مصر - Orange Egypt',
      cleanPhone,
      formattedPhone,
      brandColor: '#FF7900',
    };
  }

  // 011 -> Etisalat Cash
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('011')) {
    return {
      provider: 'etisalat',
      nameAr: 'اتصالات كاش',
      nameEn: 'Etisalat Cash (e&)',
      companyNameAr: 'إي آند مصر (اتصالات) - e& Egypt',
      cleanPhone,
      formattedPhone,
      brandColor: '#719E19',
    };
  }

  // 015 -> WE Pay
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('015')) {
    return {
      provider: 'we',
      nameAr: 'وي باي',
      nameEn: 'WE Pay',
      companyNameAr: 'المصرية للاتصالات - Telecom Egypt (WE)',
      cleanPhone,
      formattedPhone,
      brandColor: '#592C82',
    };
  }

  return {
    provider: 'generic',
    nameAr: 'محفظة كاش إلكترونية',
    nameEn: 'Smart Cash Wallet',
    companyNameAr: 'المحفظة الذكية',
    cleanPhone: cleanPhone || rawPhone,
    formattedPhone: cleanPhone || rawPhone,
    brandColor: '#EAB308',
  };
}

/**
 * Formats and normalizes TikTok username or links to the standard Universal / App Link format:
 * https://www.tiktok.com/@<clean_lowercase_username>
 * This guarantees the TikTok app opens directly to the profile on mobile devices,
 * or cleanly loads the profile page on the web, preventing it from just opening the app's home feed.
 */
export function formatTikTokTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  // 1. Remove all invisible zero-width and RTL/LTR formatting marks
  let clean = normalizeArabicNumerals(input)
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (!clean) return '';

  // 2. TikTok short video/profile share links (vm.tiktok.com / vt.tiktok.com)
  if (/^(?:https?:\/\/)?(?:vm|vt)\.tiktok\.com\//i.test(clean)) {
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }

  // 3. Full or partial tiktok.com URL
  if (clean.includes('tiktok.com')) {
    const parts = clean.split('?');
    const pathPart = parts[0];
    const queryPart = parts[1] ? `?${parts[1]}` : '';

    const match = pathPart.match(/tiktok\.com\/(?:@)?([a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      const segment = match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const reservedRoutes = ['discover', 'explore', 'upload', 'live', 'tag', 'music', 'about', 'legal', 'business', 'share', 'foryou'];
      if (!reservedRoutes.includes(segment)) {
        return `https://www.tiktok.com/@${segment}${queryPart}`;
      }
    }
    // If it's a specific link with video ID or something, just ensure https://
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }

  // 4. Raw username (e.g. foodhunteregypt, @foodhunteregypt, FoodHunterEgypt, /@Foodhunteregypt)
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w.-]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();

  if (username) {
    return `https://www.tiktok.com/@${username}`;
  }

  return clean;
}

/**
 * Normalizes Instagram usernames/URLs to https://www.instagram.com/<clean_lowercase_username>/
 */
export function formatInstagramTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = normalizeArabicNumerals(input)
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (clean.includes('instagram.com/')) {
    const match = clean.match(/instagram\.com\/([a-zA-Z0-9._]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      const user = match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim();
      return `https://www.instagram.com/${user}/`;
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w.]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (username) {
    return `https://www.instagram.com/${username}/`;
  }
  return clean;
}

/**
 * Normalizes Telegram usernames/URLs to https://t.me/<clean_lowercase_username>
 */
export function formatTelegramTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = input
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (clean.includes('t.me/') || clean.includes('telegram.me/')) {
    const match = clean.match(/(?:t\.me|telegram\.me)\/([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return `https://t.me/${match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim()}`;
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (username) {
    return `https://t.me/${username}`;
  }
  return clean;
}

/**
 * Normalizes Snapchat usernames/URLs to https://www.snapchat.com/add/<clean_lowercase_username>
 */
export function formatSnapchatTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = input
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (clean.includes('snapchat.com/add/')) {
    const match = clean.match(/snapchat\.com\/add\/([a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return `https://www.snapchat.com/add/${match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim()}`;
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w.-]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (username) {
    return `https://www.snapchat.com/add/${username}`;
  }
  return clean;
}

/**
 * Analyzes user input and produces clean, universal URLs, deep links,
 * or bank details that work flawlessly across mobile and desktop.
 */
export function processSmartLink(rawInput: string, preferredType?: AppType): DeepLinkInfo {
  const sanitized = cleanAndSanitizeUrl(rawInput);
  const trimmed = sanitized.trim();

  // Handle Bank Type
  if (preferredType === 'bank' || trimmed.startsWith('bank://')) {
    const bankDetails = decodeBankDetails(trimmed) || {
      bankName: 'حساب بنكي',
      accountNumber: trimmed.replace(/^https?:\/\//, ''),
    };

    return {
      appType: 'bank',
      appTitleAr: bankDetails.bankName || 'حساب بنكي',
      schemeUrl: trimmed.startsWith('bank://') ? trimmed : encodeBankDetails(bankDetails),
      fallbackUrl: '#',
      displayUrl: bankDetails.accountNumber ? `${bankDetails.bankName}: ${bankDetails.accountNumber}` : 'بيانات الحساب البنكي',
      isBankCard: true,
      bankDetails,
    };
  }

  // Handle Smart Wallet (Vodafone Cash, Etisalat, Orange, WE Pay)
  if (preferredType === 'wallet' || trimmed.startsWith('tel:*9*7*')) {
    const normalized = normalizeArabicNumerals(trimmed);
    let cleanTrimmed = normalized;
    if (cleanTrimmed.startsWith('tel:*9*7*')) {
      cleanTrimmed = cleanTrimmed.replace('tel:*9*7*', '').replace('#', '').replace('%23', '');
    }

    // 1. Check if input is a Web URL or Quick Payment Link (e.g. https://ipn.eg/..., https://vf.eg/..., etc.)
    if (isQuickPayUrl(cleanTrimmed)) {
      const detectedProv: WalletProvider = detectWalletProviderFromUrl(cleanTrimmed);
      const fullUrl = sanitizeQuickPayUrl(cleanTrimmed, detectedProv);
      const activeMeta = getWalletProviderMeta(detectedProv);

      return {
        appType: 'wallet',
        appTitleAr: `${activeMeta.nameAr} - ${activeMeta.nameEn}`,
        schemeUrl: fullUrl,
        fallbackUrl: fullUrl,
        displayUrl: fullUrl.replace(/^https?:\/\//, ''),
        isWalletCard: true, // Show dedicated luxury card with logo, company name, and Quick Pay button!
        walletProvider: detectedProv,
        walletProviderNameAr: activeMeta.nameAr,
        walletProviderNameEn: activeMeta.nameEn,
        walletCompanyNameAr: activeMeta.companyNameAr,
        walletPhone: fullUrl,
      };
    }

    // 2. Otherwise, input is a wallet phone number (e.g. 010..., 012..., 011..., 015..., ٠١٠...)
    const detected = detectWalletInfo(cleanTrimmed);

    return {
      appType: 'wallet',
      appTitleAr: `${detected.nameAr} - ${detected.nameEn}`,
      schemeUrl: '', // Dedicated copy screen only - NO intent or USSD
      fallbackUrl: '',
      displayUrl: detected.cleanPhone,
      isWalletCard: true, // Show dedicated luxury card with logo, company name, phone, and copy button ONLY!
      walletProvider: detected.provider,
      walletProviderNameAr: detected.nameAr,
      walletProviderNameEn: detected.nameEn,
      walletCompanyNameAr: detected.companyNameAr,
      walletPhone: detected.cleanPhone,
      walletFormattedPhone: detected.formattedPhone,
    };
  }

  if (!trimmed) {
    return {
      appType: preferredType || 'website',
      appTitleAr: 'رابط ويب',
      schemeUrl: '',
      fallbackUrl: '',
      displayUrl: '',
    };
  }

  // 1. WhatsApp
  const isWhatsAppInput =
    preferredType === 'whatsapp' ||
    trimmed.includes('wa.me') ||
    trimmed.includes('whatsapp.com') ||
    trimmed.startsWith('whatsapp://') ||
    (!preferredType && /^(?:\+?20|0)?1[0125]\d{8}$/.test(rawInput.trim()));

  if (isWhatsAppInput) {
    let phoneDigits = '';
    let customText = '';

    if (trimmed.includes('wa.me/')) {
      const parts = trimmed.split('wa.me/')[1]?.split('?') || [];
      phoneDigits = parts[0]?.replace(/[^\d]/g, '') || '';
      if (parts[1]?.includes('text=')) {
        customText = new URLSearchParams(parts[1]).get('text') || '';
      }
    } else if (trimmed.includes('phone=')) {
      try {
        const urlObj = new URL(trimmed.startsWith('http') ? trimmed : `https://${trimmed}`);
        phoneDigits = urlObj.searchParams.get('phone')?.replace(/[^\d]/g, '') || '';
        customText = urlObj.searchParams.get('text') || '';
      } catch {
        phoneDigits = trimmed.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
      }
    } else {
      phoneDigits = rawInput.trim().replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
    }

    // Auto format Egyptian numbers: 010... -> 2010...
    if (phoneDigits.startsWith('01') && phoneDigits.length === 11) {
      phoneDigits = '2' + phoneDigits;
    }

    const textQueryParam = customText ? `?text=${encodeURIComponent(customText)}` : '';
    const universalUrl = `https://wa.me/${phoneDigits}${textQueryParam}`;

    return {
      appType: 'whatsapp',
      appTitleAr: 'واتساب WhatsApp',
      schemeUrl: universalUrl,
      fallbackUrl: universalUrl,
      displayUrl: `wa.me/${phoneDigits}`,
    };
  }

  // 2. Phone Call
  const isPhone =
    preferredType === 'phone' ||
    trimmed.startsWith('tel:') ||
    (!preferredType && /^(\+|00)?\d{7,15}$/.test(rawInput.trim().replace(/[\s-]/g, '')));

  if (isPhone) {
    const cleanNumber = rawInput.trim().replace(/[^\d+]/g, '');
    const telUrl = `tel:${cleanNumber}`;
    return {
      appType: 'phone',
      appTitleAr: 'اتصال هاتفي',
      schemeUrl: telUrl,
      fallbackUrl: telUrl,
      displayUrl: cleanNumber,
    };
  }

  // 3. InstaPay Egypt (Quick Pay Link ONLY - Direct instant redirect)
  const isInstaPay =
    preferredType === 'instapay' ||
    trimmed.includes('ipn.eg') ||
    trimmed.includes('instapay');

  if (isInstaPay) {
    let paymentUrl = trimmed;
    if (paymentUrl.toLowerCase().startsWith('instapay://send?to=')) {
      const handle = decodeURIComponent(paymentUrl.replace(/^instapay:\/\/send\?to=/i, ''))
        .replace(/@instapay$/i, '')
        .trim();
      paymentUrl = `https://ipn.eg/${handle}`;
    } else if (paymentUrl.startsWith('ipn.eg/')) {
      paymentUrl = `https://${paymentUrl}`;
    } else if (!paymentUrl.startsWith('http://') && !paymentUrl.startsWith('https://')) {
      if (paymentUrl.includes('ipn.eg')) {
        paymentUrl = `https://${paymentUrl}`;
      } else {
        paymentUrl = `https://ipn.eg/${paymentUrl.replace(/^@/, '').replace(/@instapay$/i, '')}`;
      }
    }

    return {
      appType: 'instapay',
      appTitleAr: 'إنستاباي InstaPay',
      schemeUrl: paymentUrl,
      fallbackUrl: paymentUrl,
      displayUrl: paymentUrl.replace(/^https?:\/\//, ''),
      isInstaPayCard: false, // Direct instant redirect to InstaPay quick payment link!
    };
  }



  // 4. Instagram
  const isInstagram =
    preferredType === 'instagram' ||
    trimmed.includes('instagram.com') ||
    (preferredType === undefined && rawInput.trim().startsWith('@') && !trimmed.includes('instapay'));

  if (isInstagram) {
    const universalUrl = formatInstagramTargetUrl(trimmed || rawInput);
    const match = universalUrl.match(/instagram\.com\/([a-zA-Z0-9._]+)/);
    const username = match ? match[1] : trimmed.replace(/^@/, '');

    return {
      appType: 'instagram',
      appTitleAr: 'إنستجرام Instagram',
      schemeUrl: universalUrl,
      fallbackUrl: universalUrl,
      displayUrl: `@${username}`,
    };
  }

  // 5. Facebook
  const isFacebook =
    preferredType === 'facebook' ||
    trimmed.includes('facebook.com') ||
    trimmed.includes('fb.me') ||
    trimmed.includes('fb.watch');

  if (isFacebook) {
    let cleanUrl = trimmed;
    if (!cleanUrl.startsWith('http://') && !cleanUrl.startsWith('https://')) {
      cleanUrl = `https://www.facebook.com/${cleanUrl.replace(/^www\./, '')}`;
    }
    return {
      appType: 'facebook',
      appTitleAr: 'فيسبوك Facebook',
      schemeUrl: cleanUrl,
      fallbackUrl: cleanUrl,
      displayUrl: cleanUrl.replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.)?facebook\.com\/?/, 'fb.com/'),
    };
  }

  // 6. Telegram
  const isTelegram =
    preferredType === 'telegram' ||
    trimmed.includes('t.me') ||
    trimmed.includes('telegram.me');

  if (isTelegram) {
    const universalUrl = formatTelegramTargetUrl(trimmed || rawInput);
    const match = universalUrl.match(/(?:t\.me|telegram\.me)\/([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/);
    const handle = match ? match[1] : trimmed.replace(/^@/, '');

    return {
      appType: 'telegram',
      appTitleAr: 'تيليجرام Telegram',
      schemeUrl: universalUrl,
      fallbackUrl: universalUrl,
      displayUrl: `t.me/${handle}`,
    };
  }

  // 7. TikTok (Direct App & Profile Universal Link)
  const isTikTok =
    preferredType === 'tiktok' ||
    trimmed.includes('tiktok.com');

  if (isTikTok) {
    const universalUrl = formatTikTokTargetUrl(trimmed || rawInput);
    const match = universalUrl.match(/tiktok\.com\/@([a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+)/i);
    const displayUser = match ? match[1] : trimmed.replace(/^@/, '');

    return {
      appType: 'tiktok',
      appTitleAr: 'تيك توك TikTok',
      schemeUrl: universalUrl,
      fallbackUrl: universalUrl,
      displayUrl: `tiktok.com/@${displayUser}`,
    };
  }

  // 8. Snapchat
  const isSnapchat =
    preferredType === 'snapchat' ||
    trimmed.includes('snapchat.com');

  if (isSnapchat) {
    let user = trimmed;
    if (trimmed.includes('snapchat.com/add/')) {
      user = trimmed.split('snapchat.com/add/')[1]?.split('?')[0] || '';
    } else {
      user = rawInput.trim().replace(/^@/, '');
    }
    const universalUrl = `https://www.snapchat.com/add/${user}`;
    return {
      appType: 'snapchat',
      appTitleAr: 'سناب شات Snapchat',
      schemeUrl: universalUrl,
      fallbackUrl: universalUrl,
      displayUrl: `snapchat.com/add/${user}`,
    };
  }

  // 9. Standard Web URL / Fallback
  let webUrl = trimmed;
  if (!webUrl.startsWith('http://') && !webUrl.startsWith('https://')) {
    webUrl = `https://${webUrl}`;
  }

  return {
    appType: preferredType || 'website',
    appTitleAr: 'رابط ويب',
    schemeUrl: webUrl,
    fallbackUrl: webUrl,
    displayUrl: webUrl.replace(/^https?:\/\//, ''),
  };
}

/**
 * Triggers instant direct opening of the target URL or app.
 * IMPORTANT: Checks if running inside an iframe (like AI Studio preview),
 * avoiding the X-Frame-Options: DENY iframe crash.
 */
export function triggerSmartRedirect(linkInfo: DeepLinkInfo): void {
  const target = linkInfo.schemeUrl || linkInfo.fallbackUrl;
  if (!target || target === '#' || target.startsWith('intent:') || !isSafeUrlScheme(target)) return;

  const inIframe = isRunningInIframe();

  if (inIframe) {
    // If inside an iframe (AI Studio preview):
    // Trying window.location.replace() to an external domain (e.g. wa.me, ipn.eg)
    // will be blocked by the browser with X-Frame-Options: DENY.
    // Instead, open in top window or new window:
    try {
      if (window.top) {
        window.top.location.href = target;
        return;
      }
    } catch {
      // Cross-origin top access blocked, open in new tab
    }
    window.open(target, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
  } else {
    // On real mobile device or standalone browser tab:
    try {
      window.location.replace(target);
    } catch {
      window.location.href = target;
    }
  }
}
