              {/* Info guidelines */}
              <div className="bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl p-4 text-xs text-slate-300 space-y-1.5 leading-relaxed">
                <p className="font-bold text-amber-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                  قواعد التوليد التلقائي:
                </p>
                <p>• سيقوم النظام بإنشاء <strong>{isNaN(parsedCount) || parsedCount < 1 ? '—' : parsedCount}</strong> معرّف ID فريد ومستقل لكل كود باستخدام nanoid(10).</p>
                <p>• جميع الأكواد تُحفظ فورياً في قاعدة البيانات المركزية وتكون جاهزة للمسح أو التعديل.</p>
                <p>• الرابط الثابت داخل كل كود هو: <span className="font-mono text-amber-400 font-bold" dir="ltr">{domainUrl}/q/[id]</span></p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-2xl p-5 text-white">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-7 h-7 text-amber-400 flex-shrink-0" />
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-black text-sm text-amber-300">تم توليد {generatedCodes.length} كود بنجاح!</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">تم حفظ المعرفات الفريدة في قاعدة البيانات وهي جاهزة للطباعة والتوزيع الآن.</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={handleDownloadAll}
                  className="w-full sm:w-auto flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl shadow-md transition"
                >
                  <Download className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
                  تحميل الكل دفعة واحدة (PNG)
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Grid of Generated QR Codes */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 max-h-[360px] overflow-y-auto p-1">
                {generatedCodes.map(({ record, qrDataUrl }, idx) => (
                  <div
                    key={record.id}
                    className="p-3 bg-[#141418] rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/20 shadow-md hover:border-amber-400/40 transition flex flex-col items-center text-center group"
                  >
                    <div className="relative p-1.5 bg-white border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl shadow-xs">
                      <img
                        src={qrDataUrl}
                        alt={`QR ${record.id}`}
                        className="w-28 h-28 object-contain"
                      />
                      <span className="absolute top-1 right-1 text-[10px] font-mono bg-black/80 text-amber-300 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md">
                        #{idx + 1}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <span className="mt-2 text-xs font-mono font-black text-amber-300 tracking-wider bg-black/60 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg border border-amber-500/20" dir="ltr">
                      {record.id}
                    </span>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleDownloadSingle(qrDataUrl, record.id)}
                      className="mt-2 w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-1 py-1.5 px-2 bg-white/5 hover:bg-amber-400 hover:text-black text-amber-300 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition border border-amber-500/20"
                    >
                      <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      تنزيل الكود
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 px-6 py-4 border-t border-amber-500/20 bg-black/60">
          {!completed ? (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={reset}
                className="px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-xl transition"
              >
                إلغاء
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleGenerate}
                disabled={loading || !countInput || parsedCount < 1}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 disabled:opacity-40 text-black font-black text-xs rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 transition cursor-pointer"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin text-black" />
                    <span>جاري التوليد...</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
                    <span>توليد {parsedCount || ''} كود الآن</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={reset}
              className="px-6 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 text-black font-black text-xs rounded-xl transition shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 cursor-pointer"
            >
              تم والعودة للوحة التحكم
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/PrintSheetModal.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Printer, X, PackageCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import { batchUpdateQrPrintedStatus, generateQrDataUrl } from '../lib/qrService';
import { QrCodeRecord } from '../types/qr';

interface PrintSheetModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  records: QrCodeRecord[];
  domainUrl: string;
  onMarkedPrinted?: (ids: string[]) => void;
}

export function PrintSheetModal({ isOpen, onClose, records, domainUrl, onMarkedPrinted }: PrintSheetModalProps) {
  const [qrMap, setQrMap] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [columns, setColumns] = useState<number>(3);
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState<'all' | 'unprinted' | 'inactive' | 'active'>('unprinted');
  const [markingPrinted, setMarkingPrinted] = useState(false);

  const filtered = records.filter((r) => {
    if (!r || !r.id) return false;
    if (filterType === 'unprinted') return !r.is_printed;
    if (filterType === 'inactive') return !r.is_active;
    if (filterType === 'active') return r.is_active;
    return true;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    let isMounted = true;
    const generateAll = async () => {
      const map: Record<string, string> = {};
      for (const record of filtered) {
        const fullScanUrl = `${domainUrl}/q/${record.id}`;
        map[record.id] = await generateQrDataUrl(fullScanUrl, 400);
      }
      if (isMounted) setQrMap(map);
    };

    generateAll();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [isOpen, filterType, filtered.length, domainUrl]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handlePrint = () => {
    window.print();
  };

  const handleMarkShownPrinted = async () => {
    const ids = filtered.map((r) => r.id).filter(Boolean);
    if (ids.length === 0) return;
    setMarkingPrinted(true);
    try {
      await batchUpdateQrPrintedStatus(ids, true);
      onMarkedPrinted?.(ids);
    } finally {
      setMarkingPrinted(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-4xl bg-[#0e0e12] rounded-3xl shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.2)] border border-amber-500/35 overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[92vh] text-white">
        {/* Ambient Gold Glow */}
        <div className="absolute -top-12 -right-12 w-32 h-32 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-12 -left-12 w-32 h-32 bg-yellow-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Header - Not printed */}
        <div className="no-print flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-amber-500/20 bg-black/60">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black flex items-center justify-center shadow-md shadow-amber-500/20 font-black">
              <Printer className="w-5 h-5 text-black" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
                تجهيز وطباعة رموز الـ QR للبطاقات والملصقات
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">مخرجات مجهزة لورق الطباعة A4 بدقة عالية وتنسيق جاهز للقص المباشر</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 rounded-xl transition"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Toolbar - Not printed */}
        <div className="no-print px-6 py-3 bg-[#121216] border-b border-amber-500/20 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 flex-wrap">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="font-bold text-amber-200/90">تصفية:</span>
              <select
                value={filterType}
                onChange={(e) => setFilterType(e.target.value as any)}
                className="bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-amber-300 font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
              >
                <option value="unprinted">غير المطبوعة فقط (جاهزة للطباعة • {records.filter((r) => !r.is_printed).length})</option>
                <option value="all">جميع الأكواد ({records.length})</option>
                <option value="inactive">غير المفعلة فقط (جاهزة للعملاء)</option>
                <option value="active">المفعلة فقط</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="font-bold text-amber-200/90">أعمدة الورقة:</span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setColumns(2)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold transition whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                  columns === 2 
                    ? 'bg-amber-400 text-black border-amber-300 font-black shadow-md' 
                    : 'bg-black/40 text-slate-300 border-amber-500/20 hover:border-amber-400/40'
                }`}
              >
                عمودين (كروت كبيرة)
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setColumns(3)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold transition whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                  columns === 3 
                    ? 'bg-amber-400 text-black border-amber-300 font-black shadow-md' 
                    : 'bg-black/40 text-slate-300 border-amber-500/20 hover:border-amber-400/40'
                }`}
              >
                3 أعمدة (مقاس قياسي)
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
            {filtered.length > 0 && (
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={markingPrinted}
                onClick={handleMarkShownPrinted}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-cyan-500/15 hover:bg-cyan-400 hover:text-black text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-400/40 font-black rounded-xl transition whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <PackageCheck className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>{markingPrinted ? 'جاري التحديث...' : `تعليم المعروض كمطبوع ✓ (${filtered.length})`}</span>
              </button>
            )}

            <button
              onClick={handlePrint}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black font-black rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 transition whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Printer className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
              طباعة الورقة (Print A4)
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Printable Sheet Area */}
        <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto bg-black/40 flex-1">
          <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-xl min-h-[600px] border border-amber-500/20 text-black">
            {filtered.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-16 text-slate-400 font-bold">
                لا توجد أكواد مطابقة للتصفية المحددة.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div
                className={`grid gap-4 print-grid ${
                  columns === 2 ? 'grid-cols-2' : 'grid-cols-3'
                }`}
              >
                {filtered.map((r, i) => (
                  <div
                    key={r.id}
                    className="print-card p-4 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white flex flex-col items-center text-center relative"
                  >
                    {/* Header of sticker */}
                    <div className="w-full flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-bold pb-2 border-b border-slate-200">
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-black font-black font-mono">
                        <span>✦</span>
                        <span>Smart Card</span>
                      </span>
                      <span>#{i + 1}</span>
                    </div>

                    {/* Classic crisp black & white QR */}
                    <div className="my-3 p-1 bg-white">
                      {qrMap[r.id] ? (
                        <img
                          src={qrMap[r.id]}
                          alt={`QR ${r.id}`}
                          className="w-32 h-32 object-contain"
                        />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-32 h-32 bg-slate-100 animate-pulse rounded" />
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {/* Instructions and ID */}
                    <div className="w-full space-y-1">
                      <p className="text-[11px] font-black text-slate-900">
                        امسح الكود بكاميرا هاتفك
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-600 font-semibold">
                        {r.is_active ? 'فتح الرابط المباشر' : 'تفعيل الكارت الذكي'}
                      </p>
                      <div className="pt-2">
                        <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded tracking-widest inline-block border border-slate-300">
                          {r.id}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/UploadQrModal.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useRef, DragEvent } from 'react';
import { Upload, Search, AlertCircle, Loader2, X, Image as ImageIcon, Sparkles, PlusCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { decodeQrFromImageFile, extractQrIdFromUrl, lookupExistingQrCode, getQrCode } from '../lib/qrService';
import { QrCodeRecord } from '../types/qr';

interface UploadQrModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onCodeFound: (record: QrCodeRecord) => void;
}

export function UploadQrModal({ isOpen, onClose, onCodeFound }: UploadQrModalProps) {
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [notFoundId, setNotFoundId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [manualId, setManualId] = useState('');
  const [imagePreview, setImagePreview] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  // Process uploaded image file
  const handleProcessFile = async (file: File) => {
    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
      setErrorMsg('يرجى اختيار ملف صورة صالح (PNG, JPG, WEBP)');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setNotFoundId(null);

    const previewUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
    setImagePreview(previewUrl);

    try {
      const decodedText = await decodeQrFromImageFile(file);
      if (!decodedText) {
        setErrorMsg('تعذر قراءة رمز QR من الصورة المرفوعة. تأكد من وضوح الصورة وتوسط الرمز.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const extractedId = extractQrIdFromUrl(decodedText);
      const cleanId = extractedId || decodedText.trim();

      // Query database
      const record = await lookupExistingQrCode(cleanId);
      if (!record) {
        setNotFoundId(cleanId);
        setErrorMsg(`تم استخراج المعرّف [${cleanId}] ولكن الكود غير مسجل بقاعدة البيانات.`);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      // Found! Pass record to parent
      setLoading(false);
      onCodeFound(record);
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Scan error:', err);
      setErrorMsg('حدث خطأ أثناء معالجة الصورة.');
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDragOver = (e: DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDragging(true);
  };

  const handleDragLeave = () => {
    setIsDragging(false);
  };

  const handleDrop = (e: DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDragging(false);
    if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files[0]) {
      handleProcessFile(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);
    }
  };

  // Search by manual ID
  const handleManualSearch = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    let raw = manualId.trim();
    if (!raw) return;

    // Handle full URL pasted
    const extracted = extractQrIdFromUrl(raw);
    const cleanId = extracted || raw;

    setLoading(true);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setNotFoundId(null);

    try {
      const record = await lookupExistingQrCode(cleanId);
      if (!record) {
        setNotFoundId(cleanId);
        setErrorMsg(`لم يتم العثور على أي كود بالمعرّف "${cleanId}".`);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      setLoading(false);
      onCodeFound(record);
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Search error:', err);
      // Fallback
      try {
        const fallbackRecord = await getQrCode(cleanId);
        if (fallbackRecord) {
          setLoading(false);
          onCodeFound(fallbackRecord);
          onClose();
          return;
        }
      } catch {}
      setErrorMsg('تعذر البحث عن الكود، يرجى المحاولة مرة أخرى.');
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Create & Edit new card on the fly if not found
  const handleCreateAndEdit = () => {
    if (!notFoundId) return;
    const newRecord: QrCodeRecord = {
      id: notFoundId,
      is_active: false,
      target_url: null,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      total_scans: 0,
      subscription_months: 1,
      expires_at: new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
    };
    onCodeFound(newRecord);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleReset = () => {
    setImagePreview(null);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setNotFoundId(null);
    setManualId('');
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg bg-[#0e0e12] rounded-3xl shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.2)] border border-amber-500/30 overflow-hidden flex flex-col text-white">
        
        {/* Ambient gold glow */}
        <div className="absolute -top-12 -right-12 w-32 h-32 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-12 -left-12 w-32 h-32 bg-yellow-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-amber-500/20 bg-black/60 relative z-10">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black flex items-center justify-center shadow-md shadow-amber-500/20 font-black">
              <Upload className="w-5 h-5 text-black" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
                فحص وتعديل رمز الـ QR (رفع صورة أو بحث برقم الكارت)
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">ابحث برقم الكارت أو ارفع الصورة لجلب الكارت وتعديل بياناته واشتراكه</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={handleReset}
            className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 rounded-xl transition cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Content */}
        <div className="p-6 space-y-6 relative z-10">
          
          {/* Section 1: Drag & Drop Photo Area */}
          <div>
            <input
              type="file"
              ref={fileInputRef}
              accept="image/*"
              className="hidden"
              onChange={(e) => {
                if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
                  handleProcessFile(e.target.files[0]);
                }
              }}
            />

            <div
              onDragOver={handleDragOver}
              onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
              onDrop={handleDrop}
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              className={`relative border-2 border-dashed rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-8 text-center cursor-pointer transition flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[170px] ${
                isDragging
                  ? 'border-amber-400 bg-amber-400/10 scale-[0.99]'
                  : 'border-amber-500/30 hover:border-amber-400/70 bg-black/50 hover:bg-[#15151c]'
              }`}
            >
              {loading ? (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 text-amber-400">
                  <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin" />
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-amber-200">جاري قراءة وفك تشفير الـ QR Code...</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">تحليل مصفوفة الرمز واستخراج رقم الكارت</p>
                </div>
              ) : imagePreview ? (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
                  <img
                    src={imagePreview}
                    alt="Preview"
                    className="w-24 h-24 object-contain rounded-xl border border-amber-500/30 bg-white p-1.5 shadow-lg"
                  />
                  <span className="text-xs text-amber-300 font-bold underline">انقر لاختيار صورة أخرى</span>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-amber-400/15 border border-amber-400/30 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center mb-2.5">
                    <ImageIcon className="w-6 h-6" />
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-black text-slate-100">اسحب وأفلت صورة الـ QR هنا</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">أو انقر لتصفح واختيار صورة من جهازك (PNG, JPG, WEBP)</p>
                  <span className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-300">
                    <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400" />
                    <span>فك تشفير فوري واستدعاء الكارت</span>
                  </span>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Error Message */}
          {errorMsg && (
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4 bg-rose-500/15 border border-rose-500/35 rounded-2xl text-xs text-rose-300 animate-in fade-in">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 flex-shrink-0" />
                <span className="font-semibold">{errorMsg}</span>
              </div>
              {notFoundId && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleCreateAndEdit}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black font-black rounded-xl text-[11px] flex items-center gap-1 shadow-md hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 flex-shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <PlusCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>تسجيل هذا الكود وتعديله الآن</span>
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Divider with High-Contrast Label */}
          <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center py-1">
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
              <div className="w-full border-t border-amber-500/25" />
            </div>
            <span className="relative bg-[#0e0e12] px-4 text-xs font-black text-amber-400 tracking-wide">
              أو اكتب رقم الكارت مباشرة (ID)
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Section 2: Manual ID Input with Pristine High-Contrast Colors */}
          <form onSubmit={handleManualSearch} className="space-y-2.5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-200/90">
                أدخل معرّف أو رقم الكارت (ID):
              </label>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
                مثال: I9pFBZwn8Y
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5">
              <div className="relative flex-1">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="اكتب رقم الكارت هنا... مثل I9pFBZwn8Y"
                  value={manualId}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    setManualId(e.target.value);
                    setErrorMsg(null);
                    setNotFoundId(null);
                  }}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-black/80 border-2 border-amber-500/40 rounded-2xl text-amber-300 text-base font-mono font-black placeholder-slate-500 focus:bg-black focus:border-amber-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-amber-400/20 shadow-inner transition text-center sm:text-left selection:bg-amber-400 selection:text-black"
                  dir="ltr"
                  autoFocus
                />
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading || !manualId.trim()}
                className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 disabled:opacity-50 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-2xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/25 transition cursor-pointer active:scale-95 flex-shrink-0"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin text-black" />
                ) : (
                  <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
                )}
                <span>بحث وجلب الكارت</span>
              </button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 text-right">
              بمجرد النقر على "بحث وجلب الكارت" سيتم فتح نافذة التعديل مباشرة لتجديد الاشتراك، تعديل الرابط، أو تغيير المنصة.
            </p>
          </form>

        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/DeveloperSettingsModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, Check, ExternalLink, Sparkles, Phone, MessageSquare, Link, Globe } from 'lucide-react';
import { PlatformSettings } from '../types/qr';

interface DeveloperSettingsModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  settings: PlatformSettings;
  onSave: (updated: PlatformSettings) => Promise<void>;
}

export function DeveloperSettingsModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  settings,
  onSave,
}: DeveloperSettingsModalProps) {
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(settings.developer_whatsapp || '01550523365');
  const [contactUrl, setContactUrl] = useState(settings.developer_contact_url || 'https://wa.me/201550523365');
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(settings.developer_title || 'تواصل مع المطور');
  const [message, setMessage] = useState(settings.support_message || 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية');
  const [publicDomain, setPublicDomain] = useState(settings.public_domain_url || 'https://smart-card-inky.vercel.app');
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setPhone(settings.developer_whatsapp || '01550523365');
      setContactUrl(settings.developer_contact_url || 'https://wa.me/201550523365');
      setTitle(settings.developer_title || 'تواصل مع المطور');
      setMessage(settings.support_message || 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية');
      setPublicDomain(settings.public_domain_url || 'https://smart-card-inky.vercel.app');
      setSuccess(false);
    }
  }, [isOpen, settings]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  // Auto update direct contact URL when phone changes if using default wa.me scheme
  const handlePhoneChange = (val: string) => {
    setPhone(val);
    const clean = val.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
    if (clean) {
      const num = clean.startsWith('01') ? '2' + clean : clean;
      setContactUrl(`https://wa.me/${num}`);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsSaving(true);
    try {
      let finalUrl = contactUrl.trim();
      let cleanPhone = phone.trim();

      if (!finalUrl && cleanPhone) {
        let num = cleanPhone.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
        if (num.startsWith('01')) num = '2' + num;
        finalUrl = `https://wa.me/${num}`;
      }

      let cleanDomain = publicDomain.trim();
      if (cleanDomain.endsWith('/')) {
        cleanDomain = cleanDomain.slice(0, -1);
      }

      await onSave({
        developer_whatsapp: cleanPhone || '01550523365',
        developer_contact_url: finalUrl || 'https://wa.me/201550523365',
        developer_title: title.trim() || 'تواصل مع المطور',
        support_message: message.trim(),
        public_domain_url: cleanDomain,
      });
      setSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setSuccess(false);
        onClose();
      }, 1400);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleTestLink = () => {
    let url = contactUrl.trim();
    if (!url && phone.trim()) {
      let num = phone.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
      if (num.startsWith('01')) num = '2' + num;
      url = `https://wa.me/${num}`;
    }
    if (!url) url = 'https://wa.me/201550523365';
    window.open(url, '_blank');
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in">
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg bg-[#0e0e12] border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.2)] text-white space-y-6 relative overflow-hidden">
        {/* Ambient gold glow */}
        <div className="absolute -top-10 -right-10 w-28 h-28 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-10 -left-10 w-28 h-28 bg-yellow-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-amber-500/20 pb-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-400/20 to-yellow-600/10 border border-amber-400/40 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-400 shadow-inner">
              <MessageSquare className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h3 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
                  إعدادات التواصل مع المطور والتحويل
                </h3>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                تعديل رابط ورقم الواتساب الخاص بالتحويل عند حدوث مشكلة أو حذف الرمز
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-xl hover:bg-white/10 transition"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {success && (
          <div className="p-3.5 bg-amber-500/20 border border-amber-400/40 rounded-2xl text-xs text-amber-300 font-bold flex items-center gap-2 animate-in fade-in">
            <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span>تم حفظ إعدادات التواصل بنجاح! تم التحديث في السيرفر والروابط فوراً.</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Form */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          {/* WhatsApp Phone */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-200/90 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span>رقم واتساب المطور (الأدمن):</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={phone}
              onChange={(e) => handlePhoneChange(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="01550523365"
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
              dir="ltr"
              required
            />
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              الرقم الحالي المعتمد: <span className="font-mono text-amber-300 font-bold" dir="ltr">01550523365</span>
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Custom Link / URL */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-200/90 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Link className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span>الرابط المباشر للتحويل (URL):</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={contactUrl}
              onChange={(e) => setContactUrl(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="https://wa.me/201550523365"
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
              dir="ltr"
              required
            />
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              يمكنك كتابة رابط واتساب مباشر أو أي رابط مخصص تفضله للتحويل.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Button Text */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-200/90 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span>نص زر التواصل الظاهر للعميل:</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="تواصل مع المطور"
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* Public Production Domain (For printing & live cards scanning) */}
          <div className="space-y-2 p-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-500/10 via-yellow-500/5 to-transparent border border-amber-500/30 rounded-2xl">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Globe className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                <span>رابط الدومين الأونلاين للموقع (Public Production Domain):</span>
              </label>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-amber-400 bg-amber-400/20 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-amber-400/30">
                مهم للطباعة وهواتف العملاء
              </span>
            </div>
            <input
              type="url"
              value={publicDomain}
              onChange={(e) => setPublicDomain(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="مثال: https://cards.mybrand.com أو رابط السيرفر الأونلاين"
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-black/70 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl text-sm text-amber-200 placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
              dir="ltr"
            />
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
              💡 <strong>حل مشكلة المسح من هاتف آخر:</strong> إذا قمت بتوليد أو طباعة الرمز وأنت على السيرفر المحلي (localhost)، فلن يفتح في هاتف العميل الخارجي. اكتب رابط موقعك المرفوع أونلاين هنا وسيتم تضمينه تلقائياً داخل كل كود QR وكل كارت مطبوع!
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Action Buttons */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-2.5 pt-3 border-t border-amber-500/20">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestLink}
              className="w-full sm:w-auto px-4 py-2.5 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-amber-300 hover:text-amber-200 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4" />
              اختبار الرابط فوراً
            </button>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto sm:mr-auto justify-end">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              >
                إلغاء
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isSaving}
                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl shadow-[0_4px_20px_rgba(234,179,8,0.3)] transition transform active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {isSaving ? 'جاري الحفظ...' : 'حفظ الإعدادات'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/views/HomeLandingView.tsx

import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  QrCode as QrIcon, 
  Smartphone, 
  Camera, 
  Upload, 
  Search, 
  Sparkles, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  HelpCircle, 
  ArrowLeft, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  Lock,
  Building2,
  Wallet,
  MessageSquare,
  AlertCircle,
  Loader2,
  Phone
} from 'lucide-react';
import { decodeQrFromImageFile, extractQrIdFromUrl, getQrCode, fetchPlatformSettings } from '../lib/qrService';
import { QrScannerModal } from '../components/QrScannerModal';
import { PlatformSettings } from '../types/qr';

interface HomeLandingViewProps {
  onGoToAdmin: () => void;
  onSimulateScan: (id: string) => void;
  domainUrl: string;
}

export function HomeLandingView({ onGoToAdmin, onSimulateScan }: HomeLandingViewProps) {
  const [manualCode, setManualCode] = useState('');
  const [isScannerOpen, setIsScannerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [cameraDeclinedOnce, setCameraDeclinedOnce] = useState(false);
  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);
  const [feedbackMsg, setFeedbackMsg] = useState<{ type: 'error' | 'success'; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<PlatformSettings>({
    developer_whatsapp: '01550523365',
    developer_contact_url: 'https://wa.me/201550523365',
    developer_title: 'تواصل مع المطور',
    support_message: 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية',
  });
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchPlatformSettings().then((s) => {
      if (s) setSettings(s);
    });
  }, []);

  // Submit manual card ID
  const handleManualSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const cleanId = manualCode.trim();
    if (!cleanId) {
      setFeedbackMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'يرجى كتابة كود البطاقة أولاً' });
      return;
    }

    setFeedbackMsg(null);
    try {
      const record = await getQrCode(cleanId);
      if (record) {
        window.location.href = `/q/${record.id}`;
      } else {
        setFeedbackMsg({ type: 'error', text: `كود البطاقة "${cleanId}" غير موجود أو غير صحيح.` });
      }
    } catch {
      window.location.href = `/q/${cleanId}`;
    }
  };

  // Upload card/QR photo
  const handlePhotoUpload = async (file: File) => {
    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
      setFeedbackMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'يرجى اختيار ملف صورة صالح (PNG أو JPG).' });
      return;
    }

    setIsUploading(true);
    setFeedbackMsg(null);

    try {
      const decodedText = await decodeQrFromImageFile(file);
      if (!decodedText) {
        setFeedbackMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'تعذر قراءة رمز QR من الصورة المرفوعة. تأكد من وضوح الصورة وتوسط الرمز.' });
        setIsUploading(false);
        return;
      }

      const extractedId = extractQrIdFromUrl(decodedText);
      if (!extractedId) {
        setFeedbackMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'تم فحص الرمز ولكن لم يتم العثور على كود بطاقة صحيح.' });
        setIsUploading(false);
        return;
      }

      window.location.href = `/q/${extractedId}`;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      setFeedbackMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'حدث خطأ أثناء فحص الصورة. جرب إدخال الكود يدويًا.' });
      setIsUploading(false);
    }
  };

  const getWaLink = () => {
    if (settings.developer_contact_url?.trim()) {
      return settings.developer_contact_url.trim();
    }
    const clean = (settings.developer_whatsapp || '01550523365').replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
    const num = clean.startsWith('01') ? '2' + clean : clean;
    return `https://wa.me/${num}`;
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-white selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-black flex flex-col justify-between">
      {/* Top Customer Header */}
      <header className="border-b border-amber-500/20 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-md sticky top-0 z-30">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 h-16 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <img 
              src="/logo.png" 
              alt="Smart Card" 
              className="w-10 h-10 object-contain rounded-2xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 p-0.5 bg-[#121216] border border-amber-400/40 animate-3d-float" 
            />
            <div>
              <span className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black tracking-wider text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500 font-mono block">
                Smart Card
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <a
              href={getWaLink()}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-amber-400/10 hover:bg-amber-400/20 border border-amber-400/30 text-amber-300 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition"
              title="تواصل مع المطور"
            >
              <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">تواصل مع المطور</span>
            </a>

            <button
              onClick={() => setIsScannerOpen(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl transition shadow-md shadow-amber-500/20"
            >
              <Camera className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
              <span>مسح البطاقة</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Customer Portal Content */}
      <main className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-10 sm:py-16 w-full space-y-10">
        {/* Welcome & Instructions */}
        <div className="text-center space-y-4">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/30 text-amber-300 text-xs font-bold">
            <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            منصة الفحص والتوجيه الفوري — Smart Card
          </div>

          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-black text-white leading-tight">
            أهلاً بك في منصة
            <span className="bg-gradient-to-l from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent block mt-1.5 font-mono">
              Smart Card
            </span>
          </h1>

          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 max-w-lg mx-auto leading-relaxed">
            إذا قمت بمسح بطاقتك الذكية أو واجهتك أي مشكلة، يمكنك فحص البطاقة مباشرة بالكاميرا، أو رفع صورتها، أو كتابة كود البطاقة بالأسفل للوصول الفوري لبياناتك وتطبيقاتك.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Feedback message banner */}
        {feedbackMsg && (
          <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl text-xs flex items-center gap-3 animate-in fade-in ${
            feedbackMsg.type === 'error'
              ? 'bg-rose-500/15 border border-rose-500/30 text-rose-300'
              : 'bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300'
          }`}>
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span className="flex-1 font-semibold">{feedbackMsg.text}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Re-appear Camera Scan Choice Banner after first decline */}
        {cameraDeclinedOnce && !isScannerOpen && (
          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/35 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-right">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/20 border border-amber-400/30 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                <Camera className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-extrabold text-amber-200">
                  خيار مسح الرمز بالكاميرا متاح مرة أخرى
                </p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300">
                  تم إغلاق نافذة إذن الكاميرا — يمكنك إظهار اختيار التفعيل ومسح الرمز الآن في أي وقت.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setCameraDeclinedOnce(false);
                setIsScannerOpen(true);
              }}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black font-black text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md transition flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Camera className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-black" />
              <span>إظهار مسح الرمز بالكاميرا</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Hidden File Input for Image Upload */}
        <input
          type="file"
          ref={fileInputRef}
          accept="image/*"
          className="hidden"
          onChange={(e) => {
            const file = e.target.files?.[0];
            if (file) handlePhotoUpload(file);
          }}
        />

        {/* Quick Customer Action Cards */}
        <div className="bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6 shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.08)] backdrop-blur-xl stage-3d animate-3d-enter">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 stage-3d">
            {/* Option 1: Live Camera Scan */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsScannerOpen(true)}
              className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-400/10 via-yellow-500/5 to-transparent hover:from-amber-400/20 hover:to-yellow-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 hover:border-amber-400/60 group text-right flex items-center gap-4 cursor-pointer card-3d-tilt"
            >
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/15 border border-amber-400/30 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 group-hover:scale-105 transition">
                <Camera className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-400" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-white">مسح فوري بالكاميرا</h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-200/70 mt-0.5">افتح كاميرا الهاتف واقرأ البطاقة مباشرة</p>
              </div>
            </button>

            {/* Option 2: Upload Photo */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              disabled={isUploading}
              className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10 hover:border-amber-400/40 group text-right flex items-center gap-4 cursor-pointer card-3d-tilt"
            >
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 group-hover:scale-105 transition">
                {isUploading ? (
                  <Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-400 animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <Upload className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-300" />
                )}
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-white">
                  {isUploading ? 'جاري فحص الصورة...' : 'رفع صورة البطاقة'}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">من ألبوم الصور أو لقطات الشاشة</p>
              </div>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Divider with text */}
          <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center py-2">
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
              <div className="w-full border-t border-amber-500/20" />
            </div>
            <span className="relative bg-[#121216] px-3 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-300/80">
              أو اكتب كود البطاقة المطبوع عليها
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Option 3: Manual Code Entry */}
          <form onSubmit={handleManualSubmit} className="space-y-3">
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5">
              <div className="relative flex-1">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={manualCode}
                  onChange={(e) => setManualCode(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="اكتب كود البطاقة المطبوع (مثال: k9X2mQp7)"
                  className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-sm font-mono text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400 transition text-center sm:text-right"
                  dir="ltr"
                />
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={!manualCode.trim()}
                className="px-6 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 disabled:opacity-40 text-black font-black text-xs sm:text-sm rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
                فتح البطاقة
              </button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-center text-slate-400">
              تجد الكود مطبوعاً بخط واضح على البطاقة الذكية أسفل رمز الـ QR
            </p>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* Customer Guidance & Feature Cards */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 pt-2">
          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl space-y-2 text-right">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/10 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Building2 className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white">التحويل البنكي المباشر</h4>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
              نسخ فوري لرقم الحساب والآيبان IBAN مع إمكانية فتح تطبيق البنك أو InstaPay بضغطة واحدة.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl space-y-2 text-right">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/10 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Wallet className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white">محافظ الكاش الإلكترونية</h4>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
              تحويل سريع عبر فودافون كاش، أورنج، اتصالات، وWE مع كود التحويل السريع المباشر.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl space-y-2 text-right">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/10 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <MessageSquare className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white">التواصل المباشر والواتساب</h4>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
              فتح محادثات واتساب، صفحات السوشيال ميديا، أو الاتصال الهاتفي فوراً دون الحاجة لتسجيل الرقم.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Customer FAQ / Help Section with Direct WhatsApp Support Button */}
        <div className="p-6 bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/20 rounded-3xl text-right space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-400 text-xs font-bold">
              <HelpCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
              تواجه صعوبة في مسح البطاقة أو تم حذف الكود؟
            </div>

            <a
              href={getWaLink()}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl shadow-md hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 transition"
            >
              <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>تواصل مع المطور: {settings.developer_whatsapp || '01550523365'}</span>
            </a>
          </div>

          <ul className="text-[11px] text-slate-300 space-y-1.5 list-disc list-inside leading-relaxed">
            <li>تأكد من تسليط إضاءة كافية على البطاقة وعدم وجود انعكاسات قوية.</li>
            <li>إذا كنت تستخدم كاميرا الهاتف العادية، تأكد من وضوح التركيز (Focus) على رمز الـ QR.</li>
            <li>يمكنك دائماً كتابة الكود المطبوع على البطاقة في الخانة أعلاه للوصول المباشر.</li>
            <li>إذا تم مسح أو إلغاء تفعيل البطاقة، اضغط على زر تواصل مع المطور ليتم حل المشكلة فوراً.</li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Discrete Footer with Subtle Admin Login */}
      <footer className="border-t border-white/5 bg-black/60 py-6 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 text-xs text-slate-500">
          <p>© {new Date().getFullYear()} Smart Card — جميع الحقوق محفوظة</p>
          
          <button
            onClick={onGoToAdmin}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-300 transition text-[11px] py-1 px-2.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/5"
            title="تسجيل دخول الإدارة"
          >
            <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
            <span>دخول الإدارة</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </footer>

      {/* Live Camera Scanner Modal */}
      <QrScannerModal
        isOpen={isScannerOpen}
        mode="customer"
        onClose={() => setIsScannerOpen(false)}
        onDeclined={() => setCameraDeclinedOnce(true)}
        onUploadFallback={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
        onScannedId={(id) => {
          setCameraDeclinedOnce(false);
          setIsScannerOpen(false);
          window.location.href = `/q/${id}`;
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Eye, EyeOff, ShieldCheck, ArrowRight, AlertCircle, Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import { loginAdmin } from '../lib/auth';

interface AdminLoginViewProps {
  onLoginSuccess: () => void;
  onNavigateHome: () => void;
}

export function AdminLoginView({ onLoginSuccess, onNavigateHome }: AdminLoginViewProps) {
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!password.trim()) return;

    setErrorMsg(null);
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const ok = await loginAdmin(password.trim());
      if (ok) {
        onLoginSuccess();
      } else {
        setErrorMsg('كلمة المرور غير صحيحة. يرجى التأكد وإعادة المحاولة.');
      }
    } catch {
      setErrorMsg('حدث خطأ أثناء التحقق من كلمة المرور.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-white selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-black flex flex-col justify-center items-center p-4 relative overflow-hidden">
      {/* Background ambient gold glows */}
      <div className="absolute top-1/4 -left-20 w-80 h-80 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
      <div className="absolute bottom-1/4 -right-20 w-80 h-80 bg-yellow-500/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

      {/* Back to Home Button */}
      <button
        onClick={onNavigateHome}
        className="absolute top-6 right-6 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-300/80 hover:text-white bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl backdrop-blur-sm border border-amber-500/20 transition cursor-pointer"
      >
        <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
        العودة للرئيسية
      </button>

      {/* Login Card */}
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-8 sm:p-10 shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.15)] relative z-10 backdrop-blur-2xl stage-3d animate-3d-enter">
        <div className="text-center space-y-3 mb-8">
          <img 
            src="/logo.png" 
            alt="Smart Card" 
            className="w-16 h-16 object-contain rounded-2xl mx-auto shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/25 border border-amber-400/40 p-1 bg-[#121216] animate-3d-float" 
          />
          <div>
            <span className="inline-block text-[11px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-300 bg-amber-400/15 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-amber-400/30 mb-2">
              لوحة التحكم
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500 font-mono">
              Smart Card
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">
              تسجيل دخول الأدمن — إدارة الرموز والتوجيه الذكي
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {errorMsg && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-3.5 bg-rose-500/20 border border-rose-500/40 rounded-2xl text-xs text-rose-300 flex items-start gap-2.5 animate-in fade-in">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <span className="leading-relaxed font-semibold">{errorMsg}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-200/90">
              كلمة المرور السرية للأدمن:
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <input
                type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="أدخل كلمة المرور..."
                className="w-full px-4 py-3.5 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/25 rounded-2xl text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400 transition"
                dir="ltr"
                required
                autoFocus
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-300 transition cursor-pointer"
                title={showPassword ? 'إخفاء' : 'إظهار'}
              >
                {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading || !password.trim()}
            className="w-full py-3.5 px-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 disabled:opacity-50 text-black font-black text-sm rounded-2xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer active:scale-98"
          >
            {loading ? (
              <span>جاري التحقق...</span>
            ) : (
              <>
                <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-black" />
                <span>دخول لوحة التحكم</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </form>

        <div className="mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-white/5 text-center flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-slate-500">
          <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400/60" />
          <span>آمنة ومحمية</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {

  RefreshCw, 
  Smartphone,

  Activity, 
  BarChart3,

  Clock, 
  Sparkles,
  KeyRound,
  Grid,
  List,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  Trash2,
  AlertTriangle,
  AlertCircle,
  MessageSquare,
  Phone,
  Settings,
  Globe,
  Edit3,
  Calendar,
  FileText,
  Code2,
  PackageCheck,
  RotateCcw
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  fetchAllQrCodes,
  generateQrDataUrl,
  deleteQrCode,
  deactivateQrCode,
  getQrCode,
  lookupExistingQrCode,
  fetchPlatformSettings,
  updatePlatformSettings,

  batchUpdateQrPrintedStatus

import { detectWalletProviderFromUrl, getWalletProviderMeta, isQuickPayUrl } from '../lib/deepLinks';

  onLogout: () => void;

  onSimulateScan: (id: string) => void;
}

function formatDisplayTarget(url: string | null | undefined, appType?: string, wallets?: any[]): string {
  if (!url) return '— (غير معين بعد)';
  if (appType === 'wallet') {
    if (wallets && wallets.length > 1) {
      const phones = wallets.map((w: any) => w.phone).filter(Boolean).join(' • ');
      return `${wallets.length} محافظ كاش (${phones})`;
    }
    if (wallets && wallets.length === 1) {
      const w = wallets[0];
      const detectedUrlProv = isQuickPayUrl(w.phone || url) ? detectWalletProviderFromUrl(w.phone || url) : null;
      const prov = detectedUrlProv && detectedUrlProv !== 'generic' ? detectedUrlProv : w.provider;
      const provLabel = prov && prov !== 'generic' ? getWalletProviderMeta(prov).nameAr : 'محفظة كاش';
      if (w?.ownerName) {
        return `${provLabel}: ${w.phone} (${w.ownerName})`;
      }
      return `${provLabel}: ${w.phone}`;
    }
    if (isQuickPayUrl(url)) {
      const prov = detectWalletProviderFromUrl(url);
      const provLabel = prov !== 'generic' ? getWalletProviderMeta(prov).nameAr : 'رابط دفع سريع';
      return `${provLabel}: ${url}`;
    }
    if (url.startsWith('tel:*9*7*')) {
      const num = url.replace('tel:*9*7*', '').replace('#', '').replace('%23', '');
      return num ? `محفظة: ${num}` : 'محفظة كاش';
    }
    if (!url.startsWith('http://') && !url.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `محفظة: ${url}`;
    }
  }
  if (url.startsWith('bank://')) {
    return 'بيانات حساب بنكي';
  }
  return url;
}

export function AdminDashboardView({ onLogout, domainUrl, onSimulateScan }: AdminDashboardViewProps) {
  const [records, setRecords] = useState<QrCodeRecord[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState<'all' | 'active' | 'inactive' | 'expired'>('all');
  const [printFilter, setPrintFilter] = useState<'all' | 'unprinted' | 'printed'>('all');
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'grid' | 'table'>('grid');
  const [renewingId, setRenewingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [togglingPrintId, setTogglingPrintId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isBatchMarkingPrint, setIsBatchMarkingPrint] = useState(false);

  // Modals state
  const [isBulkOpen, setIsBulkOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isUploadOpen, setIsUploadOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isScannerOpen, setIsScannerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isPrintOpen, setIsPrintOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isPasswordModalOpen, setIsPasswordModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState('');
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');
  const [passwordError, setPasswordError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [passwordSaving, setPasswordSaving] = useState(false);
  const [passwordSuccess, setPasswordSuccess] = useState(false);

  // Developer Settings Modal State
  const [isSettingsModalOpen, setIsSettingsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [platformSettings, setPlatformSettings] = useState<PlatformSettings>({
    developer_whatsapp: '01550523365',
    developer_contact_url: 'https://wa.me/201550523365',
    developer_title: 'تواصل مع المطور',
    support_message: 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية',
  });

  // Edit modal
  const [selectedEditRecord, setSelectedEditRecord] = useState<QrCodeRecord | null>(null);

  // Delete modal state
  const [codeToDelete, setCodeToDelete] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false);

  // Deactivate modal state
  const [codeToDeactivate, setCodeToDeactivate] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isDeactivating, setIsDeactivating] = useState(false);

  // QR Thumbnails cache
  const [qrThumbs, setQrThumbs] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [copiedId, setCopiedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [adminNotice, setAdminNotice] = useState<{ type: 'error' | 'info' | 'success'; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [isCopyingFullCode, setIsCopyingFullCode] = useState(false);
  const [fullCodeCopied, setFullCodeCopied] = useState(false);

  const handleCopyFullPlatformCode = async () => {
    setIsCopyingFullCode(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/SMART_CARD_FULL_CODE.txt');
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch source file');
      const text = await res.text();
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setFullCodeCopied(true);
      setAdminNotice({
        type: 'success',
        text: 'تم نسخ كود المنصة بالكامل (ملف واحد) إلى الحافظة بنجاح!',
      });
      setTimeout(() => setFullCodeCopied(false), 3000);
    } catch {
      setAdminNotice({
        type: 'info',
        text: 'يمكنك فتح أو تحميل الملف الموحد مباشرة من /SMART_CARD_FULL_CODE.txt',
      });
    } finally {
      setIsCopyingFullCode(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDownloadAdminApk = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/downloads/SmartCard-Admin.apk', {
        method: 'HEAD',
        cache: 'no-store',
      });
      const contentType = (res.headers.get('content-type') || '').toLowerCase();
      if (res.ok && contentType.includes('android.package-archive')) {
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = '/downloads/SmartCard-Admin.apk';
        a.download = 'SmartCard-Admin.apk';
        document.body.appendChild(a);
        a.click();
        document.body.removeChild(a);
        return;
      }
    } catch {
      // Fallback notice below
    }
    setAdminNotice({
      type: 'info',
      text: 'كود تطبيق الأدمن بلغة Dart/Flutter جاهز بالكامل داخل مجلد smart_card_admin_dart/. لبناء ملف SmartCard-Admin.apk القابل للتثبيت نفّذ ./build_apk.sh من مستودع GitHub في بيئة تطوير بذاكرة 8GB+ ليتم حفظه في public/downloads/SmartCard-Admin.apk.',
    });
  };

  const handleConfirmDelete = async () => {
    if (!codeToDelete) return;
    setIsDeleting(true);
    try {
      await deleteQrCode(codeToDelete);
      setRecords((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r.id !== codeToDelete));
      if (selectedEditRecord && selectedEditRecord.id === codeToDelete) {
        setSelectedEditRecord(null);
      }
      setCodeToDelete(null);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Delete failed:', err);
    } finally {
      setIsDeleting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmDeactivate = async () => {
    if (!codeToDeactivate || isDeactivating) return;
    setIsDeactivating(true);
    const targetId = codeToDeactivate;
    try {
      const res = await deactivateQrCode(targetId);
      if (res.success && res.record) {
        setRecords((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r.id === targetId ? res.record! : r)));
        if (selectedEditRecord && selectedEditRecord.id === targetId) {
          setSelectedEditRecord(null);
        }
        setCodeToDeactivate(null);
        setAdminNotice({
          type: 'success',
          text: `تم إلغاء تفعيل الكود [${targetId}] ومسح بياناته بالكامل؛ عاد الآن إلى حالة (في انتظار التفعيل) وجاهزاً للتفعيل من جديد ✓`,
        });
      } else {
        setAdminNotice({
          type: 'error',
          text: res.error || `تعذر إلغاء تفعيل الكود [${targetId}]. يرجى المحاولة مرة أخرى.`,
        });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Deactivate failed:', err);
      setAdminNotice({
        type: 'error',
        text: `حدث خطأ أثناء إلغاء تفعيل الكود [${targetId}].`,
      });
    } finally {
      setIsDeactivating(false);
    }
  };

  const handleQuickRenew = async (id: string, months = 1) => {
    setRenewingId(id);
    try {
      const res = await renewQrSubscription(id, months);
      if (res.success && res.record) {
        setRecords((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r.id === id ? res.record! : r)));
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Quick renew failed:', err);
    } finally {
      setRenewingId(null);
    }
  };

  const handlePrintStatusChange = async (record: QrCodeRecord, nextPrinted: boolean) => {
    const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
    const prevPrinted = Boolean(record.is_printed);
    const prevPrintedAt = record.printed_at ?? null;
    if (prevPrinted === nextPrinted) return;

    setTogglingPrintId(record.id);
    // Optimistic state update
    setRecords((prev) =>
      prev.map((r) =>
        r.id === record.id
          ? { ...r, is_printed: nextPrinted, printed_at: nextPrinted ? nowIso : null, updated_at: nowIso }
          : r
      )
    );

    try {
      const res = await toggleQrPrintedStatus(record.id, nextPrinted);
      if (res.success && res.record) {
        setRecords((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r.id === record.id ? res.record! : r)));
        setAdminNotice({
          type: 'success',
          text: nextPrinted
            ? `تم نقل الكود [${record.id}] إلى خانة الكروت المطبوعة ✓`
            : `تمت إعادة الكود [${record.id}] إلى قائمة غير المطبوعة`,
        });
      } else {
        throw new Error('Failed to update print status');
      }
    } catch {
      setRecords((prev) =>
        prev.map((r) =>
          r.id === record.id ? { ...r, is_printed: prevPrinted, printed_at: prevPrintedAt } : r
        )
      );
      setAdminNotice({
        type: 'error',
        text: `تعذر تحديث حالة الطباعة للكود [${record.id}]. يرجى المحاولة مرة أخرى.`,
      });
    } finally {
      setTogglingPrintId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleBatchMarkVisiblePrinted = async (ids: string[]) => {
    if (ids.length === 0) return;
    setIsBatchMarkingPrint(true);
    const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
    const idSet = new Set(ids);
    setRecords((prev) =>
      prev.map((r) =>
        idSet.has(r.id) ? { ...r, is_printed: true, printed_at: nowIso, updated_at: nowIso } : r
      )
    );
    try {
      await batchUpdateQrPrintedStatus(ids, true);
      setAdminNotice({
        type: 'success',
        text: `تم نقل ${ids.length} كود إلى خانة الكروت المطبوعة ✓`,
      });
    } finally {
      setIsBatchMarkingPrint(false);
    }
  };

  const loadData = async (silent = false) => {
    if (!silent) setLoading(true);
    try {
      const data = await fetchAllQrCodes();
      const safeData = Array.isArray(data)
        ? data.filter((item): item is QrCodeRecord => Boolean(item && item.id && item.id !== 'undefined' && item.id !== 'null'))
        : [];
      setRecords(safeData);

      // Pre-generate thumbnails for first batch
      if (!silent || Object.keys(qrThumbs).length === 0) {
        const thumbs: Record<string, string> = {};
        for (const item of safeData.slice(0, 30)) {
          if (item && item.id) {
            thumbs[item.id] = await generateQrDataUrl(`${domainUrl}/q/${item.id}`, 200);
          }
        }
        setQrThumbs(thumbs);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load QR codes:', err);
    } finally {
      if (!silent) setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData(false);
    fetchPlatformSettings().then((s) => {
      if (s) setPlatformSettings(s);
    });

    // Real-time Live Firestore onSnapshot: instant updates without refresh!
    const unsubscribe = subscribeToQrCodes((liveRecords) => {
      const safe = Array.isArray(liveRecords)
        ? liveRecords.filter((r): r is QrCodeRecord => Boolean(r && r.id && r.id !== 'undefined' && r.id !== 'null'))
        : [];
      setRecords(safe);
      setLoading(false);
    });

    // Also periodic 4-second silent background refresh to guarantee live numbers
    const pollInterval = setInterval(() => {
      loadData(true);
    }, 4000);

    return () => {
      unsubscribe();
      clearInterval(pollInterval);
    };
  }, [domainUrl]);

  const handleCopyLink = (id: string) => {
    const fullUrl = `${domainUrl}/q/${id}`;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(fullUrl);
    setCopiedId(id);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedId(null), 2000);
  };

  const handleDownloadQr = async (record: QrCodeRecord) => {
    const fullUrl = `${domainUrl}/q/${record.id}`;
    const dataUrl = await generateQrDataUrl(fullUrl, 800);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = dataUrl;
    link.download = `QR_${record.id}.png`;
    link.click();
  };

  const handleUpdatePassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setPasswordError(null);
    const cleanCurrent = currentPassword.trim();
    const cleanNew = newPassword.trim();

    if (!cleanCurrent) {
      setPasswordError('الرجاء إدخال كلمة المرور الحالية.');
      return;
    }

    if (cleanNew.length < 4) {
      setPasswordError('كلمة المرور الجديدة يجب أن لا تقل عن 4 خانات.');
      return;
    }

    if (cleanNew !== confirmPassword.trim()) {
      setPasswordError('كلمتا المرور الجديدتان غير متطابقتين.');
      return;
    }

    setPasswordSaving(true);
    try {
      const res = await changeAdminPassword(cleanCurrent, cleanNew);
      if (res.success) {
        setPasswordSuccess(true);
        setTimeout(() => {
          setPasswordSuccess(false);
          setIsPasswordModalOpen(false);
          setCurrentPassword('');
          setNewPassword('');
          setConfirmPassword('');
        }, 1600);
      } else {
        setPasswordError(res.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setPasswordError('حدث خطأ أثناء حفظ كلمة المرور.');
    } finally {
      setPasswordSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveDeveloperSettings = async (updated: PlatformSettings) => {
    const res = await updatePlatformSettings(updated);
    setPlatformSettings(res);
  };

  // Filtered records with bulletproof defensive guards
  const cleanSearch = (searchTerm || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const filtered = records.filter((r) => {
    if (!r || typeof r !== 'object') return false;
    const id = String(r.id || '').trim();
    if (!id || id === 'undefined' || id === 'null') return false;

    if (cleanSearch) {
      const idStr = id.toLowerCase();
      const targetStr = String(r.target_url || '').toLowerCase();
      const labelStr = String(r.label || '').toLowerCase();

      const matchesSearch =
        idStr.includes(cleanSearch) ||
        targetStr.includes(cleanSearch) ||
        labelStr.includes(cleanSearch);

      if (!matchesSearch) return false;
    }

    if (filterStatus === 'active') {
      if (!(r.is_active && (!r.expires_at || new Date(r.expires_at).getTime() >= Date.now()))) return false;
    } else if (filterStatus === 'expired') {
      if (!(r.is_active && r.expires_at && new Date(r.expires_at).getTime() < Date.now())) return false;
    } else if (filterStatus === 'inactive') {
      if (r.is_active) return false;
    }

    if (printFilter === 'printed') {
      if (!r.is_printed) return false;
    } else if (printFilter === 'unprinted') {
      if (r.is_printed) return false;
    }

    return true;
