  });

  // KPI Calculations
  const validRecords = records.filter((r): r is QrCodeRecord => Boolean(r && r.id && r.id !== 'undefined' && r.id !== 'null'));
  const totalCodes = validRecords.length;
  const expiredCodes = validRecords.filter((r) => r.is_active && r.expires_at && new Date(r.expires_at).getTime() < Date.now()).length;
  const activeCodes = validRecords.filter((r) => r.is_active && (!r.expires_at || new Date(r.expires_at).getTime() >= Date.now())).length;
  const pendingCodes = validRecords.filter((r) => !r.is_active).length;
  const printedCodes = validRecords.filter((r) => Boolean(r.is_printed)).length;
  const unprintedCodes = validRecords.filter((r) => !r.is_printed).length;
  const totalScans = validRecords.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (Number(curr?.total_scans) || 0), 0);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-slate-100 pb-16 selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-black">
      {/* Top Navbar */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-[#0e0e12]/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-amber-500/20 shadow-lg">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-3.5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <img 
              src="/logo.png" 
              alt="Smart Card Logo" 
              className="w-10 h-10 object-contain rounded-2xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 flex-shrink-0 p-0.5 bg-[#121216] border border-amber-400/40 animate-3d-float" 
            />
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                <h1 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500 font-mono">
                  Smart Card Admin
                </h1>
                <span className="text-[10px] bg-amber-400/15 text-amber-300 font-black px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-amber-400/30">
                  الأدمن الملكي
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-200/70 hidden sm:block">إدارة المعرّفات الفريدة والتوجيه الذكي المباشر</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Top Actions */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
            {/* Developer Contact & WhatsApp Settings Button */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsSettingsModalOpen(true)}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400/10 via-yellow-400/15 to-amber-500/10 hover:bg-amber-400/25 text-amber-300 hover:text-amber-200 border border-amber-400/40 rounded-xl transition text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(212,175,55,0.15)] cursor-pointer"
              title="إعدادات التواصل مع المطور والواتساب"
            >
              <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span className="text-[11px]">تواصل مع المطور (الواتساب)</span>
            </button>

            {/* Copy Full Platform Code (Single File) */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleCopyFullPlatformCode}
              disabled={isCopyingFullCode}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-cyan-300 hover:text-black hover:bg-cyan-400 bg-cyan-400/10 rounded-xl transition text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-cyan-400/30 cursor-pointer"
              title="نسخ كود المنصة بالكامل في ملف واحد بضغطة واحدة"
            >
              {fullCodeCopied ? <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
              <span className="text-[11px]">
                {fullCodeCopied ? 'تم نسخ الكود!' : 'نسخ كود المنصة'}
              </span>
            </button>

            {/* Download Full Source Code Single Text File */}
            <a
              href="/SMART_CARD_FULL_CODE.txt"
              download="SMART_CARD_FULL_CODE.txt"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-emerald-300 hover:text-black hover:bg-emerald-400 bg-emerald-400/10 rounded-xl transition text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-emerald-400/30"
              title="فتح / تحميل كود المنصة بالكامل في ملف نصي واحد سهل النسخ"
            >
              <Code2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span className="text-[11px] hidden sm:inline">ملف الكود (.txt)</span>
            </a>

            {/* Download Documentation PDF */}
            <a
              href="/Smart_Card_Documentation_and_Updates.pdf"
              download="Smart_Card_Documentation_and_Updates.pdf"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-amber-300 hover:text-black hover:bg-amber-400 bg-amber-400/10 rounded-xl transition text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-amber-400/30"
              title="تحميل ملف التوثيق والتحديثات (PDF)"
            >
              <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span className="text-[11px] hidden sm:inline">تقرير التوثيق (PDF)</span>
            </a>

            {/* Download Android Admin APK (Admin-Only) */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleDownloadAdminApk}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-yellow-200 hover:text-black hover:bg-amber-400 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500/20 to-yellow-500/20 rounded-xl transition text-xs font-black flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-amber-400/50 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(251,191,36,0.18)]"
              title="تحميل تطبيق الأدمن للأندرويد (APK) المخصص للإدارة فقط"
            >
              <Smartphone className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span className="text-[11px]">تطبيق الأدمن (.apk)</span>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={() => setIsPasswordModalOpen(true)}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-slate-300 hover:text-white bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 rounded-xl transition text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-white/10"
              title="تغيير كلمة المرور"
            >
              <KeyRound className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span className="text-[11px]">كلمة المرور</span>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={onLogout}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-rose-500/15 hover:bg-rose-500/25 text-rose-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition border border-rose-500/30"
            >
              <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span className="text-[11px]">خروج</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Body */}
      <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 pt-8 space-y-8">
        {adminNotice && (
          <div
            className={`p-3.5 rounded-2xl border text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-between gap-3 animate-in fade-in ${
              adminNotice.type === 'error'
                ? 'bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-500/35 text-rose-200'
                : adminNotice.type === 'success'
                ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-500/35 text-emerald-200'
                : 'bg-amber-400/15 border-amber-400/35 text-amber-200'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{adminNotice.text}</span>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setAdminNotice(null)}
              className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-[11px] cursor-pointer"
            >
              إغلاق
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Localhost / Online Domain Guidance Banner */}
        {(domainUrl.includes('localhost') || domainUrl.includes('127.0.0.1')) && !platformSettings.public_domain_url && (
          <div className="p-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500/15 via-yellow-500/10 to-amber-500/15 border border-amber-500/35 rounded-2xl flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 text-right shadow-lg">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/20 border border-amber-400/40 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-400 flex-shrink-0">
                <Globe className="w-5 h-5" />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-200 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span>تنبيه مهم لمسح وتفعيل الرموز من هواتف العملاء:</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] bg-amber-400/20 text-amber-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-amber-400/30">بيئة محلية (Localhost)</span>
                </p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                  أنت تعمل حالياً على السيرفر المحلي. إذا قمت بعمل سكان للـ QR من هاتف خارجي، لن يفتح الهاتف لأن هاتف العميل لا يستطيع الاتصال بـ localhost بجهازك. لحل ذلك: اكتب رابط الدومين الأونلاين لموقعك في الإعدادات، أو ارفع الموقع مباشرة على الاستضافة (Render / Railway / VPS).
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsSettingsModalOpen(true)}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black font-black text-xs rounded-xl flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-[0_4px_15px_rgba(234,179,8,0.25)] flex-shrink-0 transition active:scale-95"
            >
              <Settings className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-black" />
              <span>ضبط رابط الموقع الأونلاين</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* KPI Stats Cards */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-3.5 stage-3d">
          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/20 hover:border-amber-400/45 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between card-3d-tilt">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">إجمالي الرموز</p>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-amber-400 mt-0.5 tabular-nums">{totalCodes}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">معرّفات nanoid فريدة</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/20 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <QrIcon className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/20 hover:border-emerald-400/45 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between card-3d-tilt">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">الاشتراكات النشطة</p>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-emerald-400 mt-0.5 tabular-nums">{activeCodes}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">سارية وتعمل الآن</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-emerald-400/10 border border-emerald-400/20 text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] rounded-2xl border border-rose-500/20 hover:border-rose-400/45 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between card-3d-tilt">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">اشتراكات منتهية</p>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-rose-400 mt-0.5 tabular-nums">{expiredCodes}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">تتطلب التجديد</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/25 text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Clock className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/20 hover:border-amber-400/45 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between card-3d-tilt">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">بانتظار التفعيل</p>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-yellow-400 mt-0.5 tabular-nums">{pendingCodes}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">كروت جديدة غير معينة</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-yellow-400/10 border border-yellow-400/20 text-yellow-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Clock className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setPrintFilter((prev) => (prev === 'printed' ? 'all' : 'printed'))}
            className={`p-4 bg-[#121216] rounded-2xl border shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between card-3d-tilt text-right transition cursor-pointer ${
              printFilter === 'printed'
                ? 'border-cyan-400/70 ring-1 ring-cyan-400/30'
                : 'border-cyan-500/25 hover:border-cyan-400/50'
            }`}
            title="اضغط لعرض الكروت المطبوعة فقط"
          >
            <div>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">الكروت المطبوعة</p>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-cyan-400 mt-0.5 tabular-nums">{printedCodes}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 tabular-nums">متبقي {unprintedCodes} للطباعة</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-cyan-400/10 border border-cyan-400/25 text-cyan-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <PackageCheck className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
          </button>

          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/20 hover:border-amber-400/45 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between card-3d-tilt">
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">إجمالي المسح</p>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/30 text-[9px] text-emerald-400 font-bold">
                  <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
                  مباشر
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-amber-300 mt-0.5 tabular-nums">{totalScans}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">توجيه فوري لحظي</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/20 text-amber-300 flex items-center justify-center">
              <BarChart3 className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Primary Action Ribbon */}
        <div className="p-6 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#121216] via-[#191920] to-[#121216] rounded-3xl text-white shadow-xl shadow-amber-500/5 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-6 border border-amber-500/30 animate-3d-enter">
          <div className="space-y-1.5 text-center md:text-right">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-full text-xs font-bold text-amber-300">
              <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              أمان ومطابقة: التعديل متاح فقط بعد مسح الرمز أو رفع صورته
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
              أدوات الإدارة والمسح والتعديل الآمن
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 max-w-xl">
              تم حجب أزرار التعديل اليدوي العشوائي؛ لتعديل أي كود يجب التحقق منه عبر مسحه بكاميرا الأدمن أو رفع صورته لضمان مطابقة البطاقة الفعلية.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2.5 w-full md:w-auto">
            {/* Live Camera Scanner to Edit */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsScannerOpen(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-700 hover:from-emerald-500 hover:to-teal-600 text-white text-xs font-extrabold rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-emerald-600/20 transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Camera className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
              مسح بالكاميرا للتعديل
            </button>

            {/* Upload QR Image to Edit */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsUploadOpen(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 bg-[#1e1e26] hover:bg-[#252530] text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 text-xs font-extrabold rounded-xl shadow-lg transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Upload className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
              رفع صورة كود للتعديل
            </button>

            {/* Bulk Generate */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsBulkOpen(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
              توليد أكواد مجمعة
            </button>

            {/* Print Sheet A4 */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsPrintOpen(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-slate-200 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Printer className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
              طباعة كروت A4
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Search, Filters, and Table */}
        <div className="bg-[#121216] rounded-3xl border border-amber-500/20 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
          {/* Controls Header */}
          <div className="p-5 border-b border-white/5 space-y-3.5">
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
              {/* Search Input */}
              <div className="relative w-full md:w-80">
                <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 absolute right-3.5 top-3" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={searchTerm}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="ابحث بالمعرّف ID أو الرابط المستهدف... (مثل I9pFBZwn8Y)"
                  className="w-full pr-10 pl-4 py-2.5 bg-black/70 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl text-xs font-mono font-black text-amber-300 placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-400/20 transition selection:bg-amber-400 selection:text-black"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Filters and View Switcher */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 w-full md:w-auto justify-between md:justify-end">
                {/* Category Tabs: Active, Pending, All */}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-black/40 p-1 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 border border-white/5 overflow-x-auto max-w-full">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setFilterStatus('active')}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl transition whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                      filterStatus === 'active'
                        ? 'bg-amber-400 text-black shadow-md font-black'
                        : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>النشطة</span>
                    <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold tabular-nums ${
                      filterStatus === 'active' ? 'bg-black/20 text-black' : 'bg-amber-400/20 text-amber-300'
                    }`}>
                      {activeCodes}
                    </span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setFilterStatus('expired')}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl transition whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                      filterStatus === 'expired'
                        ? 'bg-rose-500 text-white shadow-md font-black'
                        : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>منتهية الصلاحية</span>
                    <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold tabular-nums ${
                      filterStatus === 'expired' ? 'bg-black/30 text-white' : 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300'
                    }`}>
                      {expiredCodes}
                    </span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setFilterStatus('inactive')}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl transition whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                      filterStatus === 'inactive'
                        ? 'bg-yellow-500 text-black shadow-md font-black'
                        : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>في انتظار التفعيل</span>
                    <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold tabular-nums ${
                      filterStatus === 'inactive' ? 'bg-black/20 text-black' : 'bg-yellow-400/20 text-yellow-300'
                    }`}>
                      {pendingCodes}
                    </span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setFilterStatus('all')}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl transition whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                      filterStatus === 'all'
                        ? 'bg-white/20 text-white shadow-md font-black'
                        : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span>الكل</span>
                    <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold tabular-nums ${
                      filterStatus === 'all' ? 'bg-black/20 text-white' : 'bg-white/10 text-slate-300'
                    }`}>
                      {records.length}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* View Switcher */}
                <div className="flex items-center border border-white/10 rounded-xl overflow-hidden p-0.5 bg-black/40 shrink-0">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setViewMode('grid')}
                    className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg transition ${
                      viewMode === 'grid' ? 'bg-amber-400 text-black shadow-sm font-bold' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-white'
                    }`}
                    title="عرض شبكي"
                  >
                    <Grid className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setViewMode('table')}
                    className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg transition ${
                      viewMode === 'table' ? 'bg-amber-400 text-black shadow-sm font-bold' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-white'
                    }`}
                    title="عرض جدولي"
                  >
                    <List className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* Refresh Button */}
                <button
                  onClick={() => loadData(false)}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 rounded-xl transition border border-white/5 shrink-0"
                  title="تحديث البيانات"
                >
                  <RefreshCw className={`w-4 h-4 ${loading ? 'animate-spin text-amber-400' : ''}`} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Admin-Only Print Tracking Filter Row */}
            <div className="pt-3 border-t border-white/5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-cyan-300 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
                  <Printer className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  حالة الطباعة (للأدمن فقط):
                </span>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-black/40 p-1 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold border border-white/5 overflow-x-auto">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setPrintFilter('all')}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl transition whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                      printFilter === 'all'
                        ? 'bg-white/20 text-white shadow-sm font-black'
                        : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span>الكل</span>
                    <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] bg-white/10 text-slate-200 tabular-nums">
                      {totalCodes}
                    </span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setPrintFilter('unprinted')}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl transition whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                      printFilter === 'unprinted'
                        ? 'bg-amber-400 text-black shadow-sm font-black'
                        : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>لم تُطبع بعد</span>
                    <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold tabular-nums ${
                      printFilter === 'unprinted' ? 'bg-black/20 text-black' : 'bg-amber-400/15 text-amber-300'
                    }`}>
                      {unprintedCodes}
                    </span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setPrintFilter('printed')}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl transition whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                      printFilter === 'printed'
                        ? 'bg-cyan-400 text-black shadow-sm font-black'
                        : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <PackageCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>الكروت المطبوعة ✓</span>
                    <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold tabular-nums ${
                      printFilter === 'printed' ? 'bg-black/20 text-black' : 'bg-cyan-400/15 text-cyan-300'
                    }`}>
                      {printedCodes}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {printFilter === 'unprinted' && filtered.length > 0 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={isBatchMarkingPrint}
                  onClick={() => handleBatchMarkVisiblePrinted(filtered.map((r) => r.id))}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-cyan-400/15 hover:bg-cyan-400 hover:text-black text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-400/35 text-[11px] font-black transition whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <PackageCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>{isBatchMarkingPrint ? 'جاري النقل...' : `نقل المعروض (${filtered.length}) إلى الكروت المطبوعة ✓`}</span>
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* List Content */}
          {loading && records.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="py-24 text-center space-y-3">
              <RefreshCw className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-400 animate-spin mx-auto" />
              <p className="text-xs text-amber-200/80 font-bold">جاري تحميل بيانات الأكواد الملكية...</p>
            </div>
          ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="py-20 text-center space-y-3">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-white/5 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto border border-amber-500/20">
                <QrIcon className="w-8 h-8" />
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
                {cleanSearch ? `لم يتم العثور على نتائج محلية مطابقة لـ "${cleanSearch}"` : 'لم يتم العثور على أي رموز'}
              </p>
              {cleanSearch ? (
                <div className="space-y-2 pt-1">
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">قد يكون الكود مسجلاً بالسيرفر أو يحتاج جلباً مباشراً:</p>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={async () => {
                      setLoading(true);
                      const found = await lookupExistingQrCode(cleanSearch);
                      setLoading(false);
                      if (found) {
                        setSelectedEditRecord(found);
                        setRecords((prev) => [found, ...prev.filter((r) => r.id !== found.id)]);
                      } else {
                        setAdminNotice({
                          type: 'error',
                          text: `الكود "${cleanSearch}" غير مسجل بالسيرفر.`,
                        });
                      }
                    }}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl transition shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
                    جلب وفحص الكود [{cleanSearch}] من السيرفر
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">اضغط على زر "توليد أكواد مجمعة" لإنشاء أول مجموعة من الرموز الذكية</p>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setIsBulkOpen(true)}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl transition mt-2 shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20"
                  >
                    <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                    توليد 10 أكواد الآن
                  </button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : viewMode === 'grid' ? (
            /* Grid View */
            <div className="p-6 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-4 stage-3d">
              {filtered.map((record) => {
                const scanUrl = `${domainUrl}/q/${record.id}`;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={record.id}
                    className="p-4 bg-[#16161c] rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/20 hover:border-amber-400/55 hover:shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(212,175,55,0.16)] flex flex-col justify-between group card-3d-tilt"
                  >
                    <div>
                      {/* Top Header of card */}
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-white/5">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          {record.is_active && record.app_type && (
                            <div className="animate-icon-alive">
                              <PlatformBrandIcon
                                type={record.app_type as any}
                                className="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0"
                                targetUrl={record.target_url || undefined}
                                walletProvider={record.wallets?.[0]?.provider}
                              />
                            </div>
                          )}
                          <span className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-extrabold flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                            record.is_active
                              ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30'
                              : 'bg-yellow-500/15 text-yellow-300 border border-yellow-500/30'
                          }`}>
                            <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${record.is_active ? 'bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse' : 'bg-yellow-400'}`} />
                            {record.is_active ? 'مفعل نشط' : 'في انتظار التفعيل'}
                          </span>
                        </div>

                        {/* Prominent Eye Badge for admin */}
                        <div 
                          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-full text-amber-300 font-extrabold text-xs shadow-inner"
                          title={`تم فتح ومسح هذا الرابط ${record.total_scans || 0} مرة بواسطة العملاء`}
                        >
                          <Eye className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                          <span className="tabular-nums">{record.total_scans || 0} مسحة</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* QR Thumbnail */}
                      <div className="my-3 flex justify-center stage-3d">
                        <div className="p-2.5 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-inner qr-thumb-3d">
                          {qrThumbs[record.id] ? (
                            <img
                              src={qrThumbs[record.id]}
                              alt={`QR ${record.id}`}
                              className="w-28 h-28 object-contain"
                            />
                          ) : (
                            <div className="w-28 h-28 bg-slate-200 rounded animate-pulse" />
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* ID and info */}
                      <div className="space-y-1.5 text-center">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
                          <span className="font-mono text-xs font-black text-amber-300 bg-black/60 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg tracking-wider border border-amber-500/30" dir="ltr">
                            {record.id}
                          </span>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleCopyLink(record.id)}
                            className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-300 transition"
                            title="نسخ رابط المسح"
                          >
                            {copiedId === record.id ? (
                              <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                            ) : (
                              <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            )}
                          </button>
                        </div>

                        {record.label && (
                          <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-200/90 truncate">
                            {record.label}
                          </p>
                        )}

                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate" dir="ltr">
                          {formatDisplayTarget(record.target_url, record.app_type, record.wallets)}
                        </p>

                        {/* Prominent Eye details banner */}
                        <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-white/5 flex items-center justify-between gap-1.5 text-xs text-amber-300 font-extrabold bg-amber-400/10 py-1.5 px-2.5 rounded-xl border border-amber-400/20">
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                            <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                            <span>
                              {record.is_active 
                                ? `تم المسح: ${record.total_scans || 0} مرة`
                                : 'في انتظار التفعيل'}
                            </span>
                          </span>
                        </div>

                        {/* Subscription Expiry & Duration Management Box */}
                        <div className="mt-2.5 p-3 bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/25 rounded-xl space-y-2 text-right">
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px]">
                            <span className="font-bold text-amber-200/90 flex items-center gap-1">
                              <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                              <span>صلاحية الكارت:</span>
                            </span>
                            {record.expires_at ? (
                              new Date(record.expires_at).getTime() < Date.now() ? (
                                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/35 animate-pulse">
                                  منتهي الصلاحية ⚠️
                                </span>
                              ) : (
                                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30">
                                  باقي {Math.max(0, Math.ceil((new Date(record.expires_at).getTime() - Date.now()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)))} يوم
                                </span>
                              )
                            ) : (
                              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/30">
                                دائم / غير محدود ✦
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </div>

                          {record.expires_at && (
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400 pt-0.5">
                              <span>تاريخ الانتهاء:</span>
                              <span className="font-mono text-amber-300 font-bold" dir="ltr">
                                {new Date(record.expires_at).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', {
                                  year: 'numeric',
                                  month: 'short',
                                  day: 'numeric',
                                })}
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          )}

                          {/* Quick 1-Click Renewal Buttons */}
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-1">
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={renewingId === record.id}
                              onClick={() => handleQuickRenew(record.id, 1)}
                              className="flex-1 py-1.5 px-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400/20 to-yellow-500/20 hover:from-amber-400 hover:to-yellow-500 hover:text-black text-amber-300 border border-amber-400/35 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-black transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1 disabled:opacity-50"
                              title="تجديد/تمديد شهر إضافي (30 يوماً)"
                            >
                              <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3" />
                              <span>+1 شهر</span>
                            </button>

                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={renewingId === record.id}
                              onClick={() => handleQuickRenew(record.id, 3)}
                              className="py-1.5 px-2 bg-white/5 hover:bg-amber-400/20 text-slate-300 hover:text-amber-300 border border-white/10 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1 disabled:opacity-50"
                              title="تجديد 3 أشهر"
                            >
                              <span>+3 شهور</span>
                            </button>

                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => setSelectedEditRecord(record)}
                              className="py-1.5 px-2 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-slate-300 hover:text-white border border-white/10 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1"
                              title="تحديد الرابط وتاريخ الانتهاء"
                            >
                              <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400" />
                              <span>تحديد</span>
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Admin-Only Print Status Control (Checkbox + List Dropdown) */}
                        <div className="mt-2.5 px-3 py-2 bg-black/50 border border-white/10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px]">
                          <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
                            <input
                              type="checkbox"
                              checked={Boolean(record.is_printed)}
                              disabled={togglingPrintId === record.id}
                              onChange={(e) => handlePrintStatusChange(record, e.target.checked)}
                              className="w-4 h-4 rounded accent-cyan-400 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                            />
                            <span className={`font-extrabold ${record.is_printed ? 'text-cyan-300' : 'text-slate-300'}`}>
                              {record.is_printed ? 'اتطبع' : 'حالة الطباعة'}
                            </span>
                          </label>

                          <select
                            aria-label={`حالة طباعة الكود ${record.id}`}
                            value={record.is_printed ? 'printed' : 'unprinted'}
                            disabled={togglingPrintId === record.id}
                            onChange={(e) => handlePrintStatusChange(record, e.target.value === 'printed')}
                            className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border text-[10px] font-black focus:outline-none cursor-pointer transition ${
                              record.is_printed
                                ? 'bg-cyan-500/15 border-cyan-400/40 text-cyan-300'
                                : 'bg-black/70 border-white/15 text-amber-300'
                            }`}
                          >
                            <option value="unprinted" className="bg-[#121216] text-amber-300">لم يُطبع</option>
                            <option value="printed" className="bg-[#121216] text-cyan-300">تمت الطباعة ✓</option>
                          </select>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Actions */}
                    <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-white/5 space-y-2">
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5">
                        {/* Edit Button */}
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setSelectedEditRecord(record)}
                          className="py-2 bg-amber-400/10 hover:bg-amber-400 hover:text-black text-amber-300 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-black transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1 border border-amber-400/30"
                          title="تعديل الرابط وتاريخ الصلاحية"
                        >
                          <Edit3 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          <span>تعديل</span>
                        </button>

                        {/* Download QR */}
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleDownloadQr(record)}
                          className="py-2 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-slate-200 hover:text-white rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1 border border-white/10"
                          title="تحميل صورة الـ QR بدقة عالية"
                        >
                          <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                          <span>تحميل</span>
                        </button>

                        {/* Test Scan */}
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => onSimulateScan(record.id)}
                          className="py-2 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-amber-300 hover:text-amber-200 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1 border border-amber-500/20"
                          title="فتح رابط المسح الحقيقي"
                        >
                          <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                          <span>مسح</span>
                        </button>

                        {/* Delete Code */}
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setCodeToDelete(record.id)}
                          className="py-2 bg-rose-500/15 hover:bg-rose-500/25 text-rose-300 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1 border border-rose-500/30 cursor-pointer"
                          title="حذف هذا الكود نهائياً"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400" />
                          <span>حذف</span>
                        </button>
                      </div>

                      {record.is_active && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setCodeToDeactivate(record.id)}
                          className="w-full py-2 px-3 bg-amber-500/10 hover:bg-amber-400 hover:text-black text-amber-300 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-black transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 border border-amber-500/35 whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
                          title="إلغاء التفعيل ومسح البيانات لإرجاع الكارت غير مفعل"
                        >
                          <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          <span>إلغاء التفعيل وإرجاعه كجديد</span>
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          ) : (
            /* Table View */
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-right text-xs">
                <thead className="bg-black/50 text-amber-300/90 border-b border-white/5 font-black">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">كود الـ QR</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">الحالة</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">حالة الطباعة</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">مدة التفعيل والاشتراك</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">مرات المسح</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">المنصة</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">الرابط المستهدف</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">تاريخ الإنشاء</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">إجراءات</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-white/5">
                  {filtered.map((record) => (
                    <tr key={record.id} className="hover:bg-amber-400/5 transition">
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono font-black text-amber-300" dir="ltr">
                        {record.id}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-4">
                        <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold whitespace-nowrap ${
                          record.is_active
                            ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30'
                            : 'bg-yellow-500/15 text-yellow-300 border border-yellow-500/30'
                        }`}>
                          {record.is_active ? 'مفعل نشط' : 'في انتظار التفعيل'}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-4">
                        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap">
                          <input
                            type="checkbox"
                            checked={Boolean(record.is_printed)}
                            disabled={togglingPrintId === record.id}
                            onChange={(e) => handlePrintStatusChange(record, e.target.checked)}
                            aria-label={`تحديد الكود ${record.id} كمطبوع`}
                            className="w-4 h-4 rounded accent-cyan-400 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                          />
                          <select
                            aria-label={`حالة طباعة ${record.id}`}
                            value={record.is_printed ? 'printed' : 'unprinted'}
                            disabled={togglingPrintId === record.id}
                            onChange={(e) => handlePrintStatusChange(record, e.target.value === 'printed')}
                            className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border text-[10px] font-black focus:outline-none cursor-pointer ${
                              record.is_printed
                                ? 'bg-cyan-500/15 border-cyan-400/40 text-cyan-300'
                                : 'bg-black/60 border-white/15 text-amber-300'
                            }`}
                          >
                            <option value="unprinted" className="bg-[#121216] text-amber-300">لم يُطبع</option>
                            <option value="printed" className="bg-[#121216] text-cyan-300">تمت الطباعة ✓</option>
                          </select>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-4">
                        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 items-start">
                          {record.expires_at ? (
                            new Date(record.expires_at).getTime() < Date.now() ? (
                              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/30">
                                منتهي الصلاحية ⚠️
                              </span>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30">
                                نشط (باقي {Math.max(0, Math.ceil((new Date(record.expires_at).getTime() - Date.now()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)))} يوم)
                              </span>
                            )
                          ) : (
                            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/30">
                              دائم / غير محدود ✦
                            </span>
                          )}
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={renewingId === record.id}
                              onClick={() => handleQuickRenew(record.id, 1)}
                              className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-amber-400/20 hover:bg-amber-400 hover:text-black text-amber-300 rounded text-[10px] font-black border border-amber-400/30 transition disabled:opacity-50"
                              title="تجديد +1 شهر"
                            >
                              +1 شهر
                            </button>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => setSelectedEditRecord(record)}
                              className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-300 transition"
                              title="تعديل الصلاحية"
                            >
                              <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400" />
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-extrabold text-amber-300">
                        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-full text-xs">
                          <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                          <span className="tabular-nums">{record.total_scans || 0}</span>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-slate-300">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          {record.is_active && record.app_type && (
                            <PlatformBrandIcon
                              type={record.app_type as any}
                              className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0"
                              targetUrl={record.target_url || undefined}
                              walletProvider={record.wallets?.[0]?.provider}
                            />
                          )}
                          <span className="capitalize">{record.is_active ? (record.app_type || '—') : '—'}</span>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono text-slate-400 max-w-xs truncate" dir="ltr">
                        {formatDisplayTarget(record.target_url, record.app_type, record.wallets)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-slate-400 text-[11px]">
                        {new Date(record.created_at).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-center">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setSelectedEditRecord(record)}
                            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-400/10 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer"
                            title="تعديل الرابط وتاريخ الصلاحية"
                          >
                            <Edit3 className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                          </button>
                          {record.is_active && (
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => setCodeToDeactivate(record.id)}
                              className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-500/15 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer"
                              title="إلغاء التفعيل وإعادة الكارت كجديد"
                            >
                              <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                            </button>
                          )}
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleDownloadQr(record)}
                            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer"
                            title="تحميل"
                          >
                            <Download className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                          </button>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => onSimulateScan(record.id)}
                            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-300 hover:bg-white/10 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer"
                            title="مسح"
                          >
                            <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4" />
                          </button>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setCodeToDelete(record.id)}
                            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-400 hover:bg-rose-500/10 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer"
                            title="حذف"
                          >
                            <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400" />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Modals */}
      <BulkGenerateModal
        isOpen={isBulkOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsBulkOpen(false)}
        domainUrl={domainUrl}
        onGenerated={(newRecords) => {
          setRecords((prev) => {
            const existingIds = new Set(prev.map((r) => r.id));
            const fresh = newRecords.filter((r) => !existingIds.has(r.id));
            return [...fresh, ...prev];
          });
          newRecords.forEach((item) => {
            generateQrDataUrl(`${domainUrl}/q/${item.id}`, 200).then((url) => {
              setQrThumbs((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: url }));
            });
          });
        }}
      />

      <UploadQrModal
        isOpen={isUploadOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsUploadOpen(false)}
        onCodeFound={(rec: QrCodeRecord) => {
          setSelectedEditRecord(rec);
          setRecords((prev) => {
            const exists = prev.some((r) => r.id === rec.id);
            if (!exists) return [rec, ...prev];
            return prev.map((r) => (r.id === rec.id ? rec : r));
          });
        }}
      />

      <EditQrModal
        isOpen={!!selectedEditRecord}
        record={selectedEditRecord}
        domainUrl={domainUrl}
        onClose={() => setSelectedEditRecord(null)}
        onPurged={(purged) => {
          setRecords((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r.id === purged.id ? purged : r)));
          setSelectedEditRecord((prev) => (prev && prev.id === purged.id ? purged : prev));
        }}
        onSaved={(updated) => {
          setRecords((prev) => {
            const exists = prev.some((r) => r.id === updated.id);
            if (!exists) return [updated, ...prev];
            return prev.map((r) => (r.id === updated.id ? updated : r));
          });
          setFilterStatus('active');
        }}
        onDeactivated={(resetRecord) => {
          setRecords((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r.id === resetRecord.id ? resetRecord : r)));
          setAdminNotice({
            type: 'success',
            text: `تم إلغاء تفعيل الكود [${resetRecord.id}] ومسح بياناته بالكامل؛ عاد الآن إلى حالة (في انتظار التفعيل) وجاهزاً للتفعيل من جديد ✓`,
          });
        }}
        onDelete={(id) => setCodeToDelete(id)}
      />

      <PrintSheetModal
        isOpen={isPrintOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsPrintOpen(false)}
        records={records}
        domainUrl={domainUrl}
        onMarkedPrinted={(ids) => {
          const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
          const idSet = new Set(ids);
          setRecords((prev) =>
            prev.map((r) =>
              idSet.has(r.id) ? { ...r, is_printed: true, printed_at: nowIso, updated_at: nowIso } : r
            )
          );
          setAdminNotice({
            type: 'success',
            text: `تم نقل ${ids.length} كود إلى خانة الكروت المطبوعة ✓`,
          });
        }}
      />

      <QrScannerModal
        isOpen={isScannerOpen}
        mode="admin"
        onClose={() => setIsScannerOpen(false)}
        onUploadFallback={() => setIsUploadOpen(true)}
        onDeclined={() =>
          setAdminNotice({
            type: 'info',
            text: 'تم إغلاق إذن الكاميرا — يمكنك الضغط على "مسح بالكاميرا للتعديل" في أي وقت لإعادة التخيير وتفعيل الكاميرا.',
          })
        }
        onScannedId={async (id) => {
          setIsScannerOpen(false);
          const rec = (await getQrCode(id)) || records.find((r) => r.id === id);
          if (rec) {
            setSelectedEditRecord(rec);
          } else {
            setAdminNotice({
              type: 'error',
              text: `الرمز "${id}" غير موجود أو تم حذفه من قاعدة البيانات.`,
            });
          }
        }}
      />

      {/* Developer & WhatsApp Settings Modal */}
      <DeveloperSettingsModal
        isOpen={isSettingsModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsSettingsModalOpen(false)}
        settings={platformSettings}
        onSave={handleSaveDeveloperSettings}
      />

      {/* Password Change Modal */}
      {isPasswordModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in">
          <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/35 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl text-white space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-amber-500/20">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-base text-amber-300 flex items-center gap-2">
                <KeyRound className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400" />
                تغيير كلمة مرور الأدمن
              </h3>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setIsPasswordModalOpen(false);
                  setPasswordError(null);
                  setNewPassword('');
                  setConfirmPassword('');
                }}
                className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white p-1"
              >
                ✕
              </button>
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
              أدخل كلمة المرور الجديدة. سيتم تشفيرها بخوارزمية <span className="font-mono text-amber-300 font-bold">SHA-256</span> وتحديثها فوراً في قاعدة البيانات بالكامل (Firestore) لتصبح كلمة المرور الرسمية والوحيدة للوحة التحكم.
            </p>

            {passwordError && (
              <div className="p-3 bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/30 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 flex-shrink-0" />
                <span>{passwordError}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {passwordSuccess && (
              <div className="p-3 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
                <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
                تم حفظ وتحديث كلمة المرور الجديدة في قاعدة البيانات بالكامل بنجاح!
              </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleUpdatePassword} className="space-y-3.5">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-amber-200/90 mb-1">
                  كلمة المرور الحالية:
                </label>
                <input
                  type="password"
                  value={currentPassword}
                  onChange={(e) => setCurrentPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="أدخل كلمة المرور الحالية للتأكيد..."
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-sm text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
                  dir="ltr"
                  required
                  autoFocus
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-amber-200/90 mb-1">
                  كلمة المرور الجديدة:
                </label>
                <input
                  type="password"
                  value={newPassword}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="أدخل كلمة المرور الجديدة (4 خانات فأكثر)..."
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-sm text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
                  dir="ltr"
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-amber-200/90 mb-1">
                  تأكيد كلمة المرور الجديدة:
                </label>
                <input
                  type="password"
                  value={confirmPassword}
                  onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="أعد كتابة كلمة المرور..."
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-sm text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
                  dir="ltr"
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setIsPasswordModalOpen(false);
                    setPasswordError(null);
                    setNewPassword('');
                    setConfirmPassword('');
                  }}
                  className="px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
                >
                  إلغاء
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={passwordSaving || !newPassword.trim()}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 disabled:opacity-50 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl shadow-md transition cursor-pointer"
                >
                  {passwordSaving ? 'جاري الحفظ...' : 'حفظ كلمة المرور'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Delete Confirmation Modal */}
      {codeToDelete && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in">
          <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#121216] border border-rose-500/30 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl text-white space-y-4 text-center">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto border border-rose-500/30">
              <Trash2 className="w-7 h-7" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-black text-white">حذف الكود نهائياً؟</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">
                هل أنت متأكد من حذف الكود <span className="font-mono font-bold text-amber-300 bg-black/60 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-amber-500/20" dir="ltr">{codeToDelete}</span> من لوحة الأدمن ومن قاعدة البيانات؟
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="p-3 bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/30 rounded-xl text-[11px] text-rose-300 text-right leading-relaxed font-semibold">
              ⚠️ تحذير: سيتم حذف هذا الكود نهائياً من قاعدة بيانات الموقع، وأي كارت مطبوع يحمل هذا الرمز سيتوقف عن العمل فوراً ويحول العميل لزر التواصل مع المطور.
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setCodeToDelete(null)}
                disabled={isDeleting}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-slate-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl transition border border-white/10"
              >
                إلغاء
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleConfirmDelete}
                disabled={isDeleting}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md transition whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                {isDeleting ? 'جاري الحذف...' : 'تأكيد الحذف نهائياً'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Deactivate Confirmation Modal (Admin Only) */}
      {codeToDeactivate && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in">
          <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/40 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl text-white space-y-4 text-center">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto border border-amber-500/35">
              <RotateCcw className="w-7 h-7" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-black text-amber-300">إلغاء تفعيل الكارت وإعادته كجديد؟</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-1.5 leading-relaxed">
                هل أنت متأكد من إلغاء تفعيل الكود{' '}
                <span className="font-mono font-bold text-amber-300 bg-black/60 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-amber-500/25" dir="ltr">
                  {codeToDeactivate}
                </span>
                ؟
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="p-3 bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl text-[11px] text-amber-200 text-right leading-relaxed font-semibold">
              سيتم مسح أي رابط أو محافظ أو حساب بنكي أو وصف مكتوب وتصفير عداد المسحات، وسيعود الكارت فوراً إلى حالة <span className="text-white font-black">«في انتظار التفعيل»</span> بحيث يستطيع العميل مسحه وتفعيله لأول مرة من الصفر.
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setCodeToDeactivate(null)}
                disabled={isDeactivating}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-slate-300 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl transition border border-white/10 whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
              >
                إلغاء
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleConfirmDeactivate}
                disabled={isDeactivating}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-300 text-black font-black text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md transition whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {isDeactivating ? 'جاري الإلغاء...' : 'تأكيد إلغاء التفعيل'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/views/CustomerActivationView.tsx
