import { useState } from 'react';
import { 
  Sparkles, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  Loader2, 
  Building2, 
  Wallet, 
  ArrowRight, 
  Check,
  User,
  Phone,
  ShieldCheck,
  X
} from 'lucide-react';
import { activateQrCode } from '../lib/qrService';
import { 
  encodeBankDetails, 
  processSmartLink, 
  triggerSmartRedirect, 
  detectWalletInfo, 
  detectWalletProviderFromUrl,
  isQuickPayUrl,
  sanitizeQuickPayUrl,
  formatTikTokTargetUrl,
  formatInstagramTargetUrl,
  formatTelegramTargetUrl,
  formatSnapchatTargetUrl
} from '../lib/deepLinks';
import { AppType, QrCodeRecord, WalletItem, WalletProvider } from '../types/qr';
import { PlatformBrandIcon, WalletProviderIcon } from '../components/BrandIcons';

interface CustomerActivationViewProps {
  qrId: string;
  onActivated: (updatedRecord: QrCodeRecord) => void;
}

const WALLET_PROVIDERS: Array<{ id: WalletProvider; nameAr: string; nameEn: string; prefix: string; color: string }> = [
  { id: 'vodafone', nameAr: 'ڨودافون كاش', nameEn: 'Vodafone Cash', prefix: '010', color: 'border-red-500/40 text-red-400 bg-red-500/10' },
  { id: 'orange', nameAr: 'أورانج كاش', nameEn: 'Orange Cash', prefix: '012', color: 'border-orange-500/40 text-orange-400 bg-orange-500/10' },
  { id: 'etisalat', nameAr: 'اتصالات كاش', nameEn: 'Etisalat Cash', prefix: '011', color: 'border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-500/10' },
  { id: 'we', nameAr: 'وي باي (WE Pay)', nameEn: 'WE Pay', prefix: '015', color: 'border-purple-500/40 text-purple-400 bg-purple-500/10' },
  { id: 'instapay', nameAr: 'إنستاباي (InstaPay)', nameEn: 'InstaPay', prefix: 'IPN', color: 'border-fuchsia-500/40 text-fuchsia-300 bg-fuchsia-500/10' },
];

export function CustomerActivationView({ qrId, onActivated }: CustomerActivationViewProps) {
  const [selectedType, setSelectedType] = useState<AppType>('whatsapp');
  const [rawInput, setRawInput] = useState('');
  const [bankName, setBankName] = useState('البنك الأهلي المصري (NBE)');
  const [accountNumber, setAccountNumber] = useState('');
  const [accountName, setAccountName] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [activatedRecord, setActivatedRecord] = useState<QrCodeRecord | null>(null);

  // Multi-wallet state (1 to 4 wallets) - starts neutral without forcing any operator logo!
  const [walletCount, setWalletCount] = useState<number>(1);
  const [wallets, setWallets] = useState<Array<{ phone: string; provider: WalletProvider; ownerName: string }>>([
    { phone: '', provider: 'generic', ownerName: '' },
    { phone: '', provider: 'generic', ownerName: '' },
    { phone: '', provider: 'generic', ownerName: '' },
    { phone: '', provider: 'generic', ownerName: '' },
  ]);

  const handleWalletPhoneChange = (index: number, val: string) => {
    setWallets((prev) => {
      const next = [...prev];
      const trimmed = val.trim();
      let newProvider: WalletProvider = 'generic';

      if (trimmed) {
        const isUrl = isQuickPayUrl(trimmed);
        if (isUrl) {
          newProvider = detectWalletProviderFromUrl(trimmed);
        } else {
          const detected = detectWalletInfo(trimmed);
          if (detected.provider !== 'generic') {
            newProvider = detected.provider as WalletProvider;
          }
        }
      }

      next[index] = {
        ...next[index],
        phone: val,
        provider: newProvider,
      };
      return next;
    });
  };

  const handleWalletOwnerChange = (index: number, val: string) => {
    setWallets((prev) => {
      const next = [...prev];
      next[index] = { ...next[index], ownerName: val };
      return next;
    });
  };

  const platforms: Array<{ type: AppType; label: string; placeholder: string; example: string }> = [
    { 
      type: 'whatsapp', 
      label: 'واتساب', 
      placeholder: '01012345678 أو 2010...', 
      example: 'أدخل رقم الواتساب، وسيفتح محادثتك فوراً عند مسح الكارت' 
    },
    { 
      type: 'instapay', 
      label: 'إنستاباي', 
      placeholder: 'https://ipn.eg/S/... (رابط الدفع السريع فقط)', 
      example: 'انسخ رابط الدفع السريع من تطبيق إنستاباي (اطلب دفع > مشاركة الرابط) والصقه هنا ليفتح للعميل صفحة الدفع في التطبيق فوراً.' 
    },
    { 
      type: 'wallet', 
      label: 'محفظة كاش', 
      placeholder: '010... أو رابط دفع سريع (فودافون / أورانج / وي / اتصالات / إنستاباي)', 
      example: 'أدخل رقم المحفظة أو رابط الدفع السريع (حتى إنستاباي ipn.eg)، وسيتم التعرف على الشركة وعرض شعارها تلقائياً مع إمكانية إضافة حتى 4 محافظ.' 
    },
    { 
      type: 'bank', 
      label: 'حساب بنكي', 
      placeholder: 'رقم الحساب البنكي', 
      example: 'يعرض بطاقة بنكية رسمية للمسح مع زر لنسخ رقم الحساب فوراً' 
    },
    { 
      type: 'instagram', 
      label: 'إنستجرام', 
      placeholder: '@your_username أو رابط الحساب', 
      example: 'اكتب اسم حسابك وسيتم فتح تطبيق إنستجرام على صفحتك مباشرة' 
    },
    { 
      type: 'facebook', 
      label: 'فيسبوك', 
      placeholder: 'رابط صفحتك أو حسابك على فيسبوك', 
      example: 'مثال: https://facebook.com/yourpage' 
    },
    { 
      type: 'phone', 
      label: 'اتصال هاتفي', 
      placeholder: '01012345678 أو +20...', 
      example: 'يفتح شاشة الاتصال مباشرة على هاتف العميل برقمك' 
    },
    { 
      type: 'telegram', 
      label: 'تيليجرام', 
      placeholder: '@username أو t.me/...', 
      example: 'يفتح تطبيق تيليجرام للتواصل المباشر' 
    },
    { 
      type: 'tiktok', 
      label: 'تيك توك', 
      placeholder: '@username أو رابط تيك توك', 
      example: 'يفتح تطبيق تيك توك على ملفك الشخصي' 
    },
    { 
      type: 'website', 
      label: 'موقع ويب', 
      placeholder: 'https://yourwebsite.com', 
      example: 'أي رابط ويب ترغب في توجيه العملاء إليه (متجر، قائمة طعام، إلخ)' 
    },
  ];

  const currentPlatformInfo = platforms.find((p) => p.type === selectedType) || platforms[0];

  const handleActivate = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    let valueToSave = rawInput.trim();
    let activeWalletsToSave: WalletItem[] | undefined = undefined;

    if (selectedType === 'bank') {
      if (!accountNumber.trim()) {
        setErrorMsg('يرجى كتابة رقم الحساب البنكي.');
        return;
      }
      valueToSave = encodeBankDetails({
        bankName,
        accountNumber: accountNumber.trim(),
        accountName: accountName.trim(),
      });
    } else if (selectedType === 'wallet') {
      const activeList: WalletItem[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < walletCount; i++) {
        const item = wallets[i];
        const trimmedPhone = item.phone.trim();
        if (!trimmedPhone) {
          setErrorMsg(walletCount === 1 ? 'يرجى إدخال رقم المحفظة أو رابط الدفع السريع.' : `يرجى إدخال بيانات المحفظة رقم ${i + 1} (رقم المحفظة أو رابط الدفع السريع).`);
          return;
        }

        const trimmedOwner = item.ownerName?.trim() || '';
        if (!trimmedOwner) {
          setErrorMsg(walletCount === 1 ? 'يرجى كتابة الاسم (إلزامي).' : `يرجى كتابة الاسم للمحفظة رقم ${i + 1} (إلزامي).`);
          return;
        }

        // Check if quick pay url
        if (isQuickPayUrl(trimmedPhone)) {
          const urlProv = detectWalletProviderFromUrl(trimmedPhone);
          const resolvedProv: WalletProvider = urlProv !== 'generic' ? urlProv : (item.provider !== 'generic' ? item.provider : 'generic');
          const fullUrl = sanitizeQuickPayUrl(trimmedPhone, resolvedProv);
          activeList.push({
            phone: fullUrl,
            provider: resolvedProv,
            ownerName: trimmedOwner,
          });
        } else {
          const detected = detectWalletInfo(trimmedPhone);
          const finalProv = item.provider !== 'generic' ? item.provider : (detected.provider !== 'generic' ? detected.provider : 'generic');
          activeList.push({
            phone: detected.cleanPhone || trimmedPhone,
            provider: finalProv,
            ownerName: trimmedOwner,
          });
        }
      }

      activeWalletsToSave = activeList;
      valueToSave = activeList[0].phone;
    } else if (selectedType === 'instapay') {
      if (!valueToSave) {
        setErrorMsg('يرجى إدخال رابط الدفع السريع من تطبيق إنستاباي.');
        return;
      }
      const hasLink = valueToSave.includes('ipn.eg') || valueToSave.startsWith('http://') || valueToSave.startsWith('https://');
      if (!hasLink) {
        setErrorMsg('تطبيق إنستاباي يتطلب وضع رابط الدفع السريع المباشر (مثل: https://ipn.eg/S/...) وليس اسم المستخدم أو العنوان، حتى يتم فتح صفحة الدفع في التطبيق للعميل فوراً وبدون أي أخطاء.');
        return;
      }
      if (valueToSave.startsWith('ipn.eg/')) {
        valueToSave = `https://${valueToSave}`;
      } else if (!valueToSave.startsWith('http://') && !valueToSave.startsWith('https://')) {
        valueToSave = `https://${valueToSave}`;
      }
    } else if (selectedType === 'tiktok') {
      if (!valueToSave) {
        setErrorMsg('يرجى إدخال اسم المستخدم أو رابط حساب تيك توك.');
        return;
      }
      valueToSave = formatTikTokTargetUrl(valueToSave);
    } else if (selectedType === 'instagram') {
      if (!valueToSave) {
        setErrorMsg('يرجى إدخال اسم المستخدم أو رابط حساب إنستجرام.');
        return;
      }
      valueToSave = formatInstagramTargetUrl(valueToSave);
    } else if (selectedType === 'telegram') {
      if (!valueToSave) {
        setErrorMsg('يرجى إدخال اسم المستخدم أو رابط تيليجرام.');
        return;
      }
      valueToSave = formatTelegramTargetUrl(valueToSave);
    } else if (selectedType === 'snapchat') {
      if (!valueToSave) {
        setErrorMsg('يرجى إدخال اسم المستخدم أو رابط حساب سناب شات.');
        return;
      }
      valueToSave = formatSnapchatTargetUrl(valueToSave);
    } else {
      if (!valueToSave) {
        setErrorMsg('يرجى إدخال الرابط أو البيانات المطلوبة لتفعيل الكارت.');
        return;
      }
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setErrorMsg(null);

    const smartLink = processSmartLink(valueToSave, selectedType);
    const destinationUrl = smartLink.schemeUrl || smartLink.fallbackUrl;

    try {
      const res = await activateQrCode(qrId, valueToSave, selectedType, activeWalletsToSave);

      // 1. If bank or wallet: open dedicated card view
      if (selectedType === 'bank' || selectedType === 'wallet') {
        if (res.record) {
          setActivatedRecord(res.record);
          onActivated(res.record);
        }
        if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
          window.history.pushState({}, '', `/?view=${qrId}`);
        }
        return;
      }

      // 2. For direct redirect (WhatsApp, InstaPay Quick Pay Link, Instagram, etc.):
      if (destinationUrl) {
        try {
          window.location.replace(destinationUrl);
        } catch {
          window.location.href = destinationUrl;
        }
        return;
      } else if (res.record) {
        setActivatedRecord(res.record);
        onActivated(res.record);
        setTimeout(() => {
          window.location.href = `/q/${qrId}`;
        }, 500);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Activation failed:', err);
      if (destinationUrl && selectedType !== 'bank' && selectedType !== 'wallet') {
        try {
          window.location.replace(destinationUrl);
        } catch {
          window.location.href = destinationUrl;
        }
      } else {
        setErrorMsg('حدث خطأ أثناء تفعيل الكارت، يرجى المحاولة مرة أخرى.');
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Determine if submit button is disabled
  const isSubmitDisabled = loading || (
    selectedType === 'bank' 
      ? !accountNumber.trim() 
      : selectedType === 'wallet'
        ? wallets.slice(0, walletCount).some((w) => !w.phone.trim())
        : !rawInput.trim()
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-white selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-black flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-6 relative select-none">
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg relative z-10 stage-3d">
        {!activatedRecord ? (
          <div className="bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-8 shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(212,175,55,0.12)] space-y-6 animate-3d-enter">
            {/* Clean Header with 3D Floating Logo */}
            <div className="text-center space-y-2">
              <img src="/logo.png" alt="Smart Card" className="w-16 h-16 object-contain mx-auto mb-2 drop-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(212,175,55,0.3)] animate-3d-float" />
              <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-amber-400/15 text-amber-300 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-full text-xs font-bold mb-1">
                <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                تفعيل بطاقة Smart Card لأول مرة
              </div>
              <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500 font-mono">
                Smart Card
              </h1>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300">
                اختر التطبيق أو المنصة التي ترغب في ربطها بهذا الكارت (اختيار واحد فقط).
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Error Banner */}
            {errorMsg && (
              <div className="p-3.5 bg-rose-500/20 border border-rose-500/40 rounded-2xl text-xs text-rose-300 flex items-start gap-2.5">
                <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span>{errorMsg}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Form */}
            <form onSubmit={handleActivate} className="space-y-6">
              {/* Step 1: Platform Selection (Strict Single Select) */}
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                  <span className="font-bold text-amber-200">1. اختر منصة أو تطبيق الربط (واحد فقط):</span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-amber-400/80 font-bold bg-amber-400/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-amber-400/20">
                    محدد: {currentPlatformInfo.label}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2.5 stage-3d">
                  {platforms.map((p) => {
                     const isSelected = selectedType === p.type;
                     return (
                       <button
                         key={p.type}
                         type="button"
                         onClick={() => {
                           if (p.type !== selectedType) {
                             setSelectedType(p.type);
                             setRawInput('');
                             setAccountNumber('');
                             setAccountName('');
                             setWalletCount(1);
                             setWallets([
                               { phone: '', provider: 'generic', ownerName: '' },
                               { phone: '', provider: 'generic', ownerName: '' },
                               { phone: '', provider: 'generic', ownerName: '' },
                               { phone: '', provider: 'generic', ownerName: '' },
                             ]);
                           }
                           setErrorMsg(null);
                         }}
                         className={`p-3 rounded-2xl border flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer text-center relative platform-card-3d ${
                           isSelected
                             ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-amber-500/20 to-black border-amber-400 ring-1 ring-amber-400/50 platform-card-3d-active'
                             : 'bg-[#18181b]/70 border-white/10 hover:border-amber-400/40 hover:bg-[#18181b]'
                         }`}
                       >
                         {isSelected && (
                           <div className="absolute top-2 left-2 w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-amber-400 text-black flex items-center justify-center shadow">
                             <Check className="w-2.5 h-2.5 stroke-[3]" />
                           </div>
                         )}
                         <div className={isSelected ? 'animate-icon-alive' : 'transition-transform duration-200'}>
                           <PlatformBrandIcon type={p.type} className="w-8 h-8" />
                         </div>
                         <span className={`text-xs font-bold leading-tight ${isSelected ? 'text-amber-300 animate-name-alive' : 'text-slate-200'}`}>
                           {p.label}
                         </span>
                       </button>
                     );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Step 2: Dynamic Input Field */}
              {selectedType === 'bank' ? (
                <div className="p-4 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl space-y-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-black text-amber-200">
                    <PlatformBrandIcon type="bank" className="w-5 h-5" />
                    <span>2. بيانات الحساب البنكي:</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300">اسم البنك:</label>
                    <select
                      value={bankName}
                      onChange={(e) => setBankName(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-[#09090b] border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
                    >
                      <option value="البنك الأهلي المصري (NBE)">البنك الأهلي المصري (NBE)</option>
                      <option value="بنك مصر (Banque Misr)">بنك مصر (Banque Misr)</option>
                      <option value="البنك التجاري الدولي (CIB)">البنك التجاري الدولي (CIB)</option>
                      <option value="بنك قطر الوطني (QNB)">بنك قطر الوطني (QNB)</option>
                      <option value="بنك القاهرة (Banque du Caire)">بنك القاهرة (Banque du Caire)</option>
                      <option value="بنك الإسكندرية (AlexBank)">بنك الإسكندرية (AlexBank)</option>
                      <option value="بنك فيصل الإسلامي">بنك فيصل الإسلامي</option>
                      <option value="بنك آخر">بنك آخر</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300">رقم الحساب البنكي (إلزامي):</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={accountNumber}
                      onChange={(e) => setAccountNumber(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="مثال: 12345678901234"
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-xs font-mono font-bold text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
                      dir="ltr"
                      required
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300">اسم صاحب الحساب (اختياري):</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={accountName}
                      onChange={(e) => setAccountName(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="مثال: أحمد محمد علي"
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-xs text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : selectedType === 'wallet' ? (
                /* MULTI-WALLET ACTIVATION SECTION */
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  {/* Step 2A: Wallet Count Selector */}
                  <div className="p-4 bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/25 rounded-2xl space-y-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-black text-amber-200">
                        <Wallet className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                        <span>2. كم عدد المحافظ التي ترغب بربطها بالكارت؟</span>
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-300 font-bold bg-amber-400/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-amber-400/20">
                        حتى 4 شركات
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                      إذا كان لديك أكثر من رقم محفظة كاش (فودافون، أورانج، اتصالات، وي)، اختر العدد لعرض لوجو كل شركة والاسم بشكل منفصل:
                    </p>

                    {/* 4 Segmented Count Buttons */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 pt-1">
                      {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((count) => {
                        const isCountSelected = walletCount === count;
                        return (
                          <button
                            key={count}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setWalletCount(count)}
                            className={`py-2.5 px-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 border cursor-pointer ${
                              isCountSelected
                                ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black border-amber-300 shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/25 font-black scale-[1.02]'
                                : 'bg-[#18181b] text-slate-300 border-white/10 hover:border-amber-400/40 hover:text-white'
                            }`}
                          >
                            <span className="text-sm font-mono font-black">{count}</span>
                            <span className="text-[10px] opacity-90">
                              {count === 1 ? 'محفظة واحدة' : count === 2 ? 'محفظتان' : `${count} محافظ`}
                            </span>
                          </button>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Step 2B: Wallet Input Cards */}
                  <div className="space-y-3.5">
                    {Array.from({ length: walletCount }).map((_, index) => {
                      const item = wallets[index];
                      const hasInput = Boolean(item.phone && item.phone.trim());
                      const detected = hasInput ? detectWalletInfo(item.phone) : null;
                      const isUrl = hasInput ? isQuickPayUrl(item.phone) : false;
                      const urlProv = hasInput && isUrl ? detectWalletProviderFromUrl(item.phone) : 'generic';
                      const activeProvider: WalletProvider = (hasInput 
                        ? (item.provider !== 'generic' ? item.provider : (urlProv !== 'generic' ? urlProv : (detected && detected.provider !== 'generic' ? detected.provider : 'generic')))
                        : (item.provider !== 'generic' ? item.provider : 'generic')
                      ) as WalletProvider;
                      const isKnownBrand = hasInput && activeProvider !== 'generic';
                      const activeMeta = isKnownBrand ? WALLET_PROVIDERS.find(p => p.id === activeProvider) : null;

                      return (
                        <div 
                          key={index} 
                          className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border transition-all space-y-3 bg-black/50 animate-3d-enter ${
                            isKnownBrand && activeProvider === 'vodafone' ? 'border-red-500/35 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(230,0,0,0.1)]' :
                            isKnownBrand && activeProvider === 'orange' ? 'border-orange-500/35 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(255,121,0,0.1)]' :
                            isKnownBrand && activeProvider === 'etisalat' ? 'border-emerald-500/35 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(113,158,25,0.1)]' :
                            isKnownBrand && activeProvider === 'we' ? 'border-purple-500/35 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(102,45,145,0.15)]' :
                            isKnownBrand && activeProvider === 'instapay' ? 'border-fuchsia-500/40 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(168,85,247,0.18)]' :
                            'border-amber-500/25 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(212,175,55,0.06)]'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {/* Card Header with Provider Logo & Title (100% Automatic — No Dropdown or Auto-Detect Hint Shown) */}
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-white/10">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                              <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-black/80 border border-white/15 p-1 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 shadow ${isKnownBrand ? 'animate-icon-alive' : ''}`}>
                                <WalletProviderIcon provider={isKnownBrand ? activeProvider : 'generic'} size={28} className="w-full h-full object-contain" />
                              </div>
                              <div>
                                <span className="text-xs font-black text-white block">
                                  {walletCount > 1 ? `المحفظة رقم ${index + 1}` : 'بيانات محفظة الكاش'}
                                </span>
                                {isKnownBrand && activeMeta && (
                                  <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md border inline-block mt-0.5 animate-name-alive ${activeMeta.color}`}>
                                    {activeMeta.nameAr}
                                  </span>
                                )}
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          {/* Field 1: Phone Number OR Quick Pay Link */}
                          <div className="space-y-1.5">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-slate-300">
                              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-200">
                                <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                                <span>رقم المحفظة أو رابط الدفع السريع (إلزامي):</span>
                                {isKnownBrand && activeMeta && !isUrl && (
                                  <span className="text-amber-400 font-mono font-black">({activeMeta.prefix})</span>
                                )}
                              </span>
                              {item.phone && (
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => handleWalletPhoneChange(index, '')}
                                  className="text-[11px] text-rose-400 hover:text-rose-300 font-bold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
                                >
                                  <X className="w-3 h-3" />
                                  <span>مسح</span>
                                </button>
                              )}
                            </div>
                            <div className="relative">
                              <input
                                type="text"
                                value={item.phone}
                                onChange={(e) => handleWalletPhoneChange(index, e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="010... أو رابط الدفع السريع https://..."
                                className="w-full pr-3.5 pl-10 py-2.5 bg-black/70 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl text-xs font-mono font-black text-amber-300 placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400 transition"
                                dir="ltr"
                                required
                              />
                              {item.phone && (
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => handleWalletPhoneChange(index, '')}
                                  className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-300 transition"
                                >
                                  <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                </button>
                              )}
                            </div>

                            {/* Quick Pay URL Detected Badge */}
                            {isUrl && (
                              <div className="p-2 px-2.5 bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/25 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-amber-200 font-bold">
                                <WalletProviderIcon provider={isKnownBrand ? activeProvider : 'generic'} className="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0" />
                                <span>
                                  {isKnownBrand && activeMeta
                                    ? `تم التعرف تلقائياً على (${activeMeta.nameAr}) — سيظهر شعار الشركة وزر الدفع السريع ⚡`
                                    : 'تم التعرف على رابط دفع سريع ⚡ سيظهر زر الدفع والتحويل المباشر'}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </div>

                          {/* Field 2: Owner Name - Mandatory */}
                          <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-1">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-slate-300">
                              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-200/90 whitespace-nowrap">
                                <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
                                <span>الاسم (إلزامي):</span>
                              </span>
                              <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-400 font-bold shrink-0">إلزامي</span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="relative">
                              <input
                                type="text"
                                value={item.ownerName}
                                onChange={(e) => handleWalletOwnerChange(index, e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="مثال: أحمد محمد علي"
                                className="w-full pr-3.5 pl-8 py-2.5 bg-black/70 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400 transition"
                                required
                              />
                              {item.ownerName && (
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => handleWalletOwnerChange(index, '')}
                                  className="absolute left-2.5 top-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-300"
                                >
                                  <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                </button>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          {/* Operator Visual Confirmation */}
                          {hasInput && isKnownBrand && activeMeta && (
                            <div className="p-2.5 bg-black/40 rounded-xl border border-white/10 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px]">
                              <span className="text-slate-400">الشركة المحددة:</span>
                              <span className="font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <WalletProviderIcon provider={activeProvider} className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
                                <span>{activeMeta.nameAr} ({activeMeta.nameEn})</span>
                                <span className="text-amber-300">{isUrl ? '• رابط دفع سريع' : '• رقم محفظة'}</span>
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-black text-amber-200">
                    <PlatformBrandIcon type={selectedType} className="w-5 h-5" />
                    <span>2. أدخل بيانات {currentPlatformInfo.label}:</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={rawInput}
                      onChange={(e) => setRawInput(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder={currentPlatformInfo.placeholder}
                      autoCapitalize="none"
                      autoCorrect="off"
                      spellCheck={false}
                      className="w-full px-4 py-3.5 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400 transition font-sans"
                      dir="ltr"
                      required
                      autoFocus
                    />
                  </div>
                  {selectedType === 'instapay' && (rawInput.toLowerCase().includes('ipn.eg') || rawInput.toLowerCase().includes('instapay')) && (
                    <div className="p-2.5 bg-fuchsia-500/10 border border-fuchsia-500/30 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-fuchsia-200 animate-in fade-in">
                      <PlatformBrandIcon type="instapay" className="w-6 h-6 flex-shrink-0" />
                      <span>تم التعرف على رابط الدفع السريع لـ إنستاباي (InstaPay Egypt) ✓</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-200/70 leading-relaxed">
                    💡 {currentPlatformInfo.example}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Submit Button */}
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isSubmitDisabled}
                className="w-full py-4 px-6 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 disabled:opacity-40 text-black font-black text-base rounded-2xl shadow-xl shadow-amber-500/25 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin text-black" />
                    <span>جاري تفعيل الكارت...</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <PlatformBrandIcon type={selectedType} className="w-6 h-6" />
                    <span>
                      {selectedType === 'wallet' && walletCount > 1
                        ? `تأكيد وتفعيل الكارت بـ (${walletCount} محافظ كاش) 🚀`
                        : `تأكيد وتفعيل كارت (${currentPlatformInfo.label}) الآن 🚀`}
                    </span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* Celebration View */
          <div className="bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-8 shadow-2xl text-center space-y-5 animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-amber-400/20 border-2 border-amber-400 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/30">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-pulse text-amber-400" />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
                تم تفعيل كارتك بنجاح 🎉
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">
                جاري نقلك إلى شاشة الكارت المباشرة الآن...
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/views/SmartRedirectView.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { 
  Zap, 
  ExternalLink, 
  Smartphone, 
  RefreshCw,
  MessageSquare,
  Instagram,
  Facebook,
  CreditCard,
  Phone,
  Send,
  Video,
  Globe,
  Building2,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Wallet,
  ArrowRight,
  ShieldCheck,
  Sparkles,
  User
} from 'lucide-react';
import { processSmartLink, triggerSmartRedirect, isRunningInIframe, detectWalletInfo, detectWalletProviderFromUrl, getWalletProviderMeta, isQuickPayUrl, sanitizeQuickPayUrl } from '../lib/deepLinks';
import { QrCodeRecord, WalletItem, WalletProvider } from '../types/qr';
import { PlatformBrandIcon, WalletProviderIcon } from '../components/BrandIcons';

interface SmartRedirectViewProps {
  record: QrCodeRecord;
}

export function SmartRedirectView({ record }: SmartRedirectViewProps) {
  const [copiedField, setCopiedField] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [hasAttemptedRedirect, setHasAttemptedRedirect] = useState(false);
  const [inIframe, setInIframe] = useState(false);

  const linkInfo = processSmartLink(record.target_url || '', record.app_type);
  const isWallet = record.app_type === 'wallet' || linkInfo.isWalletCard || (Array.isArray(record.wallets) && record.wallets.length > 0) || (Boolean(record.target_url) && record.target_url!.startsWith('tel:*9*7*'));
  const isBank = record.app_type === 'bank' || linkInfo.isBankCard || (Boolean(record.target_url) && record.target_url!.startsWith('bank://'));

  useEffect(() => {
    const isIframe = isRunningInIframe();
    setInIframe(isIframe);

    // If it is NOT a bank card, wallet card, or instapay card: instant auto-redirect!
    if (!isBank && !isWallet && !linkInfo.isBankCard && !linkInfo.isWalletCard && !linkInfo.isInstaPayCard) {
      if (!isIframe) {
        triggerSmartRedirect(linkInfo);
        setHasAttemptedRedirect(true);
      } else {
        setHasAttemptedRedirect(true);
      }
    }
  }, [record.id, record.target_url]);

  const handleCopy = (text: string, fieldKey: string) => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopiedField(fieldKey);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedField(null), 2000);
  };

  const handleDirectLaunch = () => {
    const target = linkInfo.schemeUrl || linkInfo.fallbackUrl;
    if (!target) return;

    if (inIframe) {
      window.open(target, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
    } else {
      triggerSmartRedirect(linkInfo);
    }
  };

  // 1. BANK CARD VIEW: If the QR code is configured for Bank Transfer
  if (linkInfo.isBankCard || record.app_type === 'bank') {
    const bank = linkInfo.bankDetails || {
      bankName: 'حساب بنكي',
      accountNumber: record.target_url || '',
    };

    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-white selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-black flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-6 relative select-none stage-3d">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.15)] space-y-6 animate-3d-enter card-3d-tilt">
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="text-center space-y-2">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-400 via-yellow-500 to-amber-600 text-black flex items-center justify-center mx-auto shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/30 font-black animate-3d-float">
              <Building2 className="w-8 h-8 text-black" />
            </div>
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-amber-400/15 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-full text-[11px] font-black text-amber-300">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              بيانات التحويل البنكي المعتمدة
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
              {bank.bankName || 'حساب بنكي'}
            </h1>
          </div>

          {/* Bank Account Info Card */}
          <div className="bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-4">
            {/* Account Number */}
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-amber-200/80 font-semibold">
                <span>رقم الحساب البنكي:</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">اضغط للنسخ الفوري</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl p-3">
                <span className="font-mono text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-amber-300 tracking-wider select-all" dir="ltr">
                  {bank.accountNumber || '—'}
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleCopy(bank.accountNumber || '', 'acc')}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-lg shadow-md transition"
                >
                  {copiedField === 'acc' ? (
                    <>
                      <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
                      <span>تم النسخ!</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Copy className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
                      <span>نسخ</span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Account Name */}
            {bank.accountName && (
              <div className="space-y-1 pt-2 border-t border-white/5">
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">اسم المستفيد / صاحب الحساب:</span>
                <p className="font-bold text-sm text-white">{bank.accountName}</p>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* IBAN */}
            {bank.iban && (
              <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-2 border-t border-white/5">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-400">
                  <span>الآيبان الدولي (IBAN):</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl p-2.5">
                  <span className="font-mono text-xs text-amber-200 select-all truncate" dir="ltr">
                    {bank.iban}
                  </span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleCopy(bank.iban || '', 'iban')}
                    className="p-1.5 text-amber-400 hover:text-white transition"
                    title="نسخ الآيبان"
                  >
                    {copiedField === 'iban' ? <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" /> : <Copy className="w-4 h-4" />}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Quick Payment Apps Launchers */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <p className="text-xs text-center text-slate-400 font-medium">التحويل المباشر عبر التطبيقات:</p>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <a
                href="https://ipn.eg"
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 p-3 bg-amber-400/10 hover:bg-amber-400/20 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-amber-300 transition"
              >
                <CreditCard className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                <span>فتح InstaPay</span>
              </a>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleCopy(bank.accountNumber || '', 'acc')}
                className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 p-3 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white transition"
              >
                <Copy className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                <span>نسخ لتطبيق البنك</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // 2. WALLET VIEW (Vodafone Cash, Etisalat Cash, Orange Cash, WE Pay - Single or Multiple Wallets)
  if (isWallet) {
    const rawPhone = linkInfo.walletPhone || linkInfo.displayUrl || record.target_url || '';
    let cleanRaw = rawPhone;
    if (cleanRaw.startsWith('tel:*9*7*')) {
      cleanRaw = cleanRaw.replace('tel:*9*7*', '').replace(/#|%23/g, '');
    }
    
    const normalizeWalletPhone = (val: string) => {
      const raw = (val || '').trim();
      return raw.startsWith('tel:*9*7*')
        ? raw.replace('tel:*9*7*', '').replace(/#|%23/g, '').trim()
        : raw;
    };

    // Normalize wallets list (fallback to single wallet from target_url if wallets array not present)
    const activeWallets: WalletItem[] = (record.wallets && record.wallets.length > 0)
      ? record.wallets.map((w) => ({ ...w, phone: normalizeWalletPhone(w.phone) }))
      : [{
          phone: cleanRaw,
          provider: (linkInfo.walletProvider || detectWalletInfo(cleanRaw).provider || 'vodafone') as WalletProvider,
          ownerName: '',
        }];

    const isMultiWallet = activeWallets.length > 1;

    // Helper to get color/style meta per provider
    const getProviderStyle = (provider: WalletProvider) => {
      switch (provider) {
        case 'orange':
          return {
            border: 'border-orange-500/40 shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(255,121,0,0.2)]',
            badge: 'bg-orange-500/15 text-orange-300 border-orange-500/30',
            gradient: 'from-orange-200 via-orange-400 to-amber-500',
            ring: 'ring-orange-400/30',
          };
        case 'etisalat':
          return {
            border: 'border-emerald-500/40 shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(113,158,25,0.2)]',
            badge: 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30',
            gradient: 'from-emerald-200 via-emerald-400 to-green-500',
            ring: 'ring-emerald-400/30',
          };
        case 'we':
          return {
            border: 'border-purple-500/40 shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(102,45,145,0.25)]',
            badge: 'bg-purple-500/15 text-purple-300 border-purple-500/30',
            gradient: 'from-purple-200 via-purple-400 to-indigo-400',
            ring: 'ring-purple-400/30',
          };
        case 'instapay':
          return {
            border: 'border-fuchsia-500/40 shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(168,85,247,0.22)]',
            badge: 'bg-fuchsia-500/15 text-fuchsia-300 border-fuchsia-500/30',
            gradient: 'from-fuchsia-200 via-purple-300 to-amber-400',
            ring: 'ring-fuchsia-400/30',
          };
        case 'vodafone':
          return {
            border: 'border-red-500/40 shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(230,0,0,0.2)]',
            badge: 'bg-red-500/15 text-red-300 border-red-500/30',
            gradient: 'from-red-200 via-red-400 to-rose-500',
            ring: 'ring-red-400/30',
          };
        case 'generic':
        default:
          return {
            border: 'border-amber-500/40 shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(234,179,8,0.2)]',
            badge: 'bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/30',
            gradient: 'from-amber-200 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500',
            ring: 'ring-amber-400/30',
          };
      }
    };

    // --- CASE A: MULTI-WALLET VIEW (2, 3, or 4 Wallets) ---
    if (isMultiWallet) {
      return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-white selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-black flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-6 relative select-none stage-3d">
          <div className="w-full max-w-lg bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-7 space-y-5 text-center animate-3d-enter backdrop-blur-2xl shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.12)]">
            
            {/* Header with Smart Card Logo */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <img src="/logo.png" alt="Smart Card" className="w-14 h-14 object-contain mx-auto mb-1 drop-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(212,175,55,0.3)] animate-3d-float" />
              <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black bg-amber-400/15 text-amber-300 border border-amber-400/30">
                <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                <span>متاح {activeWallets.length} محافظ كاش للدفع الفوري ⚡</span>
              </div>
              <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
                محافظ الكاش المتاحة للتحويل
              </h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">
                اختر المحفظة التابعة لشبكتك لإتمام عملية التحويل أو الدفع الفوري:
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* List of Luxury Wallet Cards */}
            <div className="space-y-4 text-right">
              {activeWallets.map((walletItem, idx) => {
                const isUrl = isQuickPayUrl(walletItem.phone);
                const detected = isUrl ? null : detectWalletInfo(walletItem.phone);
                const urlProv = isUrl ? detectWalletProviderFromUrl(walletItem.phone) : 'generic';
                const explicitProv = walletItem.provider && walletItem.provider !== 'generic' ? walletItem.provider : null;
                const prov: WalletProvider = isUrl
                  ? (urlProv !== 'generic' ? urlProv : (explicitProv || 'generic'))
                  : (explicitProv || (detected && detected.provider !== 'generic' ? detected.provider : 'generic'));
                const style = getProviderStyle(prov);
                const isCopied = copiedField === `wallet_${idx}`;
                const formatted = detected?.formattedPhone || walletItem.phone;
                const provMeta = getWalletProviderMeta(prov);
                const companyTitle = detected?.companyNameAr || provMeta.companyNameAr;
                const providerNameAr = detected?.nameAr || provMeta.nameAr;
                const providerNameEn = detected?.nameEn || provMeta.nameEn;
                const cleanTargetUrl = isUrl ? sanitizeQuickPayUrl(walletItem.phone, prov) : walletItem.phone.trim();

                return (
                  <div
                    key={idx}
                    className={`bg-black/70 border rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-3.5 transition-all relative overflow-hidden card-3d-tilt ${style.border}`}
                  >
                    {/* Top Row: Brand Logo + Operator Title */}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-xl bg-black/80 border border-white/15 p-1.5 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 shadow-md animate-icon-alive">
                          <WalletProviderIcon provider={prov} size={42} className="w-full h-full object-contain" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <div className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold border mb-0.5 ${style.badge}`}>
                            <span>{companyTitle}</span>
                          </div>
                          <h2 className={`text-base font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r ${style.gradient}`}>
                            {providerNameAr}
                          </h2>
                          <p className="text-[11px] font-mono font-bold text-slate-400">
                            {providerNameEn}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-white/5 border border-white/10 font-mono">
                        #{idx + 1}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* Owner Name Badge (if set) - Single-line proportional bar */}
                    {walletItem.ownerName && (
                      <div className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5 px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/25 rounded-xl">
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
                          <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
                          <span>الاسم:</span>
                        </span>
                        <span
                          className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-left font-black text-[13px] sm:text-sm text-white whitespace-nowrap truncate"
                          dir="auto"
                          title={walletItem.ownerName}
                        >
                          {walletItem.ownerName}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {isUrl ? (
                      /* Quick Pay Link Case - Hide raw URL, keep only owner name & Quick Pay launch button */
                      <div className="pt-1">
                        <a
                          href={cleanTargetUrl}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            if (cleanTargetUrl.startsWith('intent://')) {
                              window.location.href = cleanTargetUrl;
                              e.preventDefault();
                            }
                          }}
                          className="w-full min-h-[48px] py-3 px-4 text-xs sm:text-sm font-black rounded-xl shadow-lg transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black shadow-amber-500/20 whitespace-nowrap"
                        >
                          <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4 text-black shrink-0" />
                          <span className="truncate">الانتقال للدفع السريع الآن بـ ({providerNameAr}) ⚡</span>
                        </a>
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      /* Phone Number Case - Single centered display + ONE copy button underneath */
                      <div className="space-y-3">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 border border-amber-500/25 rounded-xl py-3 px-4">
                          <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-lg sm:text-xl font-black text-amber-300 tracking-wider select-all text-center" dir="ltr">
                            {formatted}
                          </span>
                        </div>

                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleCopy(walletItem.phone, `wallet_${idx}`)}
                          className={`w-full min-h-[48px] py-3 px-4 text-xs sm:text-sm font-black rounded-xl shadow-lg transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap ${
                            isCopied
                              ? 'bg-emerald-500 hover:bg-emerald-400 text-black ring-2 ring-emerald-300'
                              : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {isCopied ? (
                            <>
                              <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-black shrink-0 animate-bounce" />
                              <span className="truncate">تم نسخ رقم ({providerNameAr}) بنجاح ✓</span>
                            </>
                          ) : (
                            <>
                              <Copy className="w-4 h-4 text-black shrink-0" />
                              <span className="truncate">نسخ رقم محفظة ({providerNameAr})</span>
                            </>
                          )}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            {/* Clear Transfer Instructions */}
            <div className="p-3.5 bg-black/40 rounded-2xl border border-white/5 text-right space-y-1.5 text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
              <p className="font-bold text-amber-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span>💡</span>
                <span>طريقة التحويل والدفع:</span>
              </p>
              <ol className="list-decimal list-inside space-y-1 pr-1 text-slate-300">
                <li>اختر المحفظة التابعة لشبكة خطك أعلاه.</li>
                <li>للدفع بالرابط السريع: اضغط على زر <strong>الانتقال للدفع السريع الآن</strong> للتحويل الفوري.</li>
                <li>للدفع برقم الهاتف: اضغط على زر <strong>نسخ رقم محفظة</strong> أسفل الرقم ثم افتح تطبيق المحفظة وأتم التحويل.</li>
              </ol>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    // --- CASE B: SINGLE WALLET VIEW (1 Wallet) ---
    const singleWallet = activeWallets[0];
    const isUrl = isQuickPayUrl(singleWallet.phone);
    const detected = isUrl ? null : detectWalletInfo(singleWallet.phone);
    const urlProv = isUrl ? detectWalletProviderFromUrl(singleWallet.phone) : 'generic';
    const explicitProv = singleWallet.provider && singleWallet.provider !== 'generic' ? singleWallet.provider : null;
    const phone = detected?.cleanPhone || singleWallet.phone;
    const formattedPhone = detected?.formattedPhone || phone;
    const provider: WalletProvider = isUrl
      ? (urlProv !== 'generic' ? urlProv : (explicitProv || 'generic'))
      : (explicitProv || (detected && detected.provider !== 'generic' ? detected.provider : 'generic'));
    const provMeta = getWalletProviderMeta(provider);
    const providerNameAr = detected?.nameAr || provMeta.nameAr;
    const providerNameEn = detected?.nameEn || provMeta.nameEn;
    const companyName = detected?.companyNameAr || provMeta.companyNameAr;
    const style = getProviderStyle(provider);
    const isCopied = copiedField === 'wallet';
    const cleanTargetUrl = isUrl ? sanitizeQuickPayUrl(singleWallet.phone, provider) : singleWallet.phone.trim();

    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-white selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-black flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-6 relative select-none stage-3d">
        <div className={`w-full max-w-md bg-[#121216] border rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6 text-center animate-3d-enter card-3d-tilt backdrop-blur-2xl ${style.border}`}>
          
          {/* Official Company Logo */}
          <div className="relative inline-block mx-auto">
            <div className="w-48 h-24 sm:w-56 sm:h-28 rounded-2xl bg-black/60 border border-white/15 p-3 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto shadow-2xl transition hover:scale-105 backdrop-blur-md animate-3d-float">
              <WalletProviderIcon provider={provider} size={140} className="w-full h-full max-h-20 object-contain drop-shadow-lg" />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Provider and Company Titles */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <div className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-black border ${style.badge}`}>
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{companyName}</span>
            </div>

            {/* Arabic Wallet Name */}
            <h1 className={`text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r ${style.gradient}`}>
              {providerNameAr}
            </h1>

            {/* English Wallet Name */}
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-slate-300 tracking-wider">
              {providerNameEn}
            </p>

            {/* Owner Name Badge (if set on single wallet) - Single-line proportional bar */}
            {singleWallet.ownerName && (
              <div className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5 px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-amber-400/15 border border-amber-400/35 rounded-xl mt-3">
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
                  <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
                  <span>الاسم:</span>
                </span>
                <span
                  className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-left font-black text-[13px] sm:text-sm text-white whitespace-nowrap truncate"
                  dir="auto"
                  title={singleWallet.ownerName}
                >
                  {singleWallet.ownerName}
                </span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {isUrl ? (
            /* Quick Pay Link Case - Hide raw URL, keep only owner name & Quick Pay launch button */
            <div className="pt-2">
              <a
                href={cleanTargetUrl}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                onClick={(e) => {
                  if (cleanTargetUrl.startsWith('intent://')) {
                    window.location.href = cleanTargetUrl;
                    e.preventDefault();
                  }
                }}
                className="w-full min-h-[48px] py-3.5 px-5 text-sm sm:text-base font-black rounded-2xl shadow-xl transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 cursor-pointer bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black shadow-amber-500/30 whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                <ExternalLink className="w-5 h-5 text-black shrink-0" />
                <span className="truncate">الانتقال للدفع السريع الآن بـ ({providerNameAr}) ⚡</span>
              </a>
            </div>
          ) : (
            /* Phone Number Display Box + ONE Copy Button Underneath */
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="bg-black/60 border border-white/10 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium">
                  <span>رقم المحفظة لتحويل الأموال:</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-400/90 font-bold">جاهز للتحويل الفوري</span>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl py-3.5 px-4">
                  <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-amber-300 tracking-wider select-all text-center" dir="ltr">
                    {formattedPhone}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Single Prominent Primary Copy Button Underneath */}
              <div className="space-y-4 pt-1">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleCopy(phone, 'wallet')}
                  className={`w-full min-h-[48px] py-3.5 px-5 text-sm sm:text-base font-black rounded-2xl shadow-xl transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap ${
                    isCopied
                      ? 'bg-emerald-500 hover:bg-emerald-400 text-black shadow-emerald-500/25 ring-2 ring-emerald-300'
                      : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black shadow-amber-500/25'
                  }`}
                >
                  {isCopied ? (
                    <>
                      <Check className="w-5 h-5 text-black shrink-0 animate-bounce" />
                      <span className="truncate">تم نسخ رقم المحفظة بنجاح ✓</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Copy className="w-5 h-5 text-black shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">نسخ رقم محفظة ({providerNameAr})</span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>

                {/* Clear, simple instructions */}
                <div className="p-3.5 bg-black/40 rounded-2xl border border-white/5 text-right space-y-2 text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                  <p className="font-bold text-amber-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <span>💡</span>
                    <span>طريقة التحويل:</span>
                  </p>
                  <ol className="list-decimal list-inside space-y-1 pr-1 text-slate-300">
                    <li>اضغط على زر <strong>نسخ رقم محفظة</strong> أسفل الرقم.</li>
                    <li>افتح تطبيق محفظتك الخاصة ({providerNameAr}).</li>
                    <li>الصق الرقم وحدد المبلغ المطلوب وأكّد عملية التحويل.</li>
                  </ol>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // 3. INSTAPAY IPA CARD VIEW
  if (linkInfo.isInstaPayCard) {
    const handle = linkInfo.instaPayHandle || record.target_url || '';
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-white selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-black flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-6 relative select-none stage-3d">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.15)] space-y-6 text-center animate-3d-enter card-3d-tilt backdrop-blur-2xl">
          <div className="w-18 h-18 rounded-2xl bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/40 p-2 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 animate-3d-float">
            <PlatformBrandIcon type="instapay" className="w-12 h-12" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-amber-400/15 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-full text-[11px] font-black text-amber-300 mb-2">
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              عنوان الدفع اللحظي (IPA)
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
              إنستاباي InstaPay
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-amber-200/70 mt-1">انقر لنسخ عنوان الدفع والتحويل في ثوانٍ</p>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-2">
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">عنوان الدفع اللحظي الخاص بي:</span>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl p-3">
              <span className="font-mono text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-amber-300 tracking-wide truncate max-w-[210px]" dir="ltr">
                {handle}
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleCopy(handle, 'instapay')}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-lg transition shadow-md shadow-amber-500/20 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {copiedField === 'instapay' ? <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-black" /> : <Copy className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />}
                <span>{copiedField === 'instapay' ? 'تم النسخ!' : 'نسخ'}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3 pt-1">
            <a
              href="https://ipn.eg"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="w-full py-3.5 px-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black font-black text-xs rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/25 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <CreditCard className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
              <span>فتح رابط موقع إنستاباي InstaPay</span>
            </a>

            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
              💡 انسخ العنوان ثم افتح التطبيق واضغط على &quot;إرسال نقود&quot; والصق العنوان.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // 4. INSTANT DIRECT REDIRECTION SPLASH (WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, Website, YouTube, etc.)
  const targetLink = linkInfo.schemeUrl || linkInfo.fallbackUrl;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-white flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center select-none relative overflow-hidden stage-3d">
      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-2xl bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/30 flex items-center justify-center shadow-xl shadow-amber-500/20 animate-3d-float">
          <PlatformBrandIcon type={linkInfo.appType} className="w-12 h-12 animate-icon-alive" />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2 mb-6">
        <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
          جاري فتح {linkInfo.appTitleAr} مباشرة...
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium">
          لحظة واحدة وسيتم تحويلك تلقائياً ⚡
        </p>
      </div>

      {targetLink && (
        <a
          href={targetLink}
          target={inIframe ? '_blank' : '_self'}
          rel="noopener noreferrer"
          className="text-xs text-amber-400/90 hover:text-amber-300 underline font-semibold transition py-2 px-4 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10"
        >
          إذا لم يفتح التطبيق تلقائياً، اضغط هنا للمتابعة
        </a>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/views/CardExpiredView.tsx

import { RefreshCw, MessageSquare, AlertCircle, Clock, ShieldAlert } from 'lucide-react';
import { QrCodeRecord, PlatformSettings } from '../types/qr';
import { PlatformBrandIcon } from '../components/BrandIcons';

interface CardExpiredViewProps {
  record: QrCodeRecord;
  settings: PlatformSettings;
}

export function CardExpiredView({ record, settings }: CardExpiredViewProps) {
  const formattedExpiry = record.expires_at
    ? new Date(record.expires_at).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', {
        year: 'numeric',
        month: 'long',
        day: 'numeric',
      })
    : 'غير محدد';

  // Format WhatsApp Link
  let cleanPhone = (settings.developer_whatsapp || '01550523365').trim();
  let contactUrl = settings.developer_contact_url || '';
  if (!contactUrl && cleanPhone) {
    let num = cleanPhone.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
    if (num.startsWith('01')) num = '2' + num;
    contactUrl = `https://wa.me/${num}`;
  }

  const renewalMessage = encodeURIComponent(
    `مرحباً، أود تجديد اشتراك الكارت الذكي رقم: (${record.id})`
  );
  const whatsappRenewalUrl = `${contactUrl}${contactUrl.includes('?') ? '&' : '?'}text=${renewalMessage}`;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-white flex flex-col justify-between items-center p-4 sm:p-6 select-none font-sans relative overflow-hidden" dir="rtl">
      {/* Ambient background glows */}
      <div className="absolute top-1/4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-96 h-96 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
      <div className="absolute bottom-10 right-10 w-72 h-72 bg-yellow-600/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

      {/* Header */}
      <header className="w-full max-w-md pt-4 pb-2 flex items-center justify-between z-10">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <img src="/logo.png" alt="Smart Card" className="w-8 h-8 object-contain rounded-lg border border-amber-400/40 p-0.5" />
          <span className="text-xs font-black tracking-wider text-amber-200/90 font-mono">Smart Card</span>
        </div>
        <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-rose-500/15 border border-rose-500/30 text-rose-400 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 animate-pulse">
          <ShieldAlert className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>اشتراك منتهي</span>
        </span>
      </header>

      {/* Central Content Card */}
      <main className="w-full max-w-md my-auto py-6 z-10">
        <div className="bg-[#121216]/95 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 text-center space-y-6 shadow-[0_15px_50px_rgba(0,0,0,0.8)] backdrop-blur-xl relative overflow-hidden">
          {/* Top highlight bar */}
          <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 h-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 via-rose-500 to-amber-500" />

          {/* Expired / Clock Icon */}
          <div className="relative mx-auto w-24 h-24 rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-rose-500/20 via-amber-500/15 to-transparent border border-rose-400/30 flex items-center justify-center shadow-inner">
            <Clock className="w-12 h-12 text-amber-400 stroke-[1.5]" />
            <div className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-4 h-4 bg-rose-500 rounded-full border-2 border-[#121216] flex items-center justify-center">
              <span className="text-[9px] text-white font-black">!</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Title & Description */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500">
              انتهت فترة اشتراك الكارت
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 leading-relaxed font-normal">
              لقد انتهت فترة الاشتراك المحددة لهذه البطاقة الذكية. لتجديد الاشتراك وإعادة تفعيل الرابط فوراً، يرجى التواصل مع الإدارة.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Card Info Box */}
          <div className="bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded-2xl p-4 text-right space-y-2.5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs pb-2 border-b border-white/5">
              <span className="text-slate-400 font-medium">رقم البطاقة الذكية:</span>
              <span className="font-mono font-black text-amber-400 text-sm tracking-wider">
                {record.id}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs pb-2 border-b border-white/5">
              <span className="text-slate-400 font-medium">تاريخ انتهاء الصلاحية:</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-rose-300">{formattedExpiry}</span>
            </div>

            {record.app_type && (
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                <span className="text-slate-400 font-medium">الوجهة المرتبطة:</span>
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-200 font-medium">
                  <PlatformBrandIcon type={record.app_type} className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>{record.app_type}</span>
                </span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Action Buttons */}
          <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
            {/* Primary WhatsApp Renewal Button */}
            <a
              href={whatsappRenewalUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="w-full py-4 px-6 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-500 to-amber-500 text-black font-black text-sm sm:text-base flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 shadow-[0_8px_25px_rgba(234,179,8,0.35)] hover:shadow-[0_12px_35px_rgba(234,179,8,0.5)] hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-[0.98] transition-all duration-200"
            >
              <MessageSquare className="w-5 h-5 fill-black" />
              <span>تجديد اشتراك الكارت عبر واتساب</span>
            </a>

            {/* Check Renewal / Reload */}
            <button
              onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
              className="w-full py-3 px-4 rounded-xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10 text-slate-300 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors"
            >
              <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>إذا تم التجديد اضغط للتحقق وإعادة التوجيه</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Contact Details Footnote */}
          <div className="pt-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
            <span>للدعم السريع:</span>
            <span className="text-amber-300 font-mono font-bold" dir="ltr">
              {settings.developer_whatsapp || '01550523365'}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="w-full max-w-md py-4 text-center text-[10px] text-slate-500 z-10 font-mono">
        Smart Card © {new Date().getFullYear()} — جميع الحقوق محفوظة
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: smart_card_admin_dart/lib/main.dart
