import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'screens/admin_dashboard_screen.dart';
import 'screens/admin_login_screen.dart';
import 'services/auth_service.dart';

void main() {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  runApp(const SmartCardAdminApp());
}

class SmartCardAdminApp extends StatefulWidget {
  const SmartCardAdminApp({super.key});

  @override
  State<SmartCardAdminApp> createState() => _SmartCardAdminAppState();
}

class _SmartCardAdminAppState extends State<SmartCardAdminApp> {
  bool _checkingAuth = true;
  bool _authenticated = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _checkSession();
  }

  Future<void> _checkSession() async {
    final authed = await AuthService.isAdminAuthenticated();
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _authenticated = authed;
      _checkingAuth = false;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MaterialApp(
      title: 'Smart Card Admin',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      theme: ThemeData(
        brightness: Brightness.dark,
        scaffoldBackgroundColor: const Color(0xFF09090B),
        colorScheme: ColorScheme.fromSeed(
          seedColor: const Color(0xFFF59E0B),
          brightness: Brightness.dark,
        ),
        useMaterial3: true,
      ),
      builder: (context, child) {
        return Directionality(
          textDirection: TextDirection.rtl,
          child: child ?? const SizedBox.shrink(),
        );
      },
      home: _checkingAuth
          ? const Scaffold(
              backgroundColor: Color(0xFF09090B),
              body: Center(
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Color(0xFFFBBF24)),
              ),
            )
          : _authenticated
              ? AdminDashboardScreen(
                  onLogout: () => setState(() => _authenticated = false),
                )
              : AdminLoginScreen(
                  onLoginSuccess: () => setState(() => _authenticated = true),
                ),
    );
  }
}

class WalletItem {
  final String phone;
  final WalletProvider provider;
  final String ownerName;

  const WalletItem({
    required this.phone,
    required this.provider,
    required this.ownerName,
  });

  factory WalletItem.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return WalletItem(
      phone: (json['phone'] ?? '').toString(),
      provider: WalletProvider.fromString(json['provider']?.toString()),
      ownerName: (json['ownerName'] ?? '').toString(),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'phone': phone.trim(),
        'provider': provider.name,
        'ownerName': ownerName.trim(),
      };
}

class BankDetails {
  final String bankName;
  final String accountNumber;
  final String accountName;
  final String iban;
  final String notes;

  const BankDetails({
    required this.bankName,
    required this.accountNumber,
    this.accountName = '',
    this.iban = '',
    this.notes = '',
  });
}

class QrCodeRecord {
  final String id;
  final bool isActive;
  final String? targetUrl;
  final AppType appType;
  final String label;
  final String createdAt;
  final String updatedAt;
  final String? lastScannedAt;
  final int totalScans;
  final String? expiresAt;
  final int subscriptionMonths;
  final List<WalletItem> wallets;
  final bool isPrinted;
  final String? printedAt;

  const QrCodeRecord({
    required this.id,
    required this.isActive,
    this.targetUrl,
    this.appType = AppType.website,
    this.label = '',
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    this.lastScannedAt,
    this.totalScans = 0,
    this.expiresAt,
    this.subscriptionMonths = 1,
    this.wallets = const [],
    this.isPrinted = false,
    this.printedAt,
  });

  bool get isExpired {
    if (expiresAt == null || expiresAt!.trim().isEmpty) return false;
    final parsed = DateTime.tryParse(expiresAt!);
    if (parsed == null) return false;
    return parsed.isBefore(DateTime.now());
  }

  factory QrCodeRecord.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    final rawWallets = json['wallets'];
    final parsedWallets = <WalletItem>[];
    if (rawWallets is List) {
      for (final item in rawWallets) {
        if (item is Map<String, dynamic>) {
          parsedWallets.add(WalletItem.fromJson(item));
        } else if (item is Map) {
          parsedWallets.add(WalletItem.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(item)));
        }
      }
    }

    return QrCodeRecord(
      id: (json['id'] ?? '').toString(),
      isActive: json['is_active'] == true,
      targetUrl: json['target_url']?.toString(),
      appType: AppType.fromString(json['app_type']?.toString()),
      label: (json['label'] ?? '').toString(),
      createdAt: (json['created_at'] ?? DateTime.now().toIso8601String()).toString(),
      updatedAt: (json['updated_at'] ?? json['created_at'] ?? DateTime.now().toIso8601String()).toString(),
      lastScannedAt: json['last_scanned_at']?.toString(),
      totalScans: (json['total_scans'] is num)
          ? (json['total_scans'] as num).toInt()
          : int.tryParse(json['total_scans']?.toString() ?? '0') ?? 0,
      expiresAt: json['expires_at']?.toString(),
      subscriptionMonths: (json['subscription_months'] is num)
          ? (json['subscription_months'] as num).toInt()
          : int.tryParse(json['subscription_months']?.toString() ?? '1') ?? 1,
      wallets: parsedWallets,
      isPrinted: json['is_printed'] == true,
      printedAt: json['printed_at']?.toString(),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    final map = <String, dynamic>{
      'id': id,
      'is_active': isActive,
      'target_url': targetUrl,
      'app_type': appType.name,
      'label': label,
      'created_at': createdAt,
      'updated_at': updatedAt,
      'total_scans': totalScans,
      'expires_at': expiresAt,
      'subscription_months': subscriptionMonths,
      'is_printed': isPrinted,
      'printed_at': printedAt,
    };
    if (lastScannedAt != null && lastScannedAt!.isNotEmpty) {
      map['last_scanned_at'] = lastScannedAt;
    }
    if (appType == AppType.wallet && wallets.isNotEmpty) {
      map['wallets'] = wallets.map((w) => w.toJson()).toList();
    }
    return map;
  }
}

  const PlatformSettings({

import '../models/qr_models.dart';

String normalizeArabicNumerals(String input) {
  const arabic = ['٠', '١', '٢', '٣', '٤', '٥', '٦', '٧', '٨', '٩'];
  var out = input;
  for (var i = 0; i < arabic.length; i++) {
    out = out.replaceAll(arabic[i], i.toString());
  }
  return out;
}

bool isSafeUrlScheme(String url) {
  if (url.trim().isEmpty) return false;
  final lower = url.trim().toLowerCase();
  return !RegExp(r'^(?:javascript|data|vbscript|file|blob|about):').hasMatch(lower);
}

bool isQuickPayUrl(String input) {
  final s = normalizeArabicNumerals(input).trim();
  if (s.isEmpty) return false;
  final lower = s.toLowerCase();
  if (lower.startsWith('http://') || lower.startsWith('https://')) return true;
  if (lower.startsWith('intent://') || lower.startsWith('android-app://') || lower.startsWith('android-app//')) {
    return true;
  }
  if (RegExp(r'^(vfcash|orangecash|etisalatcash|wepay|ipn|instapay)://', caseSensitive: false).hasMatch(lower)) {
    return true;
  }
  final hasDomain = lower.contains('.eg') ||
      lower.contains('.com') ||
      lower.contains('.net') ||
      lower.contains('.me') ||
      lower.contains('.io') ||
      lower.startsWith('www.') ||
      lower.contains('/');
  final digitsOnly = s.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^\d]'), '');
  return hasDomain && (s.contains('/') || lower.startsWith('www.') || digitsOnly.length < 8);
}

WalletProvider detectWalletProviderFromUrl(String url) {
  final raw = url.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (raw.isEmpty) return WalletProvider.generic;
  var lower = raw;
  try {
    lower = Uri.decodeFull(raw);
  } catch (_) {}

  if (lower.contains('ipn.eg') || lower.contains('instapay') || lower.startsWith('ipn://')) {
    return WalletProvider.instapay;
  }
  if (lower.contains('vodafone') ||
      lower.contains('voda') ||
      lower.contains('vf.eg') ||
      lower.contains('vfcash') ||
      lower.contains('anavodafone') ||
      lower.contains('anafodafone') ||
      lower.contains('com.vf')) {
    return WalletProvider.vodafone;
  }
  if (lower.contains('orange') || lower.contains('orangecash') || lower.contains('com.orange')) {
    return WalletProvider.orange;
  }
  if (lower.contains('etisalat') ||
      lower.contains('etisalatcash') ||
      lower.contains('e&') ||
      lower.contains('eand') ||
      lower.contains('e-and') ||
      lower.contains('com.etisalat')) {
    return WalletProvider.etisalat;
  }
  if (lower.contains('te.eg') ||
      lower.contains('telecom') ||
      lower.contains('wepay') ||
      lower.contains('we-pay') ||
      lower.contains('we.eg') ||
      RegExp(r'(?:^|[^a-z])we(?:pay|cash)?(?:[^a-z]|$)').hasMatch(lower)) {
    return WalletProvider.we;
  }
  return WalletProvider.generic;
}

class DetectedWalletInfo {
  final WalletProvider provider;
  final String cleanPhone;
  final String formattedPhone;

  const DetectedWalletInfo({
    required this.provider,
    required this.cleanPhone,
    required this.formattedPhone,
  });
}

DetectedWalletInfo detectWalletInfo(String rawPhone) {
  final normalized = normalizeArabicNumerals(rawPhone);
  var cleanPhone = normalized.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^\d]'), '');

  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('0020') && cleanPhone.length == 14) {
    cleanPhone = cleanPhone.substring(4);
  } else if (cleanPhone.startsWith('20') && cleanPhone.length == 12) {
    cleanPhone = cleanPhone.substring(2);
  }
  if (cleanPhone.length == 10 && cleanPhone.startsWith('1')) {
    cleanPhone = '0$cleanPhone';
  }

  var formattedPhone = cleanPhone;
  if (cleanPhone.length == 11) {
    formattedPhone = '${cleanPhone.substring(0, 3)} ${cleanPhone.substring(3, 7)} ${cleanPhone.substring(7)}';
  }

  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('010')) {
    return DetectedWalletInfo(provider: WalletProvider.vodafone, cleanPhone: cleanPhone, formattedPhone: formattedPhone);
  }
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('012')) {
    return DetectedWalletInfo(provider: WalletProvider.orange, cleanPhone: cleanPhone, formattedPhone: formattedPhone);
  }
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('011')) {
    return DetectedWalletInfo(provider: WalletProvider.etisalat, cleanPhone: cleanPhone, formattedPhone: formattedPhone);
  }
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('015')) {
    return DetectedWalletInfo(provider: WalletProvider.we, cleanPhone: cleanPhone, formattedPhone: formattedPhone);
  }
  return DetectedWalletInfo(
    provider: WalletProvider.generic,
    cleanPhone: cleanPhone.isNotEmpty ? cleanPhone : rawPhone.trim(),
    formattedPhone: cleanPhone.isNotEmpty ? cleanPhone : rawPhone.trim(),
  );
}

WalletProvider resolveWalletProvider(String input, [WalletProvider? explicit]) {
  final trimmed = input.trim();
  if (trimmed.isEmpty) return WalletProvider.generic;
  if (isQuickPayUrl(trimmed)) {
    final urlProv = detectWalletProviderFromUrl(trimmed);
    if (urlProv != WalletProvider.generic) return urlProv;
    if (explicit != null && explicit != WalletProvider.generic) return explicit;
    return WalletProvider.generic;
  }
  final phoneInfo = detectWalletInfo(trimmed);
  if (phoneInfo.provider != WalletProvider.generic) return phoneInfo.provider;
  if (explicit != null && explicit != WalletProvider.generic) return explicit;
  return WalletProvider.generic;
}

String sanitizeQuickPayUrl(String raw, [WalletProvider? provider]) {
  var s = raw.trim();
  if (s.isEmpty) return '';

  s = s.replaceFirst(RegExp(r'^https?:\/\/+android-app:?\/\/', caseSensitive: false), 'android-app://');
  s = s.replaceFirst(RegExp(r'^android-app\/\/', caseSensitive: false), 'android-app://');
  s = s.replaceFirst(RegExp(r'^https?:\/\/+(https?:\/\/)', caseSensitive: false), r'$1');

  final embeddedMatch = RegExp(r'https?:\/\/[^\s"<>]+', caseSensitive: false).firstMatch(s);
  if (embeddedMatch != null) {
    return embeddedMatch.group(0) ?? s;
  }

  if (s.toLowerCase().startsWith('instapay://') || s.toLowerCase().startsWith('ipn://')) {
    return s;
  }

  if (s.startsWith('android-app://')) {
    final pkg = s.replaceFirst('android-app://', '').split('/').first.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (pkg.contains('instapay') || provider == WalletProvider.instapay) {
      return 'https://ipn.eg';
    }
    if (pkg.contains('vf') || pkg.contains('vodafone') || provider == WalletProvider.vodafone) {
      return 'intent://#Intent;package=com.vf.eg;scheme=anafodafone;S.browser_fallback_url=https%3A%2F%2Fweb.vodafone.com.eg%2Far%2Fvodafone-cash;end';
    }
    if (pkg.contains('orange') || provider == WalletProvider.orange) {
      return 'intent://#Intent;package=com.orange.mail.egypt;scheme=orangecash;S.browser_fallback_url=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.orange.eg%2Far%2Forange-cash;end';
    }
    if (pkg.contains('etisalat') || provider == WalletProvider.etisalat) {
      return 'intent://#Intent;package=com.etisalat;scheme=etisalatcash;S.browser_fallback_url=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.etisalat.eg;end';
    }
    if (pkg.contains('we') || pkg.contains('telecom') || provider == WalletProvider.we) {
      return 'intent://#Intent;package=eg.telecom.we;scheme=wepay;S.browser_fallback_url=https%3A%2F%2Fte.eg;end';
    }
    if (pkg.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=$pkg';
    }
    return 'https://web.vodafone.com.eg/ar/vodafone-cash';
  }

  if (s.startsWith('intent://')) return s;
  if (!s.startsWith('http://') && !s.startsWith('https://')) {
    return 'https://$s';
  }
  return s;
}

String encodeBankDetails(BankDetails details) {
  final params = <String, String>{};
  if (details.bankName.isNotEmpty) params['b'] = details.bankName;
  if (details.accountNumber.isNotEmpty) params['acc'] = details.accountNumber;
  if (details.accountName.isNotEmpty) params['name'] = details.accountName;
  if (details.iban.isNotEmpty) params['iban'] = details.iban;
  if (details.notes.isNotEmpty) params['notes'] = details.notes;
  return Uri(scheme: 'bank', host: 'account', queryParameters: params).toString();
}

BankDetails? decodeBankDetails(String? uriStr) {
  if (uriStr == null || !uriStr.startsWith('bank://')) return null;
  try {
    final uri = Uri.parse(uriStr);
    return BankDetails(
      bankName: uri.queryParameters['b'] ?? 'البنك الأهلي المصري (NBE)',
      accountNumber: uri.queryParameters['acc'] ?? '',
      accountName: uri.queryParameters['name'] ?? '',
      iban: uri.queryParameters['iban'] ?? '',
      notes: uri.queryParameters['notes'] ?? '',
    );
  } catch (_) {
    return null;
  }
}

String _stripBidi(String input) {
  return input
      .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]'), '')
      .trim()
      .replaceAll(RegExp(r'^["\x27]|["\x27]$'), '')
      .trim();
}

String formatTikTokTargetUrl(String input) {
  final clean = _stripBidi(normalizeArabicNumerals(input));
  if (clean.isEmpty) return '';
  if (RegExp(r'^(?:https?:\/\/)?(?:vm|vt)\.tiktok\.com\/', caseSensitive: false).hasMatch(clean)) {
    return clean.startsWith('http') ? clean : 'https://$clean';
  }
  if (clean.contains('tiktok.com')) {
    final match = RegExp(r'tiktok\.com\/(?:@)?([a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+)', caseSensitive: false).firstMatch(clean);
    if (match != null && match.group(1) != null) {
      final seg = match.group(1)!.replaceFirst('@', '').toLowerCase();
      return 'https://www.tiktok.com/@$seg';
    }
    return clean.startsWith('http') ? clean : 'https://$clean';
  }
  final user = clean.replaceFirst(RegExp(r'^[/@]+'), '').split('?').first.split('/').first.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^\w.-]'), '').toLowerCase();
  return user.isNotEmpty ? 'https://www.tiktok.com/@$user' : clean;
}

String formatInstagramTargetUrl(String input) {
  final clean = _stripBidi(normalizeArabicNumerals(input));
  if (clean.isEmpty) return '';
  if (clean.contains('instagram.com/')) {
    final match = RegExp(r'instagram\.com\/([a-zA-Z0-9._]+)', caseSensitive: false).firstMatch(clean);
    if (match != null && match.group(1) != null) {
      return 'https://www.instagram.com/${match.group(1)!.toLowerCase()}/';
    }
    return clean.startsWith('http') ? clean : 'https://$clean';
  }
  final user = clean.replaceFirst(RegExp(r'^[/@]+'), '').split('?').first.split('/').first.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^\w.]'), '').toLowerCase();
  return user.isNotEmpty ? 'https://www.instagram.com/$user/' : clean;
}

String formatTelegramTargetUrl(String input) {
  final clean = _stripBidi(input);
  if (clean.isEmpty) return '';
  if (clean.contains('t.me/') || clean.contains('telegram.me/')) {
    final match = RegExp(r'(?:t\.me|telegram\.me)\/([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)', caseSensitive: false).firstMatch(clean);
    if (match != null && match.group(1) != null) {
      return 'https://t.me/${match.group(1)!.toLowerCase()}';
    }
    return clean.startsWith('http') ? clean : 'https://$clean';
  }
  final user = clean.replaceFirst(RegExp(r'^[/@]+'), '').split('?').first.split('/').first.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^\w]'), '').toLowerCase();
  return user.isNotEmpty ? 'https://t.me/$user' : clean;
}

String formatSnapchatTargetUrl(String input) {
  final clean = _stripBidi(input);
  if (clean.isEmpty) return '';
  if (clean.contains('snapchat.com/add/')) {
    final match = RegExp(r'snapchat\.com\/add\/([a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+)', caseSensitive: false).firstMatch(clean);
    if (match != null && match.group(1) != null) {
      return 'https://www.snapchat.com/add/${match.group(1)!.toLowerCase()}';
    }
    return clean.startsWith('http') ? clean : 'https://$clean';
  }
  final user = clean.replaceFirst(RegExp(r'^[/@]+'), '').split('?').first.split('/').first.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^\w.-]'), '').toLowerCase();
  return user.isNotEmpty ? 'https://www.snapchat.com/add/$user' : clean;
}

String formatWhatsappTargetUrl(String input) {
  final clean = _stripBidi(normalizeArabicNumerals(input));
  if (clean.isEmpty) return '';
  if (clean.startsWith('http://') || clean.startsWith('https://')) return clean;
  if (clean.toLowerCase().startsWith('wa.me/')) return 'https://$clean';
  var digits = clean.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^\d]'), '');
  if (digits.startsWith('00')) digits = digits.substring(2);
  if (digits.startsWith('01') && digits.length == 11) digits = '2$digits';
  if (digits.isNotEmpty) return 'https://wa.me/$digits';
  return clean;
}

String formatPhoneTargetUrl(String input) {
  final clean = _stripBidi(normalizeArabicNumerals(input));
  if (clean.isEmpty) return '';
  if (clean.startsWith('tel:')) return clean;
  final num = clean.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^\d+]'), '');
  return num.isNotEmpty ? 'tel:$num' : clean;
}

String? extractQrIdFromUrl(String rawText) {
  final trimmed = rawText.trim();
  if (trimmed.isEmpty) return null;

  final qMatch = RegExp(r'/q/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)').firstMatch(trimmed);
  if (qMatch != null) return qMatch.group(1);

  try {
    final uri = Uri.tryParse(trimmed);
    if (uri != null) {
      for (final key in ['activate', 'view', 'scan', 'expired']) {
        final v = uri.queryParameters[key];
        if (v != null && v.isNotEmpty) return v;
      }
    }
  } catch (_) {}

  if (RegExp(r'^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{5,24}$').hasMatch(trimmed)) {
    return trimmed;
  }
  return null;
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

class AuthService {
  static const String projectId = 'clean-plateau-dv4kj';
  static const String databaseId = 'ai-studio-41f04a06-1999-41b9-b7be-e25b21dc1fd0';
  static const String apiKey = 'AIzaSyAjyCP484AEQ52-9CDgp8TDkAw5woA1TFc';

  // SHA-256 of default password "admin1234"
  static const String defaultBaseHash = 'ac9689e2272427085e35b9d3e3e8bed88cb3434828b43b86fc0596cad4c6e270';

  static const String _sessionKey = 'smart_card_admin_session_active';
  static const String _sessionExpiryKey = 'smart_card_admin_session_expiry';
  static const String _cachedHashKey = 'smart_card_admin_pwd_hash';

  static String hashPassword(String plain) {
    final bytes = utf8.encode(plain);
    return sha256.convert(bytes).toString();
  }

  static Future<bool> isAdminAuthenticated() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final active = prefs.getBool(_sessionKey) ?? false;
    if (!active) return false;
    final expiry = prefs.getInt(_sessionExpiryKey) ?? 0;
    if (expiry > 0 && DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch > expiry) {
      await logoutAdmin();
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  static Future<String> getActivePasswordHash() async {
    try {
      final url = Uri.parse(
        'https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/$projectId/databases/$databaseId/documents/settings/auth?key=$apiKey',
      );
      final res = await http.get(url).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 6));
      if (res.statusCode == 200) {
        final data = jsonDecode(res.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
        final hashVal = data['fields']?['hash']?['stringValue']?.toString();
        if (hashVal != null && hashVal.length == 64) {
          final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
          await prefs.setString(_cachedHashKey, hashVal);
          return hashVal;
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final cached = prefs.getString(_cachedHashKey);
    if (cached != null && cached.length == 64) {
      return cached;
    }
    return defaultBaseHash;
  }

  static Future<bool> loginAdmin(String passwordAttempt) async {
    final clean = passwordAttempt.trim();
    if (clean.isEmpty) return false;

    final attemptHash = hashPassword(clean);
    final activeHash = await getActivePasswordHash();

    if (attemptHash == activeHash) {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      await prefs.setBool(_sessionKey, true);
      await prefs.setInt(
        _sessionExpiryKey,
        DateTime.now().add(const Duration(days: 7)).millisecondsSinceEpoch,
      );
      await prefs.setString(_cachedHashKey, attemptHash);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  static Future<({bool success, String message})> changeAdminPassword(
    String currentPassword,
    String newPassword,
  ) async {
    final cur = currentPassword.trim();
    final next = newPassword.trim();

    if (cur.isEmpty) {
      return (success: false, message: 'الرجاء إدخال كلمة المرور الحالية.');
    }
    if (next.length < 4) {
      return (success: false, message: 'كلمة المرور الجديدة يجب ألا تقل عن 4 أحرف أو أرقام.');
    }

    final curHash = hashPassword(cur);
    final activeHash = await getActivePasswordHash();
    if (curHash != activeHash) {
      return (success: false, message: 'كلمة المرور الحالية غير صحيحة.');
    }

    final newHash = hashPassword(next);
    final nowIso = DateTime.now().toUtc().toIso8601String();

    final payload = jsonEncode({
      'fields': {
        'hash': {'stringValue': newHash},
        'updated_at': {'stringValue': nowIso},
        'migrated': {'booleanValue': true},
      }
    });

    try {
      final authDocUrl = Uri.parse(
        'https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/$projectId/databases/$databaseId/documents/settings/auth?key=$apiKey',
      );
      final res = await http
          .patch(authDocUrl, headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, body: payload)
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 8));
      if (res.statusCode < 200 || res.statusCode >= 300) {
        return (success: false, message: 'تعذر حفظ كلمة المرور في قاعدة البيانات (${res.statusCode}).');
      }

      // Mirror to platform_settings/auth
      final mirrorUrl = Uri.parse(
        'https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/$projectId/databases/$databaseId/documents/platform_settings/auth?key=$apiKey',
      );
      await http.patch(mirrorUrl, headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, body: payload).catchError((_) => res);
    } catch (e) {
      return (success: false, message: 'حدث خطأ أثناء الاتصال بالسيرفر: $e');
    }

    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setString(_cachedHashKey, newHash);

    return (
      success: true,
      message: 'تم تحديث كلمة مرور الأدمن في قاعدة البيانات السحابية بنجاح!',
    );
  }

  static Future<void> logoutAdmin() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.remove(_sessionKey);
    await prefs.remove(_sessionExpiryKey);
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: smart_card_admin_dart/lib/services/firestore_service.dart

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import '../models/qr_models.dart';
import '../utils/deep_links.dart';
import 'auth_service.dart';

class FirestoreService {
  static String get _baseDocUrl =>
      'https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${AuthService.projectId}/databases/${AuthService.databaseId}/documents';

  static String get _apiKey => AuthService.apiKey;
  static const String _cacheKey = 'smart_card_admin_qr_cache_v1';
  static const String _settingsCacheKey = 'smart_card_admin_settings_v1';

  // ---- Firestore REST Value Encoding / Decoding ----

  static dynamic _parseFirestoreValue(dynamic val) {
    if (val is! Map) return null;
    if (val.containsKey('stringValue')) return val['stringValue'];
    if (val.containsKey('booleanValue')) return val['booleanValue'];
    if (val.containsKey('integerValue')) return int.tryParse(val['integerValue'].toString()) ?? 0;
    if (val.containsKey('doubleValue')) return (val['doubleValue'] as num).toDouble();
    if (val.containsKey('nullValue')) return null;
    if (val.containsKey('timestampValue')) return val['timestampValue'];
    if (val.containsKey('arrayValue')) {
      final list = (val['arrayValue']?['values'] as List?) ?? [];
      return list.map(_parseFirestoreValue).where((e) => e != null).toList();
    }
    if (val.containsKey('mapValue')) {
      final fields = (val['mapValue']?['fields'] as Map?) ?? {};
      final out = <String, dynamic>{};
      fields.forEach((k, v) {
        out[k.toString()] = _parseFirestoreValue(v);
      });
      return out;
    }
    return null;
  }

  static QrCodeRecord? _docToQrRecord(Map<String, dynamic>? doc) {
    if (doc == null || doc['fields'] is! Map) return null;
    final name = (doc['name'] ?? '').toString();
    final docId = name.contains('/') ? name.split('/').last : '';
    final fields = Map<String, dynamic>.from(doc['fields'] as Map);
    final parsed = <String, dynamic>{};
    fields.forEach((k, v) {
      parsed[k] = _parseFirestoreValue(v);
    });
    final id = (parsed['id'] ?? docId).toString();
    if (id.isEmpty || id == 'undefined' || id == 'null') return null;
    parsed['id'] = id;
    return QrCodeRecord.fromJson(parsed);
  }

  static Map<String, dynamic> _toFirestoreValue(dynamic value) {
    if (value == null) return {'nullValue': null};
    if (value is bool) return {'booleanValue': value};
    if (value is int) return {'integerValue': value.toString()};
    if (value is double) return {'doubleValue': value};
    if (value is List) {
      return {
        'arrayValue': {
          'values': value.map(_toFirestoreValue).toList(),
        }
      };
    }
    if (value is Map) {
      final fields = <String, dynamic>{};
      value.forEach((k, v) {
        fields[k.toString()] = _toFirestoreValue(v);
      });
      return {
        'mapValue': {'fields': fields}
      };
    }
    return {'stringValue': value.toString()};
  }

  static Map<String, dynamic> _recordToFirestoreFields(QrCodeRecord record) {
    final json = record.toJson();
    final fields = <String, dynamic>{};
    json.forEach((k, v) {
      fields[k] = _toFirestoreValue(v);
    });
    return fields;
  }

  // ---- Local Cache ----

  static Future<List<QrCodeRecord>> _loadCache() async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final raw = prefs.getString(_cacheKey);
      if (raw == null || raw.isEmpty) return [];
      final list = jsonDecode(raw) as List;
      return list
          .whereType<Map>()
          .map((m) => QrCodeRecord.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(m)))
          .toList();
    } catch (_) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  static Future<void> _saveCache(List<QrCodeRecord> records) async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      await prefs.setString(
        _cacheKey,
        jsonEncode(records.map((r) => r.toJson()).toList()),
      );
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // ---- Core Operations ----

  static Future<List<QrCodeRecord>> fetchAllQrCodes() async {
    try {
      final url = Uri.parse('$_baseDocUrl:runQuery?key=$_apiKey');
      final res = await http
          .post(
            url,
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: jsonEncode({
              'structuredQuery': {
                'from': [
                  {'collectionId': 'qr_codes'}
                ],
              }
            }),
          )
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 10));

      if (res.statusCode == 200) {
        final rows = jsonDecode(res.body) as List;
        final records = <QrCodeRecord>[];
        for (final row in rows) {
          if (row is Map && row['document'] is Map) {
            final rec = _docToQrRecord(Map<String, dynamic>.from(row['document'] as Map));
            if (rec != null) records.add(rec);
          }
        }
        records.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.compareTo(a.createdAt));
        await _saveCache(records);
        return records;
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    return _loadCache();
  }

  static Future<QrCodeRecord?> getQrCode(String id) async {
    final cleanId = id.trim();
    if (cleanId.isEmpty) return null;

    try {
      final url = Uri.parse('$_baseDocUrl/qr_codes/${Uri.encodeComponent(cleanId)}?key=$_apiKey');
      final res = await http.get(url).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 8));
      if (res.statusCode == 200) {
        final doc = jsonDecode(res.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
        final rec = _docToQrRecord(doc);
        if (rec != null) return rec;
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    final cached = await _loadCache();
    for (final item in cached) {
      if (item.id == cleanId) return item;
    }
    return null;
  }

  static String _generateNanoid8() {
    const alphabet = '0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz_-';
    final rand = Random.secure();
    return List.generate(8, (_) => alphabet[rand.nextInt(alphabet.length)]).join();
  }

  static Future<List<QrCodeRecord>> createBulkQrCodes(int count, int subscriptionMonths) async {
    final safeCount = count.clamp(1, 50);
    final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    final nowIso = now.toIso8601String();
    String? expiresAt;
    if (subscriptionMonths > 0 && subscriptionMonths < 900) {
      expiresAt = DateTime.utc(now.year, now.month + subscriptionMonths, now.day, now.hour, now.minute, now.second)
          .toIso8601String();
    }

    final created = <QrCodeRecord>[];
    for (var i = 0; i < safeCount; i++) {
      final id = _generateNanoid8();
      final record = QrCodeRecord(
        id: id,
        isActive: false,
        targetUrl: null,
        appType: AppType.website,
        label: '',
        createdAt: nowIso,
        updatedAt: nowIso,
        totalScans: 0,
        expiresAt: expiresAt,
        subscriptionMonths: subscriptionMonths,
        isPrinted: false,
        printedAt: null,
      );

      final url = Uri.parse('$_baseDocUrl/qr_codes/${Uri.encodeComponent(id)}?key=$_apiKey');
      final res = await http
          .patch(
            url,
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: jsonEncode({'fields': _recordToFirestoreFields(record)}),
          )
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 8));
      if (res.statusCode >= 200 && res.statusCode < 300) {
        created.add(record);
      }
    }

    return created;
  }

  /// Full canonical update: permanently overwrites the Firestore document without updateMask
  /// so any old link, old wallets array, or old platform fields are erased cleanly.
  static Future<QrCodeRecord?> updateQrCode({
    required QrCodeRecord existing,
    required AppType appType,
    required String? targetUrl,
    required String label,
    List<WalletItem>? wallets,
    String? expiresAt,
    int? subscriptionMonths,
    bool forceActive = true,
  }) async {
    final nowIso = DateTime.now().toUtc().toIso8601String();
    final cleanTarget = targetUrl?.trim();
    final hasValidTarget = cleanTarget != null && cleanTarget.isNotEmpty;
    final cleanWallets = (appType == AppType.wallet && wallets != null)
        ? wallets.where((w) => w.phone.trim().isNotEmpty).map((w) {
            final trimmedPhone = w.phone.trim();
            final resolvedProv = resolveWalletProvider(trimmedPhone, w.provider);
            final normalizedPhone = isQuickPayUrl(trimmedPhone)
                ? sanitizeQuickPayUrl(trimmedPhone, resolvedProv)
                : detectWalletInfo(trimmedPhone).cleanPhone;
            return WalletItem(
              phone: normalizedPhone,
              provider: resolvedProv,
              ownerName: w.ownerName.trim(),
            );
          }).toList()
        : <WalletItem>[];

    final effectiveActive = appType == AppType.wallet
        ? cleanWallets.isNotEmpty && forceActive
        : (hasValidTarget && forceActive);

    final effectiveTarget = appType == AppType.wallet
        ? (cleanWallets.isNotEmpty ? cleanWallets.first.phone : null)
        : (hasValidTarget ? cleanTarget : null);

    final updated = QrCodeRecord(
      id: existing.id,
      isActive: effectiveActive,
      targetUrl: effectiveTarget,
      appType: appType,
      label: label.trim(),
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: nowIso,
      lastScannedAt: existing.lastScannedAt,
      totalScans: existing.totalScans,
      expiresAt: expiresAt ?? existing.expiresAt,
      subscriptionMonths: subscriptionMonths ?? existing.subscriptionMonths,
      wallets: appType == AppType.wallet ? cleanWallets : const [],
      isPrinted: existing.isPrinted,
      printedAt: existing.printedAt,
    );

    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseDocUrl/qr_codes/${Uri.encodeComponent(existing.id)}?key=$_apiKey');
    final res = await http
        .patch(
          url,
          headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
          body: jsonEncode({'fields': _recordToFirestoreFields(updated)}),
        )
        .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 8));

    if (res.statusCode >= 200 && res.statusCode < 300) {
      return updated;
    }
    return null;
  }

  /// Permanently purges a deleted link/wallet target from Firestore immediately
  static Future<QrCodeRecord?> purgeQrTarget({
    required QrCodeRecord existing,
    required AppType appType,
    List<WalletItem>? remainingWallets,
    String? label,
  }) async {
    return updateQrCode(
      existing: existing,
      appType: appType,
      targetUrl: null,
      label: label ?? existing.label,
      wallets: remainingWallets ?? const [],
      forceActive: appType == AppType.wallet && (remainingWallets?.any((w) => w.phone.trim().isNotEmpty) ?? false),
    );
  }

  static Future<bool> resetQrScanCount(QrCodeRecord existing) async {
    final nowIso = DateTime.now().toUtc().toIso8601String();
    final updated = QrCodeRecord(
      id: existing.id,
      isActive: existing.isActive,
      targetUrl: existing.targetUrl,
      appType: existing.appType,
      label: existing.label,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: nowIso,
      lastScannedAt: existing.lastScannedAt,
      totalScans: 0,
      expiresAt: existing.expiresAt,
      subscriptionMonths: existing.subscriptionMonths,
      wallets: existing.wallets,
      isPrinted: existing.isPrinted,
      printedAt: existing.printedAt,
    );
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseDocUrl/qr_codes/${Uri.encodeComponent(existing.id)}?key=$_apiKey');
    final res = await http
        .patch(
          url,
          headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
          body: jsonEncode({'fields': _recordToFirestoreFields(updated)}),
        )
        .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 8));
    return res.statusCode >= 200 && res.statusCode < 300;
  }

  /// Admin-Only: Cancels mistaken activation, wipes target_url, wallets, label, scans, and app_type,
  /// and restores the QR card back to its fresh unactivated state (is_active: false).
  static Future<QrCodeRecord?> deactivateQrCode(QrCodeRecord existing) async {
    final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    final nowIso = now.toIso8601String();
    String? restoredExpiresAt = existing.expiresAt;
    if (existing.isExpired) {
      final months = existing.subscriptionMonths;
      if (months > 0 && months < 900) {
        restoredExpiresAt = DateTime.utc(now.year, now.month + months, now.day, now.hour, now.minute, now.second)
            .toIso8601String();
      } else {
        restoredExpiresAt = null;
      }
    }

    final resetRecord = QrCodeRecord(
      id: existing.id,
      isActive: false,
      targetUrl: null,
      appType: AppType.website,
      label: '',
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: nowIso,
      lastScannedAt: null,
      totalScans: 0,
      expiresAt: restoredExpiresAt,
      subscriptionMonths: existing.subscriptionMonths,
      wallets: const [],
      isPrinted: existing.isPrinted,
      printedAt: existing.printedAt,
    );

    // Explicit canonical inactive Firestore payload omitting app_type, wallets, last_scanned_at
    final fields = <String, dynamic>{
      'id': _toFirestoreValue(resetRecord.id),
      'is_active': _toFirestoreValue(false),
      'target_url': _toFirestoreValue(null),
      'label': _toFirestoreValue(''),
      'created_at': _toFirestoreValue(resetRecord.createdAt),
      'updated_at': _toFirestoreValue(nowIso),
      'total_scans': _toFirestoreValue(0),
      'expires_at': _toFirestoreValue(restoredExpiresAt),
      'subscription_months': _toFirestoreValue(resetRecord.subscriptionMonths),
      'is_printed': _toFirestoreValue(resetRecord.isPrinted),
      'printed_at': _toFirestoreValue(resetRecord.printedAt),
    };

    try {
      final url = Uri.parse('$_baseDocUrl/qr_codes/${Uri.encodeComponent(existing.id)}?key=$_apiKey');
      final res = await http
          .patch(
            url,
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: jsonEncode({'fields': fields}),
          )
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 8));
      if (res.statusCode >= 200 && res.statusCode < 300) {
        final cached = await _loadCache();
        final nextCache = cached.map((r) => r.id == resetRecord.id ? resetRecord : r).toList();
        await _saveCache(nextCache);
        return resetRecord;
      }
    } catch (_) {}
    return null;
  }

  static Future<QrCodeRecord?> toggleQrPrintedStatus(QrCodeRecord existing, bool isPrinted) async {
    final nowIso = DateTime.now().toUtc().toIso8601String();
    final updated = QrCodeRecord(
      id: existing.id,
      isActive: existing.isActive,
      targetUrl: existing.targetUrl,
      appType: existing.appType,
      label: existing.label,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: nowIso,
      lastScannedAt: existing.lastScannedAt,
      totalScans: existing.totalScans,
      expiresAt: existing.expiresAt,
      subscriptionMonths: existing.subscriptionMonths,
      wallets: existing.wallets,
      isPrinted: isPrinted,
      printedAt: isPrinted ? nowIso : null,
    );

    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseDocUrl/qr_codes/${Uri.encodeComponent(existing.id)}?key=$_apiKey');
    final res = await http
        .patch(
          url,
          headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
          body: jsonEncode({'fields': _recordToFirestoreFields(updated)}),
        )
        .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 8));
    return (res.statusCode >= 200 && res.statusCode < 300) ? updated : null;
  }

  static Future<QrCodeRecord?> renewQrSubscription(QrCodeRecord existing, int months) async {
    final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    String? newExpiresAt;
    if (months > 0 && months < 900) {
      final base = (existing.expiresAt != null && !existing.isExpired)
          ? (DateTime.tryParse(existing.expiresAt!) ?? now)
          : now;
      newExpiresAt = DateTime.utc(base.year, base.month + months, base.day, base.hour, base.minute, base.second)
          .toIso8601String();
    } else {
      newExpiresAt = null;
    }

    final updated = QrCodeRecord(
      id: existing.id,
      isActive: existing.isActive,
      targetUrl: existing.targetUrl,
      appType: existing.appType,
      label: existing.label,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now.toIso8601String(),
      lastScannedAt: existing.lastScannedAt,
      totalScans: existing.totalScans,
      expiresAt: newExpiresAt,
      subscriptionMonths: months,
      wallets: existing.wallets,
      isPrinted: existing.isPrinted,
      printedAt: existing.printedAt,
    );

    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseDocUrl/qr_codes/${Uri.encodeComponent(existing.id)}?key=$_apiKey');
    final res = await http
        .patch(
          url,
          headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
          body: jsonEncode({'fields': _recordToFirestoreFields(updated)}),
        )
        .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 8));
    return (res.statusCode >= 200 && res.statusCode < 300) ? updated : null;
  }

  static Future<bool> deleteQrCode(String id) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseDocUrl/qr_codes/${Uri.encodeComponent(id)}?key=$_apiKey');
    final res = await http.delete(url).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 8));
    return res.statusCode >= 200 && res.statusCode < 300;
  }

  static Future<PlatformSettings> fetchPlatformSettings() async {
    try {
      final url = Uri.parse('$_baseDocUrl/settings/platform?key=$_apiKey');
      final res = await http.get(url).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 6));
      if (res.statusCode == 200) {
        final doc = jsonDecode(res.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
        final fields = (doc['fields'] as Map?) ?? {};
        final parsed = <String, dynamic>{};
        fields.forEach((k, v) {
          parsed[k.toString()] = _parseFirestoreValue(v);
        });
        final settings = PlatformSettings.fromJson(parsed);
        final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
        await prefs.setString(_settingsCacheKey, jsonEncode(settings.toJson()));
        return settings;
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final raw = prefs.getString(_settingsCacheKey);
      if (raw != null && raw.isNotEmpty) {
        return PlatformSettings.fromJson(jsonDecode(raw) as Map<String, dynamic>);
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    return const PlatformSettings();
  }

  static Future<bool> updatePlatformSettings(PlatformSettings settings) async {
    try {
      final url = Uri.parse('$_baseDocUrl/settings/platform?key=$_apiKey');
      final fields = <String, dynamic>{};
      settings.toJson().forEach((k, v) {
        fields[k] = _toFirestoreValue(v);
      });
      final res = await http
          .patch(
            url,
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: jsonEncode({'fields': fields}),
          )
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 8));
      if (res.statusCode >= 200 && res.statusCode < 300) {
        final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
        await prefs.setString(_settingsCacheKey, jsonEncode(settings.toJson()));
        return true;
      }
    } catch (_) {}
    return false;
  }
}

import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// 3D Floating ambient logo with continuous perspective tilt
class Floating3DLogo extends StatefulWidget {
  final double size;
  final Widget child;

  const Floating3DLogo({
    super.key,
    required this.size,
    required this.child,
  });

  @override
  State<Floating3DLogo> createState() => _Floating3DLogoState();
}

class _Floating3DLogoState extends State<Floating3DLogo> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final AnimationController _controller;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _controller = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 4200),
    )..repeat();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: _controller,
      builder: (context, child) {
        final t = _controller.value * 2 * math.pi;
        final dy = math.sin(t) * 4.5;
        final rotY = math.cos(t) * 0.08;
        final rotX = math.sin(t) * 0.05;

        return Transform.translate(
          offset: Offset(0, dy),
          child: Transform(
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            transform: Matrix4.identity()
              ..setEntry(3, 2, 0.0018)
              ..rotateX(rotX)
              ..rotateY(rotY),
            child: Container(
              width: widget.size,
              height: widget.size,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(widget.size * 0.25),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: const Color(0xFFF59E0B).withValues(alpha: 0.28 + 0.1 * math.sin(t)),
                    blurRadius: 22,
                    spreadRadius: 1,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              child: child,
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
      child: widget.child,
    );
  }
}

/// 3D Card entrance animation
class Card3DEnter extends StatefulWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final Duration duration;

  const Card3DEnter({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.duration = const Duration(milliseconds: 480),
  });

  @override
  State<Card3DEnter> createState() => _Card3DEnterState();
}

class _Card3DEnterState extends State<Card3DEnter> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final AnimationController _ctrl;
  late final Animation<double> _anim;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _ctrl = AnimationController(vsync: this, duration: widget.duration);
    _anim = CurvedAnimation(parent: _ctrl, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic);
    _ctrl.forward();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _ctrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: _anim,
      builder: (context, child) {
        final v = _anim.value;
        final rotX = (1.0 - v) * 0.18;
        final dy = (1.0 - v) * 18.0;
        final scale = 0.96 + 0.04 * v;

        return Opacity(
          opacity: v,
          child: Transform.translate(
            offset: Offset(0, dy),
            child: Transform(
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              transform: Matrix4.identity()
                ..setEntry(3, 2, 0.0014)
                ..rotateX(rotX)
                ..scale(scale),
              child: child,
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
      child: widget.child,
    );
  }
}

/// Interactive 3D touch-tilt card for Admin Dashboard KPIs & QR Cards
class Tilt3DCard extends StatefulWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;
  final Color borderColor;
  final Color backgroundColor;
  final double borderRadius;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;

  const Tilt3DCard({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.onTap,
    this.borderColor = const Color(0x4DF59E0B),
    this.backgroundColor = const Color(0xFF121216),
    this.borderRadius = 20,
    this.padding = const EdgeInsets.all(14),
  });

  @override
  State<Tilt3DCard> createState() => _Tilt3DCardState();
}

class _Tilt3DCardState extends State<Tilt3DCard> {
  bool _pressed = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTapDown: (_) => setState(() => _pressed = true),
      onTapUp: (_) => setState(() => _pressed = false),
      onTapCancel: () => setState(() => _pressed = false),
      onTap: widget.onTap,
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 180),
        curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
        transformAlignment: Alignment.center,
        transform: _pressed
            ? (Matrix4.identity()
              ..setEntry(3, 2, 0.0015)
              ..rotateX(0.05)
              ..scale(0.975))
            : Matrix4.identity(),
        padding: widget.padding,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: widget.backgroundColor,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(widget.borderRadius),
          border: Border.all(
            color: _pressed ? const Color(0xFFFBBF24) : widget.borderColor,
            width: 1.2,
          ),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: const Color(0xFFF59E0B).withValues(alpha: _pressed ? 0.2 : 0.07),
              blurRadius: _pressed ? 20 : 12,
              offset: const Offset(0, 6),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: widget.child,
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 3D Platform selection card with living icon animation when selected
class PlatformTile3D extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool selected;
  final IconData icon;
  final Color iconColor;
  final String label;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const PlatformTile3D({
    super.key,
    required this.selected,
    required this.icon,
    required this.iconColor,
    required this.label,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  State<PlatformTile3D> createState() => _PlatformTile3DState();
}

class _PlatformTile3DState extends State<PlatformTile3D> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final AnimationController _pulseCtrl;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _pulseCtrl = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 2400),
    );
    if (widget.selected) {
      _pulseCtrl.repeat(reverse: true);
    }
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant PlatformTile3D oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (widget.selected && !_pulseCtrl.isAnimating) {
      _pulseCtrl.repeat(reverse: true);
    } else if (!widget.selected && _pulseCtrl.isAnimating) {
      _pulseCtrl.stop();
      _pulseCtrl.value = 0;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _pulseCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: widget.onTap,
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),
        curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
        transformAlignment: Alignment.center,
        transform: widget.selected
            ? (Matrix4.identity()
              ..setEntry(3, 2, 0.002)
              ..rotateX(-0.08)
              ..translate(0.0, -3.0)
              ..scale(1.03))
            : Matrix4.identity(),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12, horizontal: 8),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          gradient: widget.selected
              ? const LinearGradient(
                  begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                  end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                  colors: [Color(0x33F59E0B), Color(0xFF09090B)],
                )
              : null,
          color: widget.selected ? null : const Color(0xB318181B),
          border: Border.all(
            color: widget.selected ? const Color(0xFFFBBF24) : Colors.white12,
            width: widget.selected ? 1.5 : 1,
          ),
          boxShadow: widget.selected
              ? [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: const Color(0xFFF59E0B).withValues(alpha: 0.3),
                    blurRadius: 16,
                    offset: const Offset(0, 6),
                  ),
                ]
              : [],
        ),
        child: Stack(
          alignment: Alignment.center,
          children: [
            if (widget.selected)
              const Positioned(
                top: 0,
                left: 0,
                child: CircleAvatar(
                  radius: 8,
                  backgroundColor: Color(0xFFFBBF24),
                  child: Icon(Icons.check, size: 11, color: Colors.black),
                ),
              ),
            Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                AnimatedBuilder(
                  animation: _pulseCtrl,
                  builder: (context, child) {
                    final v = widget.selected ? _pulseCtrl.value : 0.0;
                    final rotY = widget.selected ? (v - 0.5) * 0.25 : 0.0;
                    final sc = widget.selected ? 1.08 + v * 0.1 : 1.0;
                    return Transform(
                      alignment: Alignment.center,
                      transform: Matrix4.identity()
                        ..setEntry(3, 2, 0.002)
                        ..rotateY(rotY)
                        ..scale(sc),
                      child: child,
                    );
                  },
                  child: Container(
                    width: 38,
                    height: 38,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: widget.iconColor.withValues(alpha: 0.16),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
                      border: Border.all(color: widget.iconColor.withValues(alpha: 0.4)),
                    ),
                    child: Icon(widget.icon, color: widget.iconColor, size: 22),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  widget.label,
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 11.5,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                    color: widget.selected ? const Color(0xFFFCD34D) : Colors.white70,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class AdminLoginScreen extends StatefulWidget {

class _AdminLoginScreenState extends State<AdminLoginScreen> {

  bool _loading = false;
  String? _error;

    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();

    if (_controller.text.trim().isEmpty || _loading) return;

      _loading = true;
      _error = null;

        _loading = false;
        _error = 'كلمة المرور غير صحيحة. يرجى التحقق والمحاولة مرة أخرى.';

                      ),

                        onPressed: _loading ? null : _submit,
                        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(

FILE: smart_card_admin_dart/lib/screens/admin_dashboard_screen.dart
