import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:qr_flutter/qr_flutter.dart';
import '../models/qr_models.dart';
import '../services/auth_service.dart';
import '../services/firestore_service.dart';
import '../utils/deep_links.dart';
import '../widgets/three_d_widgets.dart';
import 'edit_qr_sheet.dart';
import 'qr_scanner_screen.dart';
import 'settings_screen.dart';

enum _QrFilter { all, active, inactive, expired, printed, unprinted }

class AdminDashboardScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final VoidCallback onLogout;

  const AdminDashboardScreen({super.key, required this.onLogout});

  @override
  State<AdminDashboardScreen> createState() => _AdminDashboardScreenState();
}

class _AdminDashboardScreenState extends State<AdminDashboardScreen> {
  List<QrCodeRecord> _records = [];
  PlatformSettings _settings = const PlatformSettings();
  bool _loading = true;
  bool _generating = false;
  String _searchQuery = '';
  _QrFilter _filter = _QrFilter.all;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadData();
  }

  Future<void> _loadData() async {
    setState(() => _loading = true);
    final results = await Future.wait([
      FirestoreService.fetchAllQrCodes(),
      FirestoreService.fetchPlatformSettings(),
    ]);
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _records = results[0] as List<QrCodeRecord>;
      _settings = results[1] as PlatformSettings;
      _loading = false;
    });
  }

  String get _domainUrl {
    final d = _settings.publicDomainUrl.trim();
    return d.isNotEmpty ? d : 'https://smart-card-inky.vercel.app';
  }

  List<QrCodeRecord> get _filteredRecords {
    final q = _searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    return _records.where((r) {
      if (_filter == _QrFilter.active && (!r.isActive || r.isExpired)) return false;
      if (_filter == _QrFilter.inactive && r.isActive) return false;
      if (_filter == _QrFilter.expired && !r.isExpired) return false;
      if (_filter == _QrFilter.printed && !r.isPrinted) return false;
      if (_filter == _QrFilter.unprinted && r.isPrinted) return false;

      if (q.isEmpty) return true;
      if (r.id.toLowerCase().contains(q)) return true;
      if (r.label.toLowerCase().contains(q)) return true;
      if ((r.targetUrl ?? '').toLowerCase().contains(q)) return true;
      if (r.appType.labelAr.toLowerCase().contains(q)) return true;
      for (final w in r.wallets) {
        if (w.phone.toLowerCase().contains(q) || w.ownerName.toLowerCase().contains(q)) return true;
      }
      return false;
    }).toList();
  }

  Future<void> _openScanner() async {
    final scannedId = await Navigator.of(context).push<String>(
      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const QrScannerScreen()),
    );
    if (!mounted || scannedId == null || scannedId.isEmpty) return;

    QrCodeRecord? found;
    for (final r in _records) {
      if (r.id == scannedId) {
        found = r;
        break;
      }
    }
    found ??= await FirestoreService.getQrCode(scannedId);
    if (!mounted) return;

    if (found == null) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFEF4444),
          content: Text('الرمز "$scannedId" غير موجود في قاعدة البيانات.'),
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    _openEditScreen(found);
  }

  Future<void> _openBulkGenerateDialog() async {
    int count = 5;
    int months = 1;

    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (ctx, setModalState) => AlertDialog(
          backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF121216),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
            side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
          ),
          title: const Text(
            'توليد أكواد QR جديدة',
            style: TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFFBBF24), fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 16),
          ),
          content: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              const Text('عدد البطاقات المطلوبة:', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12)),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Wrap(
                spacing: 8,
                children: [1, 5, 10, 20].map((c) {
                  final sel = count == c;
                  return ChoiceChip(
                    selected: sel,
                    selectedColor: const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
                    backgroundColor: Colors.black54,
                    label: Text('$c', style: TextStyle(color: sel ? Colors.black : Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
                    onSelected: (_) => setModalState(() => count = c),
                  );
                }).toList(),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              const Text('مدة الصلاحية الافتراضية:', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12)),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Wrap(
                spacing: 8,
                runSpacing: 6,
                children: [
                  (1, 'شهر'),
                  (3, '3 شهور'),
                  (6, '6 شهور'),
                  (12, 'سنة'),
                  (999, 'دائم'),
                ].map((opt) {
                  final sel = months == opt.$1;
                  return ChoiceChip(
                    selected: sel,
                    selectedColor: const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
                    backgroundColor: Colors.black54,
                    label: Text(opt.$2, style: TextStyle(color: sel ? Colors.black : Colors.white, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                    onSelected: (_) => setModalState(() => months = opt.$1),
                  );
                }).toList(),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: const Text('إلغاء')),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFFBBF24), foregroundColor: Colors.black),
              child: const Text('توليد الآن', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );

    if (confirmed != true || !mounted) return;
    setState(() => _generating = true);
    final created = await FirestoreService.createBulkQrCodes(count, months);
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _records = [...created, ..._records];
      _generating = false;
    });
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF10B981),
        content: Text('تم توليد ${created.length} رمز QR جديد وحفظهم في قاعدة البيانات.'),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _openEditScreen(QrCodeRecord record) {
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => EditQrSheet(
          record: record,
          domainUrl: _domainUrl,
          onSaved: (updated) {
            setState(() {
              _records = _records.map((r) => r.id == updated.id ? updated : r).toList();
            });
          },
          onDeleted: (deletedId) {
            setState(() {
              _records = _records.where((r) => r.id != deletedId).toList();
            });
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _togglePrinted(QrCodeRecord record, bool nextPrinted) async {
    final nowIso = DateTime.now().toUtc().toIso8601String();
    setState(() {
      _records = _records.map((r) {
        if (r.id != record.id) return r;
        return QrCodeRecord(
          id: r.id,
          isActive: r.isActive,
          targetUrl: r.targetUrl,
          appType: r.appType,
          label: r.label,
          createdAt: r.createdAt,
          updatedAt: nowIso,
          lastScannedAt: r.lastScannedAt,
          totalScans: r.totalScans,
          expiresAt: r.expiresAt,
          subscriptionMonths: r.subscriptionMonths,
          wallets: r.wallets,
          isPrinted: nextPrinted,
          printedAt: nextPrinted ? nowIso : null,
        );
      }).toList();
    });

    final updated = await FirestoreService.toggleQrPrintedStatus(record, nextPrinted);
    if (!mounted) return;
    if (updated != null) {
      setState(() {
        _records = _records.map((r) => r.id == updated.id ? updated : r).toList();
      });
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          backgroundColor: nextPrinted ? const Color(0xFF06B6D4) : const Color(0xFFF59E0B),
          content: Text(
            nextPrinted
                ? 'تم نقل الكود [${record.id}] إلى خانة الكروت المطبوعة ✓'
                : 'تمت إعادة الكود [${record.id}] إلى قائمة غير المطبوعة',
            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _deactivateCard(QrCodeRecord record) async {
    final confirm = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF18181B),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0x66F59E0B)),
        ),
        title: const Text(
          'إلغاء التفعيل وإعادة الكارت كجديد؟',
          style: TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFFBBF24), fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 15),
        ),
        content: Text(
          'هل أنت متأكد من إلغاء تفعيل البطاقة [${record.id}]؟ سيتم مسح الروابط/المحافظ والوصف وتصفير المسحات لتعود كارتاً جديداً متاحاً للتفعيل.',
          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12.5, height: 1.5),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: const Text('تراجع')),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFFBBF24), foregroundColor: Colors.black),
            child: const Text('تأكيد إلغاء التفعيل', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (confirm != true || !mounted) return;
    final reset = await FirestoreService.deactivateQrCode(record);
    if (!mounted) return;
    if (reset != null) {
      setState(() {
        _records = _records.map((r) => r.id == reset.id ? reset : r).toList();
      });
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF59E0B),
          content: Text(
            'تم إلغاء تفعيل الكود [${record.id}] وإرجاعه لحالة متاح للتفعيل ✓',
            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
          ),
        ),
      );
    } else {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          backgroundColor: Color(0xFFEF4444),
          content: Text('تعذر إلغاء التفعيل، تحقق من الاتصال وحاول مجدداً.'),
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final total = _records.length;
    final activeCount = _records.where((r) => r.isActive && !r.isExpired).length;
    final inactiveCount = _records.where((r) => !r.isActive).length;
    final expiredCount = _records.where((r) => r.isExpired).length;
    final printedCount = _records.where((r) => r.isPrinted).length;
    final unprintedCount = _records.where((r) => !r.isPrinted).length;
    final totalScans = _records.fold<int>(0, (acc, r) => acc + r.totalScans);
    final filtered = _filteredRecords;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF09090B),
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF121216),
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        elevation: 0,
        title: Row(
          children: [
            Floating3DLogo(
              size: 36,
              child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: const Color(0xFF18181B),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x66FBBF24)),
                ),
                child: Image.asset('assets/logo.png', fit: BoxFit.contain),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            const Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Smart Card Admin',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: Color(0xFFFBBF24)),
                ),
                Text(
                  'لوحة الإدارة الحصرية',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: Colors.white60),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded, color: Color(0xFFFBBF24)),
            tooltip: 'تحديث',
            onPressed: _loadData,
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.settings_rounded, color: Colors.white70),
            tooltip: 'الإعدادات والأمان',
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.of(context).push(
                MaterialPageRoute(
                  builder: (_) => SettingsScreen(
                    initialSettings: _settings,
                    onSettingsUpdated: (s) => setState(() => _settings = s),
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.logout_rounded, color: Color(0xFFF87171)),
            tooltip: 'تسجيل الخروج',
            onPressed: () async {
              await AuthService.logoutAdmin();
              widget.onLogout();
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: _openScanner,
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
        foregroundColor: Colors.black,
        icon: const Icon(Icons.qr_code_scanner_rounded),
        label: const Text('مسح بالكاميرا', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        color: const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF121216),
        onRefresh: _loadData,
        child: CustomScrollView(
          slivers: [
            // KPI Row + Quick Actions
            SliverToBoxAdapter(
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 14, 14, 8),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Expanded(child: _kpiTile('إجمالي الأكواد', '$total', const Color(0xFFFBBF24))),
                        const SizedBox(width: 6),
                        Expanded(child: _kpiTile('المفعّلة', '$activeCount', const Color(0xFF10B981))),
                        const SizedBox(width: 6),
                        Expanded(child: _kpiTile('المطبوعة', '$printedCount', const Color(0xFF22D3EE))),
                        const SizedBox(width: 6),
                        Expanded(child: _kpiTile('المسحات', '$totalScans', const Color(0xFF38BDF8))),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 10),
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Expanded(
                          child: SizedBox(
                            height: 46,
                            child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                              onPressed: _generating ? null : _openBulkGenerateDialog,
                              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                                backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
                                foregroundColor: Colors.black,
                                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                              ),
                              icon: _generating
                                  ? const SizedBox(width: 16, height: 16, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.black))
                                  : const Icon(Icons.add_box_rounded, size: 18),
                              label: const Text('توليد أكواد جديدة', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 12.5)),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Expanded(
                          child: SizedBox(
                            height: 46,
                            child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                              onPressed: _openScanner,
                              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                                foregroundColor: const Color(0xFFFCD34D),
                                side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
                                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                              ),
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.camera_alt_rounded, size: 18),
                              label: const Text('فحص رمز QR', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 12.5)),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextField(
                      onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _searchQuery = v),
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        hintText: 'ابحث بالكود، الاسم، الرقم، أو المنصة...',
                        hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white38, fontSize: 12.5),
                        prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search_rounded, color: Color(0xFFFBBF24)),
                        filled: true,
                        fillColor: const Color(0xFF121216),
                        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12, horizontal: 14),
                        border: OutlineInputBorder(
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Color(0x33F59E0B)),
                        ),
                        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Color(0x33F59E0B)),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 10),
                    SingleChildScrollView(
                      scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          _filterChip('الكل ($total)', _QrFilter.all),
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          _filterChip('الكروت المطبوعة ✓ ($printedCount)', _QrFilter.printed),
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          _filterChip('لم تُطبع بعد ($unprintedCount)', _QrFilter.unprinted),
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          _filterChip('مفعّلة ($activeCount)', _QrFilter.active),
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          _filterChip('غير مفعّلة ($inactiveCount)', _QrFilter.inactive),
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          _filterChip('منتهية ($expiredCount)', _QrFilter.expired),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),

            if (_loading)
              const SliverFillRemaining(
                child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Color(0xFFFBBF24))),
              )
            else if (filtered.isEmpty)
              const SliverFillRemaining(
                child: Center(
                  child: Text('لا توجد بطاقات مطابقة للبحث.', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)),
                ),
              )
            else
              SliverPadding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 4, 14, 90),
                sliver: SliverList(
                  delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
                    (context, index) {
                      final r = filtered[index];
                      return Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                        child: _buildQrCard(r),
                      );
                    },
                    childCount: filtered.length,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _kpiTile(String title, String value, Color accent) {
    return Tilt3DCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12, horizontal: 10),
      borderColor: accent.withValues(alpha: 0.35),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Text(
            value,
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: accent),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(
            title,
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: Colors.white70),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _filterChip(String label, _QrFilter value) {
    final selected = _filter == value;
    return ChoiceChip(
      selected: selected,
      selectedColor: const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF121216),
      side: BorderSide(color: selected ? const Color(0xFFFDE68A) : Colors.white12),
      label: Text(
        label,
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 11.5,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
          color: selected ? Colors.black : Colors.white70,
        ),
      ),
      onSelected: (_) => setState(() => _filter = value),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildQrCard(QrCodeRecord r) {
    final scanUrl = '${_domainUrl.replaceAll(RegExp(r'/+$'), '')}/q/${r.id}';
    final statusColor = r.isExpired
        ? const Color(0xFFEF4444)
        : r.isActive
            ? const Color(0xFF10B981)
            : const Color(0xFFF59E0B);
    final statusText = r.isExpired ? 'منتهي' : (r.isActive ? 'مفعّل' : 'متاح للتفعيل');
    final walletProv = r.appType == AppType.wallet && (r.targetUrl ?? '').isNotEmpty
        ? resolveWalletProvider(r.targetUrl!, r.wallets.isNotEmpty ? r.wallets.first.provider : null)
        : null;
    final effectiveLabel = (r.appType == AppType.wallet && walletProv != null && walletProv != WalletProvider.generic)
        ? walletProv.nameAr
        : r.appType.labelAr;
    final effectiveColor = (r.appType == AppType.wallet && walletProv != null && walletProv != WalletProvider.generic)
        ? walletProv.color
        : r.appType.brandColor;

    return Tilt3DCard(
      onTap: () => _openEditScreen(r),
      borderColor: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.35),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(5),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
            ),
            child: QrImageView(
              data: scanUrl,
              version: QrVersions.auto,
              size: 56,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      r.id,
                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: Color(0xFFFBBF24)),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 7, vertical: 2),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.16),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
                        border: Border.all(color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.45)),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        statusText,
                        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: statusColor),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  r.isActive
                      ? (r.label.isNotEmpty ? r.label : '$effectiveLabel • ${r.targetUrl ?? "بدون رابط"}')
                      : 'في انتظار التفعيل • متاح لأول مسح',
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.white70),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(r.isActive ? r.appType.icon : Icons.hourglass_empty_rounded, size: 13, color: r.isActive ? effectiveColor : Colors.white54),
                    const SizedBox(width: 4),
                    Text(
                      r.isActive ? effectiveLabel : 'غير معيّن',
                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, color: Colors.white60, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    const Icon(Icons.visibility_rounded, size: 13, color: Colors.white54),
                    const SizedBox(width: 3),
                    Text(
                      '${r.totalScans} مسحة',
                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, color: Colors.white60),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    SizedBox(
                      width: 24,
                      height: 24,
                      child: Checkbox(
                        value: r.isPrinted,
                        activeColor: const Color(0xFF22D3EE),
                        checkColor: Colors.black,
                        side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white38),
                        onChanged: (val) => _togglePrinted(r, val ?? false),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 4),
                    Text(
                      r.isPrinted ? 'تمت الطباعة ✓' : 'لم يُطبع بعد',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 10.5,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                        color: r.isPrinted ? const Color(0xFF22D3EE) : Colors.white54,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.copy_rounded, size: 18, color: Color(0xFFFCD34D)),
                tooltip: 'نسخ رابط البطاقة',
                onPressed: () {
                  Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: scanUrl));
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(content: Text('تم نسخ رابط البطاقة: $scanUrl')),
                  );
                },
              ),
              if (r.isActive)
                IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.restart_alt_rounded, size: 20, color: Color(0xFFF59E0B)),
                  tooltip: 'إلغاء التفعيل وإرجاع الكارت كجديد',
                  onPressed: () => _deactivateCard(r),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

FILE: smart_card_admin_dart/lib/screens/qr_scanner_screen.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import 'package:mobile_scanner/mobile_scanner.dart';
import 'package:permission_handler/permission_handler.dart';
import '../utils/deep_links.dart';

class QrScannerScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const QrScannerScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<QrScannerScreen> createState() => _QrScannerScreenState();
}

class _QrScannerScreenState extends State<QrScannerScreen> {
  final MobileScannerController _scannerController = MobileScannerController(
    detectionSpeed: DetectionSpeed.noDuplicates,
  );
  bool _checkingPermission = true;
  bool _hasPermission = false;
  bool _permanentlyDenied = false;
  bool _handledScan = false;
  bool _torchOn = false;
  String? _statusMsg;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _requestCameraPermission();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scannerController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _requestCameraPermission() async {
    setState(() {
      _checkingPermission = true;
      _statusMsg = null;
    });

    final status = await Permission.camera.request();
    if (!mounted) return;

    setState(() {
      _checkingPermission = false;
      _hasPermission = status.isGranted;
      _permanentlyDenied = status.isPermanentlyDenied;
      if (!status.isGranted) {
        _statusMsg = 'تم رفض إذن الكاميرا — يمكنك منح الإذن الآن لمسح الرمز مباشرة.';
      }
    });
  }

  void _onRawQrDetected(String rawValue) {
    if (_handledScan) return;
    final extracted = extractQrIdFromUrl(rawValue);
    if (extracted != null && extracted.isNotEmpty) {
      _handledScan = true;
      Navigator.of(context).pop(extracted);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pickImageAndDecode() async {
    final picker = ImagePicker();
    final file = await picker.pickImage(source: ImageSource.gallery);
    if (file == null) return;

    final capture = await _scannerController.analyzeImage(file.path);
    if (!mounted) return;

    if (capture != null && capture.barcodes.isNotEmpty) {
      for (final b in capture.barcodes) {
        final raw = b.rawValue;
        if (raw != null && raw.isNotEmpty) {
          final id = extractQrIdFromUrl(raw);
          if (id != null) {
            Navigator.of(context).pop(id);
            return;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    setState(() {
      _statusMsg = 'لم يتم العثور على رمز QR صالح داخل الصورة المختارة.';
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF09090B),
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF121216),
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        title: const Text(
          'مسح رمز QR بالكاميرا',
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
        ),
        actions: [
          if (_hasPermission)
            IconButton(
              icon: Icon(
                _torchOn ? Icons.flash_on_rounded : Icons.flash_off_rounded,
                color: const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
              ),
              onPressed: () async {
                await _scannerController.toggleTorch();
                setState(() => _torchOn = !_torchOn);
              },
              tooltip: 'الفلاش',
            ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.photo_library_rounded, color: Color(0xFFFBBF24)),
            onPressed: _pickImageAndDecode,
            tooltip: 'مسح صورة من المعرض',
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: _checkingPermission
          ? const Center(
              child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Color(0xFFFBBF24)),
            )
          : !_hasPermission
              ? Center(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                    child: Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: const Color(0xFF121216),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
                        border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
                      ),
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            width: 68,
                            height: 68,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: const Color(0x1AF59E0B),
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                              border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
                            ),
                            child: const Icon(Icons.camera_alt_rounded, color: Color(0xFFFBBF24), size: 34),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 16),
                          const Text(
                            'إذن الكاميرا لمسح الرمز',
                            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: Colors.white),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 8),
                          Text(
                            _statusMsg ?? 'اختر السماح باستخدام الكاميرا لمسح أي رمز Smart Card وتعديله فوراً.',
                            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, color: Colors.white70),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 20),
                          SizedBox(
                            width: double.infinity,
                            height: 48,
                            child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                              onPressed: _permanentlyDenied ? openAppSettings : _requestCameraPermission,
                              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                                backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
                                foregroundColor: Colors.black,
                                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                              ),
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.camera_enhance_rounded),
                              label: Text(
                                _permanentlyDenied ? 'فتح إعدادات الإذن' : 'إعطاء إذن الكاميرا الآن',
                                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 10),
                          SizedBox(
                            width: double.infinity,
                            height: 46,
                            child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                              onPressed: _pickImageAndDecode,
                              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                                foregroundColor: const Color(0xFFFCD34D),
                                side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
                                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                              ),
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.upload_rounded),
                              label: const Text('أو اختيار صورة QR من المعرض', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                )
              : Stack(
                  children: [
                    MobileScanner(
                      controller: _scannerController,
                      onDetect: (capture) {
                        for (final barcode in capture.barcodes) {
                          final raw = barcode.rawValue;
                          if (raw != null && raw.isNotEmpty) {
                            _onRawQrDetected(raw);
                            break;
                          }
                        }
                      },
                    ),
                    Center(
                      child: Container(
                        width: 240,
                        height: 240,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFFFBBF24), width: 3),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (_statusMsg != null)
                      Positioned(
                        bottom: 24,
                        left: 20,
                        right: 20,
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.black87,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                            border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x66EF4444)),
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            _statusMsg!,
                            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFFCA5A5), fontSize: 12),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
    );
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: smart_card_admin_dart/lib/screens/edit_qr_sheet.dart

import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:ui' as ui;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/rendering.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:qr_flutter/qr_flutter.dart';
import 'package:share_plus/share_plus.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';
import '../models/qr_models.dart';
import '../services/firestore_service.dart';
import '../utils/deep_links.dart';
import '../widgets/three_d_widgets.dart';

const List<String> _egyptianBanks = [
  'البنك الأهلي المصري (NBE)',
  'بنك مصر (Banque Misr)',
  'البنك التجاري الدولي (CIB)',
  'بنك قطر الوطني (QNB)',
  'بنك القاهرة (Banque du Caire)',
  'بنك الإسكندرية (AlexBank)',
  'بنك فيصل الإسلامي',
  'مصرف أبوظبي الإسلامي (ADIB)',
  'بنك البركة مصر',
  'البنك العربي الأفريقي الدولي (AAIB)',
  'بنك كريدي أجريكول مصر',
  'بنك HSBC مصر',
  'بنك آخر / حساب دولي',
];

class EditQrSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final QrCodeRecord record;
  final String domainUrl;
  final ValueChanged<QrCodeRecord> onSaved;
  final ValueChanged<String> onDeleted;

  const EditQrSheet({
    super.key,
    required this.record,
    required this.domainUrl,
    required this.onSaved,
    required this.onDeleted,
  });

  @override
  State<EditQrSheet> createState() => _EditQrSheetState();
}

class _EditQrSheetState extends State<EditQrSheet> {
  final GlobalKey _qrKey = GlobalKey();
  late QrCodeRecord _record;
  late AppType _selectedType;
  late TextEditingController _labelCtrl;
  late TextEditingController _targetCtrl;

  // Bank fields
  String _bankName = _egyptianBanks.first;
  late TextEditingController _bankAccCtrl;
  late TextEditingController _bankOwnerCtrl;
  late TextEditingController _bankIbanCtrl;
  late TextEditingController _bankNotesCtrl;

  // Multi-wallet (1 to 4) with 100% automatic operator detection
  int _walletCount = 1;
  final List<TextEditingController> _walletPhoneCtrls = List.generate(4, (_) => TextEditingController());
  final List<TextEditingController> _walletOwnerCtrls = List.generate(4, (_) => TextEditingController());
  final List<WalletProvider> _walletProviders = List.filled(4, WalletProvider.generic);

  bool _saving = false;
  bool _purging = false;
  bool _deactivating = false;
  String? _errorMsg;
  String? _noticeMsg;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _record = widget.record;
    _selectedType = _record.appType;
    _labelCtrl = TextEditingController(text: _record.label);
    _targetCtrl = TextEditingController(text: _record.targetUrl ?? '');
    _bankAccCtrl = TextEditingController();
    _bankOwnerCtrl = TextEditingController();
    _bankIbanCtrl = TextEditingController();
    _bankNotesCtrl = TextEditingController();

    if (_selectedType == AppType.bank || (_record.targetUrl?.startsWith('bank://') ?? false)) {
      _selectedType = AppType.bank;
      final bank = decodeBankDetails(_record.targetUrl);
      if (bank != null) {
        _bankName = _egyptianBanks.contains(bank.bankName) ? bank.bankName : _egyptianBanks.last;
        _bankAccCtrl.text = bank.accountNumber;
        _bankOwnerCtrl.text = bank.accountName;
        _bankIbanCtrl.text = bank.iban;
        _bankNotesCtrl.text = bank.notes;
      }
    } else if (_selectedType == AppType.wallet || _record.wallets.isNotEmpty) {
      _selectedType = AppType.wallet;
      if (_record.wallets.isNotEmpty) {
        _walletCount = _record.wallets.length.clamp(1, 4);
        for (var i = 0; i < _record.wallets.length && i < 4; i++) {
          final w = _record.wallets[i];
          _walletPhoneCtrls[i].text = w.phone;
          _walletOwnerCtrls[i].text = w.ownerName;
          _walletProviders[i] = resolveWalletProvider(w.phone, w.provider);
        }
      } else if ((_record.targetUrl ?? '').isNotEmpty) {
        _walletPhoneCtrls[0].text = _record.targetUrl!;
        _walletProviders[0] = resolveWalletProvider(_record.targetUrl!);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _labelCtrl.dispose();
    _targetCtrl.dispose();
    _bankAccCtrl.dispose();
    _bankOwnerCtrl.dispose();
    _bankIbanCtrl.dispose();
    _bankNotesCtrl.dispose();
    for (final c in _walletPhoneCtrls) {
      c.dispose();
    }
    for (final c in _walletOwnerCtrls) {
      c.dispose();
    }
    super.dispose();
  }

  String get _scanUrl => '${widget.domainUrl.replaceAll(RegExp(r'/+$'), '')}/q/${_record.id}';

  void _onWalletPhoneChanged(int idx, String val) {
    setState(() {
      final trimmed = val.trim();
      if (trimmed.isEmpty) {
        _walletProviders[idx] = WalletProvider.generic;
      } else if (isQuickPayUrl(trimmed)) {
        _walletProviders[idx] = detectWalletProviderFromUrl(trimmed);
      } else {
        _walletProviders[idx] = detectWalletInfo(trimmed).provider;
      }
    });
  }

  Future<void> _purgeTargetFromDb({int? walletIndex}) async {
    setState(() {
      _purging = true;
      _errorMsg = null;
      _noticeMsg = null;
    });

    if (walletIndex != null) {
      _walletPhoneCtrls[walletIndex].clear();
      _walletProviders[walletIndex] = WalletProvider.generic;
      final remaining = <WalletItem>[];
      for (var i = 0; i < _walletCount; i++) {
        final phone = _walletPhoneCtrls[i].text.trim();
        if (phone.isNotEmpty) {
          remaining.add(WalletItem(
            phone: phone,
            provider: resolveWalletProvider(phone, _walletProviders[i]),
            ownerName: _walletOwnerCtrls[i].text.trim(),
          ));
        }
      }
      final updated = await FirestoreService.purgeQrTarget(
        existing: _record,
        appType: AppType.wallet,
        remainingWallets: remaining,
        label: _labelCtrl.text,
      );
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (updated != null) {
        _record = updated;
        widget.onSaved(updated);
        _noticeMsg = 'تم حذف الرابط/الرقم القديم نهائياً من قاعدة البيانات.';
      }
    } else {
      _targetCtrl.clear();
      final updated = await FirestoreService.purgeQrTarget(
        existing: _record,
        appType: _selectedType,
        label: _labelCtrl.text,
      );
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (updated != null) {
        _record = updated;
        widget.onSaved(updated);
        _noticeMsg = 'تم حذف الرابط القديم نهائياً من قاعدة البيانات.';
      }
    }

    setState(() => _purging = false);
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    setState(() {
      _errorMsg = null;
      _noticeMsg = null;
    });

    String? finalTarget = _targetCtrl.text.trim();
    List<WalletItem>? finalWallets;

    if (_selectedType == AppType.bank) {
      if (_bankAccCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty) {
        setState(() => _errorMsg = 'يرجى كتابة رقم الحساب البنكي.');
        return;
      }
      finalTarget = encodeBankDetails(BankDetails(
        bankName: _bankName,
        accountNumber: _bankAccCtrl.text.trim(),
        accountName: _bankOwnerCtrl.text.trim(),
        iban: _bankIbanCtrl.text.trim(),
        notes: _bankNotesCtrl.text.trim(),
      ));
    } else if (_selectedType == AppType.wallet) {
      final list = <WalletItem>[];
      for (var i = 0; i < _walletCount; i++) {
        final phone = _walletPhoneCtrls[i].text.trim();
        final owner = _walletOwnerCtrls[i].text.trim();
        if (phone.isEmpty) {
          setState(() => _errorMsg = _walletCount == 1
              ? 'يرجى إدخال رقم المحفظة أو رابط الدفع السريع.'
              : 'يرجى إدخال بيانات المحفظة رقم ${i + 1}.');
          return;
        }
        if (owner.isEmpty) {
          setState(() => _errorMsg = _walletCount == 1
              ? 'يرجى كتابة الاسم (إلزامي).'
              : 'يرجى كتابة الاسم للمحفظة رقم ${i + 1} (إلزامي).');
          return;
        }
        final prov = resolveWalletProvider(phone, _walletProviders[i]);
        list.add(WalletItem(
          phone: isQuickPayUrl(phone) ? sanitizeQuickPayUrl(phone, prov) : detectWalletInfo(phone).cleanPhone,
          provider: prov,
          ownerName: owner,
        ));
      }
      finalWallets = list;
      finalTarget = list.first.phone;
    } else if (_selectedType == AppType.instapay) {
      if (finalTarget.isEmpty) {
        setState(() => _errorMsg = 'يرجى إدخال رابط الدفع السريع من تطبيق إنستاباي.');
        return;
      }
      if (!finalTarget.startsWith('http://') && !finalTarget.startsWith('https://')) {
        finalTarget = 'https://$finalTarget';
      }
    } else if (_selectedType == AppType.whatsapp && finalTarget.isNotEmpty) {
      finalTarget = formatWhatsappTargetUrl(finalTarget);
    } else if (_selectedType == AppType.phone && finalTarget.isNotEmpty) {
      finalTarget = formatPhoneTargetUrl(finalTarget);
    } else if (_selectedType == AppType.tiktok && finalTarget.isNotEmpty) {
      finalTarget = formatTikTokTargetUrl(finalTarget);
    } else if (_selectedType == AppType.instagram && finalTarget.isNotEmpty) {
      finalTarget = formatInstagramTargetUrl(finalTarget);
    } else if (_selectedType == AppType.telegram && finalTarget.isNotEmpty) {
      finalTarget = formatTelegramTargetUrl(finalTarget);
    } else if (_selectedType == AppType.snapchat && finalTarget.isNotEmpty) {
      finalTarget = formatSnapchatTargetUrl(finalTarget);
    } else if (finalTarget.isEmpty) {
      setState(() => _errorMsg = 'يرجى إدخال الرابط أو البيانات المطلوبة.');
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _saving = true);
    final updated = await FirestoreService.updateQrCode(
      existing: _record,
      appType: _selectedType,
      targetUrl: finalTarget,
      label: _labelCtrl.text,
      wallets: finalWallets,
    );
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() => _saving = false);

    if (updated != null) {
      _record = updated;
      widget.onSaved(updated);
      Navigator.of(context).pop();
    } else {
      setState(() => _errorMsg = 'تعذر الحفظ في قاعدة البيانات. تحقق من الاتصال.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _renew(int months) async {
    setState(() => _saving = true);
    final updated = await FirestoreService.renewQrSubscription(_record, months);
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() => _saving = false);
    if (updated != null) {
      setState(() {
        _record = updated;
        _noticeMsg = months >= 900 ? 'تم التفعيل الدائم بنجاح.' : 'تم تجديد الاشتراك +$months شهر بنجاح.';
      });
      widget.onSaved(updated);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _deactivateCard() async {
    final confirm = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF18181B),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0x66F59E0B)),
        ),
        title: const Text(
          'إلغاء التفعيل وإعادة الكارت كجديد؟',
          style: TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFFBBF24), fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 15.5),
        ),
        content: Text(
          'سيتم مسح جميع البيانات المكتوبة على البطاقة (${_record.id}) بما في ذلك الروابط وأرقام المحافظ والوصف وعداد المسحات، وستعود إلى حالة "غير مفعل" ليتمكن العميل من تفعيلها من الصفر.',
          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12.5, height: 1.5),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: const Text('تراجع')),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFFBBF24), foregroundColor: Colors.black),
            child: const Text('تأكيد إلغاء التفعيل', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (confirm != true || !mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _deactivating = true;
      _errorMsg = null;
      _noticeMsg = null;
    });

    final reset = await FirestoreService.deactivateQrCode(_record);
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() => _deactivating = false);

    if (reset != null) {
      _record = reset;
      widget.onSaved(reset);
      Navigator.of(context).pop();
    } else {
      setState(() => _errorMsg = 'تعذر إلغاء التفعيل في قاعدة البيانات، تحقق من الاتصال.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _shareQrPng() async {
    try {
      final boundary = _qrKey.currentContext?.findRenderObject() as RenderRepaintBoundary?;
      if (boundary == null) return;
      final image = await boundary.toImage(pixelRatio: 3.0);
      final byteData = await image.toByteData(format: ui.ImageByteFormat.png);
      if (byteData == null) return;
      final bytes = byteData.buffer.asUint8List();
      final dir = await getTemporaryDirectory();
      final file = File('${dir.path}/smart_card_${_record.id}.png');
      await file.writeAsBytes(bytes);
      await Share.shareXFiles([XFile(file.path)], text: 'Smart Card QR: ${_record.id}\n$_scanUrl');
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF09090B),
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF121216),
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        title: Text(
          'إدارة البطاقة: ${_record.id}',
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
        ),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_forever_rounded, color: Color(0xFFF87171)),
            tooltip: 'حذف الرمز نهائياً',
            onPressed: () async {
              final confirm = await showDialog<bool>(
                context: context,
                builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
                  backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF18181B),
                  title: const Text('حذف رمز QR؟', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                  content: Text('سيتم حذف الرمز ${_record.id} نهائياً من قاعدة البيانات.', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70)),
                  actions: [
                    TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: const Text('إلغاء')),
                    TextButton(
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
                      child: const Text('حذف', style: TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFF87171))),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              );
              if (confirm == true) {
                final ok = await FirestoreService.deleteQrCode(_record.id);
                if (!context.mounted) return;
                if (ok) {
                  widget.onDeleted(_record.id);
                  Navigator.of(context).pop();
                }
              }
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Card3DEnter(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              // QR Header Card
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: const Color(0xFF121216),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    RepaintBoundary(
                      key: _qrKey,
                      child: Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                        ),
                        child: QrImageView(
                          data: _scanUrl,
                          version: QrVersions.auto,
                          size: 92,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 14),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            _record.id,
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 16,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                              color: Color(0xFFFBBF24),
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 4),
                          Text(
                            'المسحات: ${_record.totalScans} • ${_record.isActive ? "مفعّل" : "غير مفعّل"}',
                            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.white70),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 10),
                          Wrap(
                            spacing: 6,
                            runSpacing: 6,
                            children: [
                              OutlinedButton.icon(
                                onPressed: () {
                                  Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: _scanUrl));
                                  setState(() => _noticeMsg = 'تم نسخ رابط البطاقة.');
                                },
                                style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                                  foregroundColor: const Color(0xFFFCD34D),
                                  side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
                                ),
                                icon: const Icon(Icons.copy_rounded, size: 15),
                                label: const Text('نسخ', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11)),
                              ),
                              OutlinedButton.icon(
                                onPressed: _shareQrPng,
                                style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                                  foregroundColor: const Color(0xFFFCD34D),
                                  side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
                                ),
                                icon: const Icon(Icons.share_rounded, size: 15),
                                label: const Text('مشاركة PNG', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11)),
                              ),
                              OutlinedButton.icon(
                                onPressed: () => launchUrl(Uri.parse(_scanUrl), mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication),
                                style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                                  foregroundColor: Colors.white70,
                                  side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white24),
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
                                ),
                                icon: const Icon(Icons.open_in_new_rounded, size: 15),
                                label: const Text('اختبار', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11)),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),

              if (_errorMsg != null) ...[
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: const Color(0x26EF4444),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                    border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x66EF4444)),
                  ),
                  child: Text(_errorMsg!, style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFFCA5A5), fontSize: 12)),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
              ],
              if (_noticeMsg != null) ...[
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: const Color(0x2610B981),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                    border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x6610B981)),
                  ),
                  child: Text(_noticeMsg!, style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF6EE7B7), fontSize: 12)),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
              ],

              // Label input
              TextField(
                controller: _labelCtrl,
                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'اسم / وصف البطاقة (اختياري)',
                  labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12.5),
                  filled: true,
                  fillColor: const Color(0xFF121216),
                  border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // 3D Platform Grid
              const Text(
                'اختر المنصة المرتبطة بالكارت:',
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: Color(0xFFFCD34D)),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              GridView.count(
                crossAxisCount: 3,
                shrinkWrap: true,
                physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                crossAxisSpacing: 8,
                mainAxisSpacing: 8,
                childAspectRatio: 1.05,
                children: AppType.values.map((t) {
                  return PlatformTile3D(
                    selected: _selectedType == t,
                    icon: t.icon,
                    iconColor: t.brandColor,
                    label: t.labelAr,
                    onTap: () {
                      setState(() {
                        if (_selectedType != t) {
                          _selectedType = t;
                          _targetCtrl.clear();
                          _errorMsg = null;
                        }
                      });
                    },
                  );
                }).toList(),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // Dynamic Platform Inputs
              if (_selectedType == AppType.wallet) _buildWalletEditor()
              else if (_selectedType == AppType.bank) _buildBankEditor()
              else _buildStandardTargetEditor(),

              const SizedBox(height: 18),

              // Subscription & Scan Reset Bar
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: const Color(0xFF121216),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                  border: Border.all(color: Colors.white12),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    const Text(
                      'إدارة الاشتراك والعداد:',
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: Color(0xFFFCD34D)),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 10),
                    Wrap(
                      spacing: 8,
                      runSpacing: 8,
                      children: [
                        ActionChip(
                          backgroundColor: Colors.black54,
                          label: const Text('+1 شهر', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 11.5)),
                          onPressed: () => _renew(1),
                        ),
                        ActionChip(
                          backgroundColor: Colors.black54,
                          label: const Text('+3 شهور', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 11.5)),
                          onPressed: () => _renew(3),
                        ),
                        ActionChip(
                          backgroundColor: Colors.black54,
                          label: const Text('+12 شهر', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 11.5)),
                          onPressed: () => _renew(12),
                        ),
                        ActionChip(
                          backgroundColor: const Color(0x33F59E0B),
                          label: const Text('تفعيل دائم', style: TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFFCD34D), fontSize: 11.5)),
                          onPressed: () => _renew(999),
                        ),
                        ActionChip(
                          backgroundColor: const Color(0x26EF4444),
                          label: const Text('تصفير المسحات', style: TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFFCA5A5), fontSize: 11.5)),
                          onPressed: () async {
                            final ok = await FirestoreService.resetQrScanCount(_record);
                            if (ok && mounted) {
                              final updated = QrCodeRecord(
                                id: _record.id,
                                isActive: _record.isActive,
                                targetUrl: _record.targetUrl,
                                appType: _record.appType,
                                label: _record.label,
                                createdAt: _record.createdAt,
                                updatedAt: DateTime.now().toUtc().toIso8601String(),
                                totalScans: 0,
                                expiresAt: _record.expiresAt,
                                subscriptionMonths: _record.subscriptionMonths,
                                wallets: _record.wallets,
                                isPrinted: _record.isPrinted,
                                printedAt: _record.printedAt,
                              );
                              setState(() {
                                _record = updated;
                                _noticeMsg = 'تم تصفير عداد المسحات.';
                              });
                              widget.onSaved(updated);
                            }
                          },
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const Divider(color: Colors.white12, height: 22),
                    SwitchListTile.adaptive(
                      contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      activeColor: const Color(0xFF22D3EE),
                      title: const Text(
                        'حالة الطباعة (تمت طباعة البطاقة ✓)',
                        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: Color(0xFF67E8F9)),
                      ),
                      subtitle: Text(
                        _record.isPrinted ? 'موجودة الآن في قائمة الكروت المطبوعة' : 'في انتظار الطباعة (غير مطبوعة)',
                        style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: Colors.white60),
                      ),
                      value: _record.isPrinted,
                      onChanged: (val) async {
                        final res = await FirestoreService.toggleQrPrintedStatus(_record, val);
                        if (res != null && mounted) {
                          setState(() {
                            _record = res;
                            _noticeMsg = val
                                ? 'تم نقل البطاقة إلى خانة الكروت المطبوعة ✓'
                                : 'تمت إعادة البطاقة إلى قائمة غير المطبوعة';
                          });
                          widget.onSaved(res);
                        }
                      },
                    ),
                    if (_record.isActive) ...[
                      const Divider(color: Colors.white12, height: 20),
                      SizedBox(
                        width: double.infinity,
                        child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                          onPressed: (_saving || _purging || _deactivating) ? null : _deactivateCard,
                          style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                            foregroundColor: const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
                            side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0x66F59E0B)),
                            backgroundColor: const Color(0x1AF59E0B),
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12, horizontal: 14),
                            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                          ),
                          icon: _deactivating
                              ? const SizedBox(
                                  width: 16,
                                  height: 16,
                                  child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: Color(0xFFFBBF24)),
                                )
                              : const Icon(Icons.restart_alt_rounded, size: 18),
                          label: Text(
                            _deactivating ? 'جاري إلغاء التفعيل...' : 'إلغاء التفعيل وإرجاع الكارت كجديد',
                            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),

              SizedBox(
                height: 52,
                child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: (_saving || _purging || _deactivating) ? null : _save,
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
                    foregroundColor: Colors.black,
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
                  ),
                  icon: _saving
                      ? const SizedBox(width: 18, height: 18, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2.5, color: Colors.black))
                      : const Icon(Icons.save_rounded),
                  label: Text(
                    _saving ? 'جاري الحفظ...' : 'حفظ وتحديث البطاقة فوراً',
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStandardTargetEditor() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFF121216),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(
                'بيانات ${_selectedType.labelAr}:',
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: Color(0xFFFCD34D)),
              ),
              if (_targetCtrl.text.trim().isNotEmpty)
                TextButton.icon(
                  onPressed: _purging ? null : () => _purgeTargetFromDb(),
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_sweep_rounded, size: 16, color: Color(0xFFF87171)),
                  label: const Text('حذف من قاعدة البيانات', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: Color(0xFFF87171))),
                ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          TextField(
            controller: _targetCtrl,
            textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13.5),
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              hintText: _selectedType.placeholder,
              hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white38, fontSize: 12),
              filled: true,
              fillColor: Colors.black54,
              border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBankEditor() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFF121216),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            'بيانات الحساب البنكي:',
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: Color(0xFFFCD34D)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
            value: _bankName,
            dropdownColor: const Color(0xFF18181B),
            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13),
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              filled: true,
              fillColor: Colors.black54,
              border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            ),
            items: _egyptianBanks
                .map((b) => DropdownMenuItem(value: b, child: Text(b, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)))
                .toList(),
            onChanged: (v) {
              if (v != null) setState(() => _bankName = v);
            },
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          TextField(
            controller: _bankAccCtrl,
            textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,

              labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12),
