              fillColor: Colors.black54,
              border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          TextField(
            controller: _bankOwnerCtrl,
            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              labelText: 'اسم صاحب الحساب (اختياري)',
              labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12),
              filled: true,
              fillColor: Colors.black54,
              border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          TextField(
            controller: _bankIbanCtrl,
            textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              labelText: 'IBAN (اختياري)',
              labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12),
              filled: true,
              fillColor: Colors.black54,
              border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildWalletEditor() {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: const Color(0xFF121216),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
            border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
          ),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              const Text(
                'عدد المحافظ المرتبطة بالكارت:',
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: Color(0xFFFCD34D)),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              Row(
                children: [1, 2, 3, 4].map((count) {
                  final selected = _walletCount == count;
                  return Expanded(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 3),
                      child: InkWell(
                        onTap: () => setState(() => _walletCount = count),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: selected ? const Color(0xFFFBBF24) : Colors.black54,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                            border: Border.all(color: selected ? const Color(0xFFFDE68A) : Colors.white12),
                          ),
                          child: Column(
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                '$count',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                                  color: selected ? Colors.black : Colors.white,
                                ),
                              ),
                              Text(
                                count == 1 ? 'محفظة' : 'محافظ',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  fontSize: 10,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                  color: selected ? Colors.black87 : Colors.white60,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                }).toList(),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        ...List.generate(_walletCount, (idx) {
          final text = _walletPhoneCtrls[idx].text.trim();
          final prov = text.isNotEmpty ? resolveWalletProvider(text, _walletProviders[idx]) : WalletProvider.generic;
          final isKnown = text.isNotEmpty && prov != WalletProvider.generic;

          return Container(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xFF121216),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
              border: Border.all(
                color: isKnown ? prov.color.withValues(alpha: 0.6) : const Color(0x4DF59E0B),
              ),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          width: 34,
                          height: 34,
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: (isKnown ? prov.color : const Color(0xFFF59E0B)).withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                          ),
                          child: Icon(
                            Icons.account_balance_wallet_rounded,
                            color: isKnown ? prov.color : const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
                            size: 18,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Text(
                              _walletCount > 1 ? 'المحفظة رقم ${idx + 1}' : 'بيانات المحفظة',
                              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: Colors.white),
                            ),
                            if (isKnown)
                              Text(
                                '${prov.nameAr} (${prov.nameEn})',
                                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: prov.color),
                              ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    if (text.isNotEmpty)
                      TextButton.icon(
                        onPressed: _purging ? null : () => _purgeTargetFromDb(walletIndex: idx),
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_sweep_rounded, size: 15, color: Color(0xFFF87171)),
                        label: const Text('حذف من القاعدة', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: Color(0xFFF87171))),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                TextField(
                  controller: _walletPhoneCtrls[idx],
                  textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
                  onChanged: (v) => _onWalletPhoneChanged(idx, v),
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFFCD34D), fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'رقم المحفظة أو رابط الدفع السريع (إلزامي)',
                    labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 11.5),
                    hintText: '010... أو https://...',
                    hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white30),
                    filled: true,
                    fillColor: Colors.black54,
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                TextField(
                  controller: _walletOwnerCtrls[idx],
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'الاسم (إلزامي)',
                    labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 11.5),
                    hintText: 'مثال: أحمد محمد علي',
                    hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white30),
                    filled: true,
                    fillColor: Colors.black54,
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        }),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../models/qr_models.dart';
import '../services/auth_service.dart';
import '../services/firestore_service.dart';
import '../widgets/three_d_widgets.dart';

class SettingsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final PlatformSettings initialSettings;
  final ValueChanged<PlatformSettings> onSettingsUpdated;

  const SettingsScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.initialSettings,
    required this.onSettingsUpdated,
  });

  @override
  State<SettingsScreen> createState() => _SettingsScreenState();
}

class _SettingsScreenState extends State<SettingsScreen> {
  late TextEditingController _domainCtrl;
  late TextEditingController _waCtrl;
  late TextEditingController _titleCtrl;

  final _curPwdCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _newPwdCtrl = TextEditingController();

  bool _savingSettings = false;
  bool _savingPwd = false;
  String? _statusMsg;
  bool _isError = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _domainCtrl = TextEditingController(text: widget.initialSettings.publicDomainUrl);
    _waCtrl = TextEditingController(text: widget.initialSettings.developerWhatsapp);
    _titleCtrl = TextEditingController(text: widget.initialSettings.developerTitle);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _domainCtrl.dispose();
    _waCtrl.dispose();
    _titleCtrl.dispose();
    _curPwdCtrl.dispose();
    _newPwdCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _saveSettings() async {
    setState(() {
      _savingSettings = true;
      _statusMsg = null;
      _isError = false;
    });

    var cleanDomain = _domainCtrl.text.trim();
    if (cleanDomain.endsWith('/')) {
      cleanDomain = cleanDomain.substring(0, cleanDomain.length - 1);
    }
    final cleanWa = _waCtrl.text.trim();
    final digits = cleanWa.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^\d]'), '');
    final waNum = digits.startsWith('01') ? '2$digits' : digits;

    final updated = PlatformSettings(
      developerWhatsapp: cleanWa.isEmpty ? '01550523365' : cleanWa,
      developerContactUrl: 'https://wa.me/$waNum',
      developerTitle: _titleCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty ? 'تواصل مع المطور' : _titleCtrl.text.trim(),
      supportMessage: widget.initialSettings.supportMessage,
      publicDomainUrl: cleanDomain.isEmpty ? 'https://smart-card-inky.vercel.app' : cleanDomain,
    );

    final ok = await FirestoreService.updatePlatformSettings(updated);
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _savingSettings = false;
      _isError = !ok;
      _statusMsg = ok ? 'تم حفظ إعدادات الدومين والتواصل في قاعدة البيانات بنجاح.' : 'تعذر حفظ الإعدادات.';
    });
    if (ok) {
      widget.onSettingsUpdated(updated);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _changePassword() async {
    setState(() {
      _savingPwd = true;
      _statusMsg = null;
      _isError = false;
    });
    final res = await AuthService.changeAdminPassword(_curPwdCtrl.text, _newPwdCtrl.text);
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _savingPwd = false;
      _isError = !res.success;
      _statusMsg = res.message;
      if (res.success) {
        _curPwdCtrl.clear();
        _newPwdCtrl.clear();
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF09090B),
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF121216),
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        title: const Text('إعدادات المنصة والأمان', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
      ),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Card3DEnter(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              if (_statusMsg != null) ...[
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: _isError ? const Color(0x26EF4444) : const Color(0x2610B981),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                    border: Border.all(
                      color: _isError ? const Color(0x66EF4444) : const Color(0x6610B981),
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    _statusMsg!,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: _isError ? const Color(0xFFFCA5A5) : const Color(0xFF6EE7B7),
                      fontSize: 12.5,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
              ],

              // Domain & Contact Settings
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: const Color(0xFF121216),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    const Text(
                      'إعدادات الدومين ورابط البطاقات',
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: Color(0xFFFBBF24)),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextField(
                      controller: _domainCtrl,
                      textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'الدومين الأساسي للبطاقات',
                        labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12),
                        filled: true,
                        fillColor: Colors.black54,
                        border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 10),
                    TextField(
                      controller: _waCtrl,
                      textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'رقم واتساب المطور',
                        labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12),
                        filled: true,
                        fillColor: Colors.black54,
                        border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 14),
                    SizedBox(
                      width: double.infinity,
                      height: 46,
                      child: ElevatedButton(
                        onPressed: _savingSettings ? null : _saveSettings,
                        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                          backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
                          foregroundColor: Colors.black,
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          _savingSettings ? 'جاري الحفظ...' : 'حفظ إعدادات المنصة',
                          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // Password Change
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: const Color(0xFF121216),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  border: Border.all(color: Colors.white12),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    const Text(
                      'تغيير كلمة مرور الأدمن',
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: Color(0xFFFBBF24)),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextField(
                      controller: _curPwdCtrl,
                      obscureText: true,
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'كلمة المرور الحالية',
                        labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12),
                        filled: true,
                        fillColor: Colors.black54,
                        border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 10),
                    TextField(
                      controller: _newPwdCtrl,
                      obscureText: true,
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'كلمة المرور الجديدة',
                        labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12),
                        filled: true,
                        fillColor: Colors.black54,
                        border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 14),
                    SizedBox(
                      width: double.infinity,
                      height: 46,
                      child: ElevatedButton(
                        onPressed: _savingPwd ? null : _changePassword,
                        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                          backgroundColor: Colors.white10,
                          foregroundColor: const Color(0xFFFCD34D),
                          side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0x4DF59E0B)),
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          _savingPwd ? 'جاري التحديث...' : 'تحديث كلمة المرور في السحابة',
                          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
